# Code review: HUD and menus rework (two skins), Glyphbound (UE 5.9, C++)

You are reviewing, read-only, a rework of the game's whole on-screen UI. Everything is drawn with the HUD canvas
(`AHUD::DrawHUD`, no UMG widgets). Two skins switch with the held item: a 1990s first-person RPG look (staff and
sword) and a modern cyberpunk look (gun).

## Files to review (all under `Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/`)
- `UI/GBHUD.h` (class layout, painting API), `UI/GBHUDShared.h` (palettes, helpers)
- `UI/GBHUD.cpp` (frame, hands, crosshair, prompt, toasts, QTE, flash, fade)
- `UI/GBHUD_Paint.cpp` (primitives, pixel font drawing, Slate-font text on the canvas, text roles)
- `UI/GBHUD_Rpg.cpp`, `UI/GBHUD_Cyber.cpp` (the two status HUDs)
- `UI/GBHUD_Menus.cpp` (menu input/state code at the top is older; the drawing part from "drawing: shared widgets" on is new)
- `UI/GBHUD_DamageNumbers.cpp` (only `DrawDamageNumbers` changed)
- `UI/GBPixelFont.h/.cpp` (new: the game's own 5x7 pixel font, baked into atlases)
- `UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h/.cpp` (small change: public `CreatePixelTexture`, `HasSprite`)
- `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp`: only the block that starts at `// ---- UI block (gb.Smoke ui)` (smoke steps that take screenshots)

Use `git diff HEAD -- <file>` for changed files; `GBHUD_Paint.cpp`, `GBHUD_Rpg.cpp`, `GBHUD_Cyber.cpp`,
`GBPixelFont.*` are new (untracked): read them whole. Ignore other modified files in the tree (another person's
work: GBProjectile, GBPixelVfx, Spells.json, GDD.md).

## What to look for (ranked)
1. Crashes or undefined behaviour: null derefs (Canvas, PixelFont, RuntimeFont, Player components), out-of-range
   indexing, division by zero, dangling pointers (e.g. a pointer into a TArray kept across a modification:
   `const FGBActiveStatusEffect* Enchant` in `DrawCyberWeapon`), lambdas capturing by reference past their scope.
2. Behaviour regressions against the old HUD: every clickable must still be registered with `AddMenuHit` with the
   same kinds/indices as before (mouse support), menu keyboard navigation and confirm unchanged, the death screen
   still respawns, toasts still show.
3. Garbage collection: UObjects (textures, the pixel font, the runtime UFont) must be held by UPROPERTY members.
   `UGBPixelFont` keeps textures in a UPROPERTY TMap. Static caches of `FSlateFontInfo` in `GBHUD_Paint.cpp`.
4. Packaged build risks: the cyberpunk text loads TTF files from `Engine/Content/Slate/Fonts` by path and needs
   `/Engine/EngineFonts/Roboto` as a runtime UFont (a ConstructorHelpers reference in the HUD constructor). Is that
   enough for a packaged Win64 build? Anything that only works in the editor?
5. Per-frame cost: anything that scales badly (allocations per glyph, FText::FromString per draw, measuring text
   many times, scanline loops) — only flag what would matter at 60 fps on a 1440p screen.
6. Layout logic errors that a person would see: text that can overflow its box at 1280x720 or 2560x1440, values
   computed with the wrong scale (`UIScale()` for the cyberpunk layout in 1080p units, `ArtPixel()` whole art pixels
   for the RPG layout).

Do not suggest style rewrites, renames or new features. For each finding give: file:line, what goes wrong, a
concrete scenario, and the smallest fix. Say "no findings" for a category if there are none. Be concise.

codex
I’ll compare the changed code with HEAD, read the new files in full, and check the six categories you listed. I’ll leave the unrelated changes alone.

+void AGBHUD::UpdateTrails(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+{
+	const double Now = GetRealTime();
+	const float Dt = LastTrailUpdateTime < 0.0 ? 0.f : FMath::Clamp((float)(Now - LastTrailUpdateTime), 0.f, 0.1f);
+	LastTrailUpdateTime = Now;
+	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Player ? Player->GetAttributes() : nullptr;
+	if (!Attributes || Attributes->GetMaxHealth() <= 0.f)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	HealthShown01 = FMath::Clamp(Attributes->GetHealth() / Attributes->GetMaxHealth(), 0.f, 1.f);
+	if (HealthShown01 >= HealthTrail01)
+	{
+		HealthTrail01 = HealthShown01;
+	}
+	else if (Now - LastDamageTime > GBHudLayout::TrailHoldTime)
+	{
+		HealthTrail01 = FMath::Max(HealthShown01, HealthTrail01 - GBHudLayout::TrailDrainPerSecond * Dt);
+	}
+}
+

  * Draws, every frame, from the player's components (it owns no gameplay state):
- *  - health / mana bars, ammo or spell slots with cooldowns, quick-use item, skill points, active effects;
+ *  - the status HUD in two skins (GDD 13): a 1990s first-person RPG status bar of carved stone, iron and gold in
+ *    pixel art with the game's own pixel font (staff and sword), and a modern cyberpunk HUD of thin red / cyan
+ *    lines, cut corners and condensed type (gun);
  *  - the first-person pixel-art hands and staff (sprites from UGBPixelSpriteLibrary), art rule: hands are
  *    always pixel art, even when holding a 3D gun;
- *  - crosshair, interact prompt, toast messages, damage flash;
+ *  - crosshair, interact prompt, toast messages, damage flash, in the held item's skin;
  *  - the QTE overlay (key sequence with right / wrong marks, gesture to draw with its trail, time bar);
  *  - the era-travel fade (UGBEraSubsystem::GetFadeAlpha);
  *  - floating damage numbers over whatever the player hits (UGBCombatLibrary::OnDamageDealt);
  *  - the summoned shield: indicator near the vitals, and the shield sprite raised in first person while blocking;
- *  - menus: pause, inventory (use, equip armor, combine, owned weapons, loadout, enchant element), skill trees,
- *    death screen; keyboard, gamepad and mouse (hover moves the shared cursor, click = confirm).
- * The skin follows the held item: staff and sword = Rpg90s, gun = Cyberpunk.
+ *  - menus in the held item's skin: pause, inventory (use, equip armor, combine, owned weapons, loadout, enchant
+ *    element), skill trees (spell tree always RPG, weapon tree always cyberpunk), death screen; keyboard, gamepad
+ *    and mouse (hover moves the shared cursor, click = confirm).
  */
 UCLASS()
 class GLYPHBOUND_API AGBHUD : public AHUD

+/** sRGB hex -> linear color. */
+inline FLinearColor GBHex(const TCHAR* Hex, float Alpha = 1.f)
+{
+	FLinearColor Color = FLinearColor::FromSRGBColor(FColor::FromHex(Hex));
+	Color.A = Alpha;
+	return Color;
+}
+
+inline FLinearColor GBAlpha(const FLinearColor& Color, float Alpha)
+{
+	return FLinearColor(Color.R, Color.G, Color.B, Color.A * Alpha);
+}

 succeeded in 525ms:
   1 // The game's own pixel font for the 90s RPG skin (GDD 13): 5x7 capitals, lowercase with descenders, drawn as text
   2 // rows in GBPixelFont.cpp during the jam and baked here into small nearest-filtered atlases (no font asset import).
   3 #pragma once
   4 
   5 #include "CoreMinimal.h"
   6 #include "UObject/Object.h"
   7 #include "GBPixelFont.generated.h"
   8 
   9 class UTexture2D;
  10 
  11 /** A vertical color ramp baked into the glyph pixels: Top for the first rows, Mid in the middle, Bottom for the last. */
  12 struct FGBPixelInk
  13 {
  14 	FColor Top = FColor::White;
  15 	FColor Mid = FColor::White;
  16 	FColor Bottom = FColor::White;
  17 
  18 	FGBPixelInk() = default;
  19 	FGBPixelInk(FColor InTop, FColor InMid, FColor InBottom) : Top(InTop), Mid(InMid), Bottom(InBottom) {}
  20 	/** One flat color (tinted at draw time). */
  21 	static FGBPixelInk Flat() { return FGBPixelInk(); }
  22 	uint32 Key() const { return HashCombine(HashCombine(GetTypeHash(Top), GetTypeHash(Mid)), GetTypeHash(Bottom)); }
  23 };
  24 
  25 /**
  26  * Glyph metrics, in font pixels: capitals are 7 rows tall from the top of the cell, two more rows hold descenders,
  27  * one empty column separates glyphs. Every glyph has a 1-pixel outline around it in the atlas.
  28  */
  29 UCLASS(Transient)
  30 class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBPixelFont : public UObject
  31 {
  32 	GENERATED_BODY()
  33 
  34 public:
  35 	static constexpr int32 CapHeight = 7;
  36 	static constexpr int32 CellRows = 9;
  37 	static constexpr int32 Spacing = 1;
  38 
  39 	struct FGlyph
  40 	{
  41 		/** Top-left of the glyph's padded cell in the atlas (the padding is the 1-pixel outline ring). */
  42 		int32 AtlasX = 0;
  43 		int32 AtlasY = 0;
  44 		int32 Width = 0;
  45 	};
  46 
  47 	/** Builds the glyph table and the outline atlas. Safe to call more than once. */
  48 	void Init();
  49 
  50 	const FGlyph* FindGlyph(TCHAR Char) const;
  51 	/** Width in font pixels of a single line (no trailing spacing). */
  52 	int32 MeasureWidth(const FString& Text) const;
  53 
  54 	/** Atlas with the glyph pixels colored by Ink (built on first use, cached). */
  55 	UTexture2D* GetInkAtlas(const FGBPixelInk& Ink);
  56 	/** Atlas with only the outline ring of every glyph, white (tinted at draw time). */
  57 	UTexture2D* GetOutlineAtlas() const { return OutlineAtlas; }
  58 	FIntPoint GetAtlasSize() const { return AtlasSize; }
  59 
  60 	/** Pure: glyph rows of a character ('#' = ink), or null. Used by the tests. */
  61 	static const TCHAR* GetGlyphRows(TCHAR Char);
  62 
  63 private:
  64 	TMap<TCHAR, FGlyph> Glyphs;
  65 	/** Glyph masks in atlas space (1 = ink), row-major. */
  66 	TArray<uint8> Mask;
  67 	FIntPoint AtlasSize = FIntPoint::ZeroValue;
  68 
  69 	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<UTexture2D> OutlineAtlas;
  70 	UPROPERTY() TMap<uint32, TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>> InkAtlases;
  71 };

 111 
 112 	static constexpr int32 AtlasColumns = 16;
 113 	// Padded cell: 5 columns of glyph at most + the outline ring on each side; 9 rows + the ring.
 114 	static constexpr int32 CellW = 5 + 2;
 115 	static constexpr int32 CellH = UGBPixelFont::CellRows + 2;
 116 
 117 	static int32 RowWidth(const TCHAR* Rows)
 118 	{
 119 		int32 Width = 0;
 120 		while (Rows[Width] != 0 && Rows[Width] != TEXT('|'))
 121 		{
 122 			++Width;
 123 		}
 124 		return Width;
 125 	}
 126 
 127 	static FColor LerpColor(const FColor& A, const FColor& B, float T)
 128 	{
 129 		return FColor(
 130 			(uint8)FMath::RoundToInt(FMath::Lerp((float)A.R, (float)B.R, T)),
 131 			(uint8)FMath::RoundToInt(FMath::Lerp((float)A.G, (float)B.G, T)),
 132 			(uint8)FMath::RoundToInt(FMath::Lerp((float)A.B, (float)B.B, T)),
 133 			255);
 134 	}
 135 }
 136 
 137 const TCHAR* UGBPixelFont::GetGlyphRows(TCHAR Char)
 138 {
 139 	for (const GBPixelFontData::FEntry& Entry : GBPixelFontData::Entries)
 140 	{
 141 		if (Entry.Char == Char)
 142 		{
 143 			return Entry.Rows;
 144 		}
 145 	}
 146 	return nullptr;
 147 }
 148 
 149 void UGBPixelFont::Init()
 150 {
 151 	using namespace GBPixelFontData;
 152 	if (OutlineAtlas)
 153 	{
 154 		return;
 155 	}
 156 	const int32 Count = UE_ARRAY_COUNT(Entries);
 157 	const int32 RowsOfCells = (Count + AtlasColumns - 1) / AtlasColumns;
 158 	AtlasSize = FIntPoint(AtlasColumns * CellW, RowsOfCells * CellH);
 159 	Mask.SetNumZeroed(AtlasSize.X * AtlasSize.Y);
 160 
 161 	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count; ++Index)
 162 	{
 163 		const FEntry& Entry = Entries[Index];
 164 		FGlyph Glyph;
 165 		Glyph.AtlasX = (Index % AtlasColumns) * CellW;
 166 		Glyph.AtlasY = (Index / AtlasColumns) * CellH;
 167 		Glyph.Width = RowWidth(Entry.Rows);
 168 		int32 Row = 0;
 169 		int32 Col = 0;
 170 		for (const TCHAR* C = Entry.Rows; *C; ++C)
 171 		{
 172 			if (*C == TEXT('|'))
 173 			{
 174 				++Row;
 175 				Col = 0;
 176 				continue;
 177 			}
 178 			if (*C == TEXT('#') && Row < CellRows && Col < CellW - 2)
 179 			{
 180 				Mask[(Glyph.AtlasY + 1 + Row) * AtlasSize.X + Glyph.AtlasX + 1 + Col] = 1;
 181 			}
 182 			++Col;
 183 		}
 184 		Glyphs.Add(Entry.Char, Glyph);
 185 	}
 186 
 187 	// Outline ring: every empty pixel next to ink (8 neighbours), inside the same cell.
 188 	TArray<FColor> Pixels;
 189 	Pixels.Init(FColor(0, 0, 0, 0), AtlasSize.X * AtlasSize.Y);
 190 	for (int32 Y = 0; Y < AtlasSize.Y; ++Y)
 191 	{
 192 		for (int32 X = 0; X < AtlasSize.X; ++X)
 193 		{
 194 			if (Mask[Y * AtlasSize.X + X])
 195 			{
 196 				continue;
 197 			}
 198 			const int32 CellX0 = (X / CellW) * CellW;
 199 			const int32 CellY0 = (Y / CellH) * CellH;
 200 			bool bNear = false;
 201 			for (int32 DY = -1; DY <= 1 && !bNear; ++DY)
 202 			{
 203 				for (int32 DX = -1; DX <= 1 && !bNear; ++DX)
 204 				{
 205 					const int32 NX = X + DX;
 206 					const int32 NY = Y + DY;
 207 					if (NX < CellX0 || NY < CellY0 || NX >= CellX0 + CellW || NY >= CellY0 + CellH)
 208 					{
 209 						continue;
 210 					}
 211 					bNear = Mask[NY * AtlasSize.X + NX] != 0;
 212 				}
 213 			}
 214 			if (bNear)
 215 			{
 216 				Pixels[Y * AtlasSize.X + X] = FColor::White;
 217 			}
 218 		}
 219 	}
 220 	OutlineAtlas = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreatePixelTexture(Pixels, AtlasSize.X, AtlasSize.Y, TEXT("PixelFontOutline"), false);
 221 }
 222 
 223 const UGBPixelFont::FGlyph* UGBPixelFont::FindGlyph(TCHAR Char) const
 224 {
 225 	if (const FGlyph* Glyph = Glyphs.Find(Char))
 226 	{
 227 		return Glyph;
 228 	}
 229 	// Letters missing in one case fall back to the other; anything else shows as '?'.
 230 	if (const FGlyph* Upper = Glyphs.Find(FChar::ToUpper(Char)))
 231 	{
 232 		return Upper;
 233 	}
 234 	return Glyphs.Find(TEXT('?'));
 235 }
 236 
 237 int32 UGBPixelFont::MeasureWidth(const FString& Text) const
 238 {
 239 	int32 Width = 0;
 240 	for (const TCHAR Char : Text)
 241 	{
 242 		if (const FGlyph* Glyph = FindGlyph(Char))
 243 		{
 244 			Width += Glyph->Width + Spacing;
 245 		}
 246 	}
 247 	return FMath::Max(0, Width - Spacing);
 248 }
 249 
 250 UTexture2D* UGBPixelFont::GetInkAtlas(const FGBPixelInk& Ink)
 251 {
 252 	using namespace GBPixelFontData;
 253 	Init();
 254 	const uint32 Key = Ink.Key();
 255 	if (TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>* Found = InkAtlases.Find(Key))
 256 	{
 257 		return *Found;
 258 	}
 259 	TArray<FColor> Pixels;
 260 	Pixels.Init(FColor(0, 0, 0, 0), AtlasSize.X * AtlasSize.Y);
 261 	for (int32 Y = 0; Y < AtlasSize.Y; ++Y)
 262 	{
 263 		// Ramp over the capital height: Top on row 0, Mid around row 3, Bottom from row 6 (descenders keep Bottom).
 264 		const int32 Row = FMath::Clamp((Y % CellH) - 1, 0, CellRows - 1);
 265 		const float T = FMath::Clamp(Row / (float)(CapHeight - 1), 0.f, 1.f);
 266 		const FColor Color = T < 0.5f ? LerpColor(Ink.Top, Ink.Mid, T * 2.f) : LerpColor(Ink.Mid, Ink.Bottom, (T - 0.5f) * 2.f);
 267 		for (int32 X = 0; X < AtlasSize.X; ++X)
 268 		{
 269 			if (Mask[Y * AtlasSize.X + X])
 270 			{
 271 				Pixels[Y * AtlasSize.X + X] = Color;
 272 			}
 273 		}
 274 	}
 275 	UTexture2D* Texture = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreatePixelTexture(Pixels, AtlasSize.X, AtlasSize.Y, TEXT("PixelFontInk"), false);
 276 	InkAtlases.Add(Key, Texture);
 277 	return Texture;
 278 }

 succeeded in 576ms:
   1 // Painting layer of AGBHUD shared by both skins: primitives (rects, polygons, glows), the pixel font of the RPG
   2 // skin, the condensed vector type of the cyberpunk skin, and the skin-neutral text roles the menus use.
   3 #include "UI/GBHUD.h"
   4 
   5 #include "UI/GBHUDShared.h"
   6 #include "UI/GBPixelFont.h"
   7 #include "UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h"
   8 
   9 #include "CanvasItem.h"
  10 #include "CanvasTypes.h"
  11 #include "Engine/Canvas.h"
  12 #include "Engine/Engine.h"
  13 #include "Engine/Font.h"
  14 #include "Engine/Texture2D.h"
  15 #include "Engine/World.h"
  16 #include "EngineFontServices.h"
  17 #include "Fonts/FontMeasure.h"
  18 #include "Fonts/SlateFontInfo.h"
  19 #include "Misc/Paths.h"

  21 namespace GBPaintLocal
  22 {
  23 	/** Deterministic 0..1 noise from integer coordinates (art generation). */
  24 	float Hash01(int32 X, int32 Y, int32 Seed)
  25 	{
  26 		uint32 H = (uint32)X * 374761393u + (uint32)Y * 668265263u + (uint32)Seed * 2246822519u;
  27 		H = (H ^ (H >> 13)) * 1274126177u;
  28 		H ^= H >> 16;
  29 		return (H & 0xFFFF) / 65535.f;
  30 	}
  31 
  32 	FColor Shade(const FColor& Base, float Amount)
  33 	{
  34 		auto Channel = [Amount](uint8 C) { return (uint8)FMath::Clamp(FMath::RoundToInt(C * (1.f + Amount)), 0, 255); };
  35 		return FColor(Channel(Base.R), Channel(Base.G), Channel(Base.B), Base.A);
  36 	}
  37 
  38 	/**
  39 	 * Dark obsidian blocks, 16 x 8 art pixels, two courses offset by half a block (tile 32 x 16), 1-pixel mortar,
  40 	 * light from the top left, a little wear noise (VisualStyleGuide 5: noise only on rough materials).
  41 	 */
  42 	void BuildStone(TArray<FColor>& Pixels, int32& OutW, int32& OutH)
  43 	{
  44 		const int32 W = 32;
  45 		const int32 H = 16;
  46 		OutW = W;
  47 		OutH = H;
  48 		Pixels.SetNum(W * H);
  49 		const FColor Mortar = FColor::FromHex(TEXT("101017"));
  50 		const FColor Bases[3] = { FColor::FromHex(TEXT("252734")), FColor::FromHex(TEXT("2A2B37")), FColor::FromHex(TEXT("22232E")) };
  51 		const FColor Light = FColor::FromHex(TEXT("3B3C4C"));
  52 		const FColor Dark = FColor::FromHex(TEXT("1A1A22"));
  53 		const FColor Violet = FColor::FromHex(TEXT("3A3346"));
  54 		for (int32 Y = 0; Y < H; ++Y)
  55 		{
  56 			const int32 Course = Y / 8;
  57 			const int32 LY = Y % 8;
  58 			for (int32 X = 0; X < W; ++X)
  59 			{
  60 				const int32 Shift = Course == 0 ? 0 : 8;
  61 				const int32 BX = (X + Shift) % W;
  62 				const int32 Brick = BX / 16 + Course * 2;
  63 				const int32 LX = BX % 16;
  64 				FColor C;
  65 				if (LY == 7 || LX == 15)
  66 				{
  67 					C = Mortar;
  68 				}
  69 				else if (LY == 0 || LX == 0)
  70 				{
  71 					C = Light;
  72 				}
  73 				else if (LY == 6 || LX == 14)
  74 				{
  75 					C = Dark;
  76 				}
  77 				else
  78 				{
  79 					C = Bases[Brick % 3];
  80 					const float N = Hash01(X, Y, 7);
  81 					if (N > 0.93f)
  82 					{
  83 						C = Shade(C, 0.22f);
  84 					}
  85 					else if (N < 0.07f)
  86 					{
  87 						C = Shade(C, -0.25f);
  88 					}
  89 					else if (N > 0.86f && Hash01(Brick, 3, 11) > 0.5f)
  90 					{
  91 						C = Violet;
  92 					}
  93 				}
  94 				Pixels[Y * W + X] = C;
  95 			}
  96 		}
  97 	}
  98 
  99 	UTexture2D* MakeSmoothTexture(const TArray<FColor>& Pixels, int32 W, int32 H, const TCHAR* Name)
 100 	{
 101 		UTexture2D* Texture = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreatePixelTexture(Pixels, W, H, Name, false);
 102 		if (Texture)
 103 		{
 104 			Texture->Filter = TF_Bilinear;
 105 			Texture->SRGB = false;
 106 			Texture->UpdateResource();
 107 		}
 108 		return Texture;
 109 	}
 110 
 111 	const FSlateFontInfo& CyFont(uint8 Face)
 112 	{
 113 		static TMap<uint8, FSlateFontInfo> Cache;
 114 		if (const FSlateFontInfo* Found = Cache.Find(Face))
 115 		{
 116 			return *Found;
 117 		}
 118 		const TCHAR* File = TEXT("Roboto-Regular.ttf");
 119 		switch (Face)
 120 		{
 121 		case 0: File = TEXT("Roboto-BoldCondensed.ttf"); break;
 122 		case 1: File = TEXT("Roboto-Regular.ttf"); break;
 123 		case 2: File = TEXT("Roboto-Medium.ttf"); break;
 124 		case 3: File = TEXT("Roboto-Light.ttf"); break;
 125 		case 4: File = TEXT("Roboto-Black.ttf"); break;
 126 		case 5: File = TEXT("DroidSansMono.ttf"); break;
 127 		default: break;
 128 		}
 129 		const FString Path = FPaths::EngineContentDir() / TEXT("Slate/Fonts") / File;
 130 		return Cache.Add(Face, FSlateFontInfo(Path, 16.f));
 131 	}
 132 }
 133 
 134 // ======================================================================== basics
 135 
 136 double AGBHUD::GetRealTime() const
 137 {
 138 	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
 139 	return World ? World->GetRealTimeSeconds() : 0.0;
 140 }
 141 
 142 float AGBHUD::UIScale() const
 143 {
 144 	return Canvas ? FMath::Max(0.1f, Canvas->ClipY / 1080.f) : 1.f;
 145 }
 146 
 147 float AGBHUD::ArtPixel() const
 148 {
 149 	return Canvas ? FMath::Max(2.f, (float)FMath::RoundToInt(Canvas->ClipY / 270.f)) : 4.f;
 150 }
 151 
 152 void AGBHUD::EnsureArt()
 153 {
 154 	using namespace GBPaintLocal;
 155 	if (!PixelFont)
 156 	{
 157 		PixelFont = NewObject<UGBPixelFont>(this);
 158 		PixelFont->Init();
 159 	}
 160 	if (!RpgStoneTexture)
 161 	{
 162 		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
 163 		int32 W = 0;
 164 		int32 H = 0;
 165 		BuildStone(Pixels, W, H);
 166 		RpgStoneTexture = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreatePixelTexture(Pixels, W, H, TEXT("RpgStone"), true);
 167 	}
 168 	if (!GlowTexture)
 169 	{
 170 		const int32 N = 64;
 171 		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
 172 		Pixels.SetNum(N * N);
 173 		for (int32 Y = 0; Y < N; ++Y)
 174 		{
 175 			for (int32 X = 0; X < N; ++X)
 176 			{
 177 				const float DX = (X + 0.5f) / N * 2.f - 1.f;
 178 				const float DY = (Y + 0.5f) / N * 2.f - 1.f;
 179 				const float R = FMath::Clamp(FMath::Sqrt(DX * DX + DY * DY), 0.f, 1.f);
 180 				const float A = FMath::Square(1.f - R) * (1.f - R * 0.3f);
 181 				Pixels[Y * N + X] = FColor(255, 255, 255, (uint8)FMath::RoundToInt(A * 255.f));
 182 			}
 183 		}
 184 		GlowTexture = MakeSmoothTexture(Pixels, N, N, TEXT("HudGlow"));
 185 	}
 186 	if (!RampTexture)
 187 	{
 188 		const int32 N = 64;
 189 		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
 190 		Pixels.SetNum(N);
 191 		for (int32 X = 0; X < N; ++X)
 192 		{
 193 			Pixels[X] = FColor(255, 255, 255, (uint8)FMath::RoundToInt((1.f - X / (float)(N - 1)) * 255.f));
 194 		}
 195 		RampTexture = MakeSmoothTexture(Pixels, N, 1, TEXT("HudRamp"));
 196 	}
 197 }
 198 
 199 // ======================================================================== primitives
 200 
 201 void AGBHUD::FillRect(float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FLinearColor& Color)
 202 {
 203 	if (W <= 0.f || H <= 0.f || Color.A <= 0.f)
 204 	{
 205 		return;
 206 	}
 207 	DrawRect(Color, FMath::RoundToFloat(X), FMath::RoundToFloat(Y), FMath::RoundToFloat(X + W) - FMath::RoundToFloat(X), FMath::RoundToFloat(Y + H) - FMath::RoundToFloat(Y));
 208 }
 209 
 210 void AGBHUD::DrawOutline(float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FLinearColor& Color, float Thickness)
 211 {
 212 	const float T = FMath::Max(1.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(Thickness));
 213 	FillRect(X, Y, W, T, Color);
 214 	FillRect(X, Y + H - T, W, T, Color);
 215 	FillRect(X, Y + T, T, H - 2.f * T, Color);
 216 	FillRect(X + W - T, Y + T, T, H - 2.f * T, Color);
 217 }
 218 
 219 void AGBHUD::FillPoly(const TArray<FVector2D>& Points, const FLinearColor& Color)
 220 {
 221 	if (!Canvas || Points.Num() < 3 || Color.A <= 0.f)
 222 	{
 223 		return;
 224 	}
 225 	TArray<FCanvasUVTri> Tris;
 226 	Tris.Reserve(Points.Num() - 2);
 227 	for (int32 i = 1; i + 1 < Points.Num(); ++i)
 228 	{
 229 		FCanvasUVTri Tri;
 230 		Tri.V0_Pos = Points[0];
 231 		Tri.V1_Pos = Points[i];
 232 		Tri.V2_Pos = Points[i + 1];
 233 		Tri.V0_UV = Tri.V1_UV = Tri.V2_UV = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 234 		Tri.V0_Color = Tri.V1_Color = Tri.V2_Color = Color;
 235 		Tris.Add(Tri);
 236 	}
 237 	FCanvasTriangleItem Item(Tris, GWhiteTexture);
 238 	Item.BlendMode = SE_BLEND_Translucent;
 239 	Canvas->DrawItem(Item);
 240 }
 241 
 242 void AGBHUD::StrokePoly(const TArray<FVector2D>& Points, const FLinearColor& Color, float Thickness)
 243 {
 244 	if (Points.Num() < 2 || Color.A <= 0.f)
 245 	{
 246 		return;
 247 	}
 248 	for (int32 i = 0; i < Points.Num(); ++i)
 249 	{
 250 		const FVector2D& A = Points[i];
 251 		const FVector2D& B = Points[(i + 1) % Points.Num()];
 252 		DrawLine(A.X, A.Y, B.X, B.Y, Color, Thickness);
 253 	}
 254 }
 255 
 256 void AGBHUD::DrawGlow(float CX, float CY, float RX, float RY, const FLinearColor& Color)
 257 {
 258 	EnsureArt();
 259 	if (!GlowTexture || Color.A <= 0.f)
 260 	{
 261 		return;
 262 	}
 263 	DrawTexture(GlowTexture, CX - RX, CY - RY, 2.f * RX, 2.f * RY, 0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f, Color, BLEND_Translucent);
 264 }
 265 
 266 void AGBHUD::DrawFade(float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bFadeRight)
 267 {
 268 	EnsureArt();
 269 	if (!RampTexture || W <= 0.f || H <= 0.f)
 270 	{
 271 		return;
 272 	}
 273 	if (bFadeRight)
 274 	{
 275 		DrawTexture(RampTexture, X, Y, W, H, 0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f, Color, BLEND_Translucent);
 276 	}
 277 	else
 278 	{
 279 		DrawTexture(RampTexture, X, Y, W, H, 1.f, 0.f, -1.f, 1.f, Color, BLEND_Translucent);
 280 	}
 281 }
 282 
 283 void AGBHUD::DrawSprite(UTexture2D* Texture, float X, float Y, float PixelScale, const FLinearColor& Tint)
 284 {
 285 	if (!Texture)
 286 	{
 287 		return;
 288 	}
 289 	const float W = Texture->GetSizeX() * PixelScale;
 290 	const float H = Texture->GetSizeY() * PixelScale;
 291 	DrawTexture(Texture, FMath::RoundToFloat(X), FMath::RoundToFloat(Y), W, H, 0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f, Tint, BLEND_Translucent);
 292 }
 293 
 294 bool AGBHUD::DrawSpriteFit(FName SpriteId, float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FLinearColor& Tint, bool bSilhouette)
 295 {
 296 	UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Sprites = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this);
 297 	if (!Sprites || SpriteId == NAME_None || !Sprites->HasSprite(SpriteId))
 298 	{
 299 		return false;
 300 	}
 301 	UTexture2D* Texture = Sprites->GetSprite(SpriteId);
 302 	if (!Texture)
 303 	{
 304 		return false;
 305 	}
 306 	const float SW = Texture->GetSizeX();
 307 	const float SH = Texture->GetSizeY();
 308 	float Scale = FMath::Min(W / SW, H / SH);
 309 	// Whole pixels when there is room; a smaller fractional scale only for sprites larger than the box.
 310 	if (Scale >= 1.f)
 311 	{
 312 		Scale = FMath::FloorToFloat(Scale);
 313 	}
 314 	const float DW = SW * Scale;
 315 	const float DH = SH * Scale;
 316 	const float DX = FMath::RoundToFloat(X + (W - DW) * 0.5f);
 317 	const float DY = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y + (H - DH) * 0.5f);
 318 	if (bSilhouette)
 319 	{
 320 		// A soft glow under the icon (holographic look of the cyberpunk skin).
 321 		DrawGlow(DX + DW * 0.5f, DY + DH * 0.5f, DW * 0.7f, DH * 0.7f, GBAlpha(Tint, 0.25f));
 322 	}
 323 	DrawTexture(Texture, DX, DY, DW, DH, 0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f, Tint, BLEND_Translucent);
 324 	return true;
 325 }
 326 
 327 // ======================================================================== engine font (debug overlays)
 328 
 329 void AGBHUD::DrawLabel(const FString& Text, const FLinearColor& Color, float X, float Y, float SizePx, float AlignX, bool bShadow, bool bLarge)
 330 {
 331 	if (!Canvas || !GEngine || Text.IsEmpty())
 332 	{
 333 		return;
 334 	}
 335 	UFont* Font = bLarge ? GEngine->GetLargeFont() : GEngine->GetMediumFont();
 336 	if (!Font)
 337 	{
 338 		return;
 339 	}
 340 	const float Scale = SizePx * UIScale() / FMath::Max(1.f, Font->GetMaxCharHeight());
 341 	float W = 0.f;
 342 	float H = 0.f;
 343 	GetTextSize(Text, W, H, Font, Scale);
 344 	const float DrawX = FMath::RoundToFloat(X - W * AlignX);
 345 	const float DrawY = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y);
 346 	if (bShadow)
 347 	{
 348 		const float Off = FMath::Max(1.f, 2.f * UIScale());
 349 		DrawText(Text, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.8f * Color.A), DrawX + Off, DrawY + Off, Font, Scale);
 350 	}
 351 	DrawText(Text, Color, DrawX, DrawY, Font, Scale);
 352 }
 353 
 354 FVector2D AGBHUD::MeasureLabel(const FString& Text, float SizePx, bool bLarge) const
 355 {
 356 	if (!Canvas || !GEngine || Text.IsEmpty())
 357 	{
 358 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 359 	}
 360 	UFont* Font = bLarge ? GEngine->GetLargeFont() : GEngine->GetMediumFont();
 361 	if (!Font)
 362 	{
 363 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 364 	}
 365 	const float Scale = SizePx * UIScale() / FMath::Max(1.f, Font->GetMaxCharHeight());
 366 	float W = 0.f;
 367 	float H = 0.f;
 368 	GetTextSize(Text, W, H, Font, Scale);
 369 	return FVector2D(W, H);
 370 }
 371 
 372 // ======================================================================== pixel font
 373 
 374 FVector2D AGBHUD::MeasurePixelText(const FString& Text, int32 Scale) const
 375 {
 376 	if (!PixelFont || Text.IsEmpty())
 377 	{
 378 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 379 	}
 380 	return FVector2D(PixelFont->MeasureWidth(Text) * Scale, UGBPixelFont::CapHeight * Scale);
 381 }
 382 
 383 FVector2D AGBHUD::DrawPixelText(const FString& Text, float X, float Y, const FPixelTextStyle& Style, float AlignX)
 384 {
 385 	EnsureArt();
 386 	if (!Canvas || !PixelFont || Text.IsEmpty() || Style.Color.A <= 0.f)
 387 	{
 388 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 389 	}
 390 	const int32 Scale = FMath::Max(1, Style.Scale);
 391 	const FVector2D Size = MeasurePixelText(Text, Scale);
 392 	const float X0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(X - Size.X * AlignX);
 393 	const float Y0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y);
 394 	const FIntPoint Atlas = PixelFont->GetAtlasSize();
 395 	if (Atlas.X <= 0 || Atlas.Y <= 0)
 396 	{
 397 		return Size;
 398 	}
 399 	UTexture2D* InkAtlas = PixelFont->GetInkAtlas(Style.Ink ? *Style.Ink : FGBPixelInk::Flat());
 400 	UTexture2D* OutlineAtlas = PixelFont->GetOutlineAtlas();
 401 	const FLinearColor InkTint = Style.Ink ? FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, Style.Color.A) : Style.Color;
 402 	const float CellH = (float)(UGBPixelFont::CellRows + 2);
 403 
 404 	auto Pass = [&](UTexture2D* Texture, const FLinearColor& Tint, float OffX, float OffY)
 405 	{
 406 		if (!Texture || Tint.A <= 0.f)
 407 		{
 408 			return;
 409 		}
 410 		float Pen = X0;
 411 		for (const TCHAR Char : Text)
 412 		{
 413 			const UGBPixelFont::FGlyph* Glyph = PixelFont->FindGlyph(Char);
 414 			if (!Glyph)
 415 			{
 416 				continue;
 417 			}
 418 			const float CellW = (float)(Glyph->Width + 2);
 419 			DrawTexture(Texture, Pen - Scale + OffX, Y0 - Scale + OffY, CellW * Scale, CellH * Scale,
 420 				Glyph->AtlasX / (float)Atlas.X, Glyph->AtlasY / (float)Atlas.Y, CellW / Atlas.X, CellH / Atlas.Y, Tint, BLEND_Translucent);
 421 			Pen += (Glyph->Width + UGBPixelFont::Spacing) * Scale;
 422 		}
 423 	};
 424 
 425 	const bool bOutline = Style.Outline.A > 0.f;
 426 	if (Style.Shadow.A > 0.f)
 427 	{
 428 		const FLinearColor Shadow = GBAlpha(Style.Shadow, Style.Color.A);
 429 		if (bOutline)
 430 		{
 431 			Pass(OutlineAtlas, Shadow, Scale, Scale);
 432 		}
 433 		Pass(InkAtlas, Shadow, Scale, Scale);
 434 	}
 435 	if (bOutline)
 436 	{
 437 		Pass(OutlineAtlas, GBAlpha(Style.Outline, Style.Color.A), 0.f, 0.f);
 438 	}
 439 	Pass(InkAtlas, InkTint, 0.f, 0.f);
 440 	return Size;
 441 }
 442 
 443 // ======================================================================== cyberpunk type
 444 
 445 FVector2D AGBHUD::MeasureCyText(const FString& Text, const FCyTextStyle& Style) const
 446 {
 447 	if (Text.IsEmpty())
 448 	{
 449 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 450 	}
 451 	FSlateFontInfo Font = GBPaintLocal::CyFont((uint8)Style.Face);
 452 	Font.Size = FMath::Max(1.f, Style.Px * UIScale() * 0.75f);
 453 	Font.LetterSpacing = Style.Spacing;
 454 	const TSharedPtr<FSlateFontMeasure> Measure = FEngineFontServices::IsInitialized() ? FEngineFontServices::Get().GetFontMeasure() : nullptr;
 455 	if (!Measure.IsValid())
 456 	{
 457 		return FVector2D(Text.Len() * Style.Px * UIScale() * 0.5f, Style.Px * UIScale());
 458 	}
 459 	const FString Shown = Style.bUpper ? Text.ToUpper() : Text;
 460 	return FVector2D(Measure->Measure(Shown, Font));
 461 }
 462 
 463 FVector2D AGBHUD::DrawCyText(const FString& Text, float X, float Y, const FCyTextStyle& Style, float AlignX)
 464 {
 465 	if (!Canvas || !RuntimeFont || Text.IsEmpty() || Style.Color.A <= 0.f)
 466 	{
 467 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 468 	}
 469 	FSlateFontInfo Font = GBPaintLocal::CyFont((uint8)Style.Face);
 470 	Font.Size = FMath::Max(1.f, Style.Px * UIScale() * 0.75f);
 471 	Font.LetterSpacing = Style.Spacing;
 472 	const FString Shown = Style.bUpper ? Text.ToUpper() : Text;
 473 	const FVector2D Size = MeasureCyText(Text, Style);
 474 	const FVector2D Pos(FMath::RoundToFloat(X - Size.X * AlignX), FMath::RoundToFloat(Y));
 475 	const FText AsText = FText::FromString(Shown);
 476 
 477 	auto Draw = [this, &Font, &AsText](const FVector2D& At, const FLinearColor& Color)
 478 	{
 479 		FCanvasTextItem Item(At, AsText, Font, Color);
 480 		Item.Font = RuntimeFont;
 481 		Item.BlendMode = SE_BLEND_Translucent;
 482 		Canvas->DrawItem(Item);
 483 	};
 484 	const FLinearColor Shadow = Style.Shadow.A > 0.f ? Style.Shadow : (bForceTextShadow ? FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.55f) : FLinearColor::Transparent);
 485 	if (Shadow.A > 0.f)
 486 	{
 487 		const float Off = FMath::Max(1.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(2.f * UIScale()));
 488 		Draw(Pos + FVector2D(Off, Off), GBAlpha(Shadow, Style.Color.A));
 489 	}
 490 	if (Style.Split > 0.f)
 491 	{
 492 		Draw(Pos + FVector2D(-Style.Split, 0.f), GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.7f * Style.Color.A));
 493 		Draw(Pos + FVector2D(Style.Split, 0.f), GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.7f * Style.Color.A));
 494 	}
 495 	Draw(Pos, Style.Color);
 496 	return Size;
 497 }
 498 
 499 // ======================================================================== text roles
 500 
 501 FLinearColor AGBHUD::ToneColor(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextTone Tone) const
 502 {
 503 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s)
 504 	{
 505 		switch (Tone)
 506 		{
 507 		case EGBTextTone::Normal: return GBRpg::Bone;
 508 		case EGBTextTone::Accent: return GBRpg::Gold3;
 509 		case EGBTextTone::Muted: return GBRpg::BoneDim;
 510 		case EGBTextTone::Value: return GBRpg::Gold4;
 511 		case EGBTextTone::Good: return GBRpg::Good;
 512 		case EGBTextTone::Bad: return GBHex(TEXT("#FF5A40"));
 513 		case EGBTextTone::Warn: return GBRpg::EmberLight;
 514 		case EGBTextTone::Selected: return GBRpg::Gold4;
 515 		case EGBTextTone::Disabled: return GBRpg::BoneDark;
 516 		}
 517 		return GBRpg::Bone;
 518 	}
 519 	switch (Tone)
 520 	{
 521 	case EGBTextTone::Normal: return GBCy::RedBright;
 522 	case EGBTextTone::Accent: return GBCy::Red;
 523 	case EGBTextTone::Muted: return GBCy::RedDim;
 524 	case EGBTextTone::Value: return GBCy::Cyan;
 525 	case EGBTextTone::Good: return GBCy::Green;
 526 	case EGBTextTone::Bad: return GBCy::Red;
 527 	case EGBTextTone::Warn: return GBCy::Yellow;
 528 	case EGBTextTone::Selected: return GBCy::Pale;
 529 	case EGBTextTone::Disabled: return GBCy::Grey;
 530 	}
 531 	return GBCy::RedBright;
 532 }
 533 
 534 namespace GBPaintLocal
 535 {
 536 	int32 PixScale(float ClipY, float AtTenEighty)
 537 	{
 538 		return FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(AtTenEighty * ClipY / 1080.f));
 539 	}
 540 
 541 	int32 RpgScale(float ClipY, EGBTextSize Size)
 542 	{
 543 		switch (Size)
 544 		{
 545 		case EGBTextSize::Title: return PixScale(ClipY, 6.f);
 546 		case EGBTextSize::Heading: return PixScale(ClipY, 4.f);
 547 		case EGBTextSize::Body: return PixScale(ClipY, 3.f);
 548 		case EGBTextSize::Small: return FMath::Max(2, PixScale(ClipY, 2.f));
 549 		case EGBTextSize::Number: return PixScale(ClipY, 5.f);
 550 		}
 551 		return 2;
 552 	}
 553 
 554 }
 555 
 556 namespace
 557 {
 558 	struct FCyRole
 559 	{
 560 		uint8 Face;
 561 		float Px;
 562 		int32 Spacing;
 563 		bool bUpper;
 564 	};
 565 	FCyRole CyRole(EGBTextSize Size)
 566 	{
 567 		switch (Size)
 568 		{
 569 		case EGBTextSize::Title: return { 0, 54.f, 60, true };
 570 		case EGBTextSize::Heading: return { 0, 30.f, 50, true };
 571 		case EGBTextSize::Body: return { 1, 21.f, 0, false };
 572 		case EGBTextSize::Small: return { 2, 16.f, 0, false };
 573 		case EGBTextSize::Number: return { 0, 64.f, 0, false };
 574 		}
 575 		return { 1, 20.f, 0, false };
 576 	}
 577 }
 578 
 579 FVector2D AGBHUD::UiMeasure(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextSize Size, const FString& Text) const
 580 {
 581 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s)
 582 	{
 583 		return MeasurePixelText(Text, GBPaintLocal::RpgScale(Canvas ? Canvas->ClipY : 1080.f, Size));
 584 	}
 585 	const FCyRole CyR = CyRole(Size);
 586 	FCyTextStyle Style;
 587 	Style.Face = (ECyFace)CyR.Face;
 588 	Style.Px = CyR.Px;
 589 	Style.Spacing = CyR.Spacing;
 590 	Style.bUpper = CyR.bUpper;
 591 	return MeasureCyText(Text, Style);
 592 }
 593 
 594 float AGBHUD::UiLineHeight(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextSize Size) const
 595 {
 596 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s)
 597 	{
 598 		const int32 Scale = GBPaintLocal::RpgScale(Canvas ? Canvas->ClipY : 1080.f, Size);
 599 		return (UGBPixelFont::CellRows + 2) * Scale;
 600 	}
 601 	return CyRole(Size).Px * UIScale() * 1.32f;
 602 }
 603 
 604 FVector2D AGBHUD::UiText(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextSize Size, EGBTextTone Tone, const FString& Text, float X, float Y, float AlignX, const FLinearColor* OverrideColor)
 605 {
 606 	if (Text.IsEmpty())
 607 	{
 608 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 609 	}
 610 	const FLinearColor Color = OverrideColor ? *OverrideColor : ToneColor(Skin, Tone);
 611 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s)
 612 	{
 613 		FPixelTextStyle Style;
 614 		Style.Scale = GBPaintLocal::RpgScale(Canvas ? Canvas->ClipY : 1080.f, Size);
 615 		Style.Color = Color;
 616 		const bool bBig = Size == EGBTextSize::Title || Size == EGBTextSize::Heading || Size == EGBTextSize::Number;
 617 		if (!OverrideColor)
 618 		{
 619 			switch (Tone)
 620 			{
 621 			case EGBTextTone::Normal: Style.Ink = bBig ? &GBRpg::InkGold : &GBRpg::InkBone; break;
 622 			case EGBTextTone::Accent:
 623 			case EGBTextTone::Selected: Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold; break;
 624 			case EGBTextTone::Warn: Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkEmber; break;
 625 			case EGBTextTone::Bad: Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBlood; break;
 626 			case EGBTextTone::Value: Style.Ink = bBig ? &GBRpg::InkBone : nullptr; break;
 627 			default: break;
 628 			}
 629 		}
 630 		Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.85f);
 631 		if (bBig)
 632 		{
 633 			Style.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
 634 		}
 635 		return DrawPixelText(Text, X, Y, Style, AlignX);
 636 	}
 637 	const FCyRole CyR = CyRole(Size);
 638 	FCyTextStyle Style;
 639 	Style.Face = (ECyFace)CyR.Face;
 640 	Style.Px = CyR.Px;
 641 	Style.Spacing = CyR.Spacing;
 642 	Style.bUpper = CyR.bUpper;
 643 	Style.Color = Color;
 644 	return DrawCyText(Text, X, Y, Style, AlignX);
 645 }
 646 
 647 float AGBHUD::UiWrapped(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextSize Size, EGBTextTone Tone, const FString& Text, float X, float Y, float MaxWidth, const FLinearColor* OverrideColor)
 648 {
 649 	TArray<FString> Words;
 650 	Text.ParseIntoArrayWS(Words);
 651 	const float LineH = UiLineHeight(Skin, Size);
 652 	float CurY = Y;
 653 	FString Line;
 654 	for (const FString& Word : Words)
 655 	{
 656 		const FString Candidate = Line.IsEmpty() ? Word : Line + TEXT(" ") + Word;
 657 		if (!Line.IsEmpty() && UiMeasure(Skin, Size, Candidate).X > MaxWidth)
 658 		{
 659 			UiText(Skin, Size, Tone, Line, X, CurY, 0.f, OverrideColor);
 660 			CurY += LineH;
 661 			Line = Word;
 662 		}
 663 		else
 664 		{
 665 			Line = Candidate;
 666 		}
 667 	}
 668 	if (!Line.IsEmpty())
 669 	{
 670 		UiText(Skin, Size, Tone, Line, X, CurY, 0.f, OverrideColor);
 671 		CurY += LineH;
 672 	}
 673 	return CurY - Y;
 674 }

   1 // Painting layer of AGBHUD shared by both skins: primitives (rects, polygons, glows), the pixel font of the RPG

  22 {
  23 	/** Deterministic 0..1 noise from integer coordinates (art generation). */
  24 	float Hash01(int32 X, int32 Y, int32 Seed)
  25 	{
  26 		uint32 H = (uint32)X * 374761393u + (uint32)Y * 668265263u + (uint32)Seed * 2246822519u;
  27 		H = (H ^ (H >> 13)) * 1274126177u;
  28 		H ^= H >> 16;
  29 		return (H & 0xFFFF) / 65535.f;
  30 	}
  31 
  32 	FColor Shade(const FColor& Base, float Amount)
  33 	{
  34 		auto Channel = [Amount](uint8 C) { return (uint8)FMath::Clamp(FMath::RoundToInt(C * (1.f + Amount)), 0, 255); };
  35 		return FColor(Channel(Base.R), Channel(Base.G), Channel(Base.B), Base.A);
  36 	}
  37 
  38 	/**
  39 	 * Dark obsidian blocks, 16 x 8 art pixels, two courses offset by half a block (tile 32 x 16), 1-pixel mortar,
  40 	 * light from the top left, a little wear noise (VisualStyleGuide 5: noise only on rough materials).
  41 	 */
  42 	void BuildStone(TArray<FColor>& Pixels, int32& OutW, int32& OutH)
  43 	{
  44 		const int32 W = 32;
  45 		const int32 H = 16;
  46 		OutW = W;
  47 		OutH = H;
  48 		Pixels.SetNum(W * H);
  49 		const FColor Mortar = FColor::FromHex(TEXT("101017"));
  50 		const FColor Bases[3] = { FColor::FromHex(TEXT("252734")), FColor::FromHex(TEXT("2A2B37")), FColor::FromHex(TEXT("22232E")) };
  51 		const FColor Light = FColor::FromHex(TEXT("3B3C4C"));
  52 		const FColor Dark = FColor::FromHex(TEXT("1A1A22"));
  53 		const FColor Violet = FColor::FromHex(TEXT("3A3346"));
  54 		for (int32 Y = 0; Y < H; ++Y)
  55 		{
  56 			const int32 Course = Y / 8;
  57 			const int32 LY = Y % 8;
  58 			for (int32 X = 0; X < W; ++X)
  59 			{
  60 				const int32 Shift = Course == 0 ? 0 : 8;
  61 				const int32 BX = (X + Shift) % W;
  62 				const int32 Brick = BX / 16 + Course * 2;
  63 				const int32 LX = BX % 16;
  64 				FColor C;
  65 				if (LY == 7 || LX == 15)
  66 				{
  67 					C = Mortar;
  68 				}
  69 				else if (LY == 0 || LX == 0)
  70 				{
  71 					C = Light;
  72 				}
  73 				else if (LY == 6 || LX == 14)
  74 				{
  75 					C = Dark;
  76 				}
  77 				else
  78 				{
  79 					C = Bases[Brick % 3];
  80 					const float N = Hash01(X, Y, 7);
  81 					if (N > 0.93f)
  82 					{
  83 						C = Shade(C, 0.22f);
  84 					}
  85 					else if (N < 0.07f)
  86 					{
  87 						C = Shade(C, -0.25f);
  88 					}
  89 					else if (N > 0.86f && Hash01(Brick, 3, 11) > 0.5f)
  90 					{
  91 						C = Violet;
  92 					}
  93 				}
  94 				Pixels[Y * W + X] = C;
  95 			}
  96 		}
  97 	}
  98 
  99 	UTexture2D* MakeSmoothTexture(const TArray<FColor>& Pixels, int32 W, int32 H, const TCHAR* Name)
 100 	{
 101 		UTexture2D* Texture = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreatePixelTexture(Pixels, W, H, Name, false);
 102 		if (Texture)
 103 		{
 104 			Texture->Filter = TF_Bilinear;
 105 			Texture->SRGB = false;
 106 			Texture->UpdateResource();
 107 		}
 108 		return Texture;
 109 	}
 110 
 111 	const FSlateFontInfo& CyFont(uint8 Face)
 112 	{
 113 		static TMap<uint8, FSlateFontInfo> Cache;
 114 		if (const FSlateFontInfo* Found = Cache.Find(Face))
 115 		{
 116 			return *Found;
 117 		}
 118 		const TCHAR* File = TEXT("Roboto-Regular.ttf");
 119 		switch (Face)
 120 		{
 121 		case 0: File = TEXT("Roboto-BoldCondensed.ttf"); break;
 122 		case 1: File = TEXT("Roboto-Regular.ttf"); break;
 123 		case 2: File = TEXT("Roboto-Medium.ttf"); break;
 124 		case 3: File = TEXT("Roboto-Light.ttf"); break;
 125 		case 4: File = TEXT("Roboto-Black.ttf"); break;
 126 		case 5: File = TEXT("DroidSansMono.ttf"); break;
 127 		default: break;
 128 		}
 129 		const FString Path = FPaths::EngineContentDir() / TEXT("Slate/Fonts") / File;
 130 		return Cache.Add(Face, FSlateFontInfo(Path, 16.f));
 131 	}
 132 }

 136 double AGBHUD::GetRealTime() const

 141 
 142 float AGBHUD::UIScale() const
 143 {
 144 	return Canvas ? FMath::Max(0.1f, Canvas->ClipY / 1080.f) : 1.f;
 145 }
 146 
 147 float AGBHUD::ArtPixel() const
 148 {
 149 	return Canvas ? FMath::Max(2.f, (float)FMath::RoundToInt(Canvas->ClipY / 270.f)) : 4.f;

 151 
 152 void AGBHUD::EnsureArt()
 153 {
 154 	using namespace GBPaintLocal;
 155 	if (!PixelFont)
 156 	{
 157 		PixelFont = NewObject<UGBPixelFont>(this);
 158 		PixelFont->Init();
 159 	}
 160 	if (!RpgStoneTexture)
 161 	{
 162 		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
 163 		int32 W = 0;
 164 		int32 H = 0;
 165 		BuildStone(Pixels, W, H);
 166 		RpgStoneTexture = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreatePixelTexture(Pixels, W, H, TEXT("RpgStone"), true);
 167 	}
 168 	if (!GlowTexture)
 169 	{
 170 		const int32 N = 64;
 171 		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
 172 		Pixels.SetNum(N * N);
 173 		for (int32 Y = 0; Y < N; ++Y)
 174 		{
 175 			for (int32 X = 0; X < N; ++X)
 176 			{
 177 				const float DX = (X + 0.5f) / N * 2.f - 1.f;
 178 				const float DY = (Y + 0.5f) / N * 2.f - 1.f;
 179 				const float R = FMath::Clamp(FMath::Sqrt(DX * DX + DY * DY), 0.f, 1.f);
 180 				const float A = FMath::Square(1.f - R) * (1.f - R * 0.3f);
 181 				Pixels[Y * N + X] = FColor(255, 255, 255, (uint8)FMath::RoundToInt(A * 255.f));
 182 			}
 183 		}
 184 		GlowTexture = MakeSmoothTexture(Pixels, N, N, TEXT("HudGlow"));
 185 	}
 186 	if (!RampTexture)
 187 	{
 188 		const int32 N = 64;
 189 		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
 190 		Pixels.SetNum(N);
 191 		for (int32 X = 0; X < N; ++X)
 192 		{
 193 			Pixels[X] = FColor(255, 255, 255, (uint8)FMath::RoundToInt((1.f - X / (float)(N - 1)) * 255.f));
 194 		}
 195 		RampTexture = MakeSmoothTexture(Pixels, N, 1, TEXT("HudRamp"));
 196 	}
 197 }

 201 void AGBHUD::FillRect(float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FLinearColor& Color)
 202 {
 203 	if (W <= 0.f || H <= 0.f || Color.A <= 0.f)
 204 	{
 205 		return;
 206 	}
 207 	DrawRect(Color, FMath::RoundToFloat(X), FMath::RoundToFloat(Y), FMath::RoundToFloat(X + W) - FMath::RoundToFloat(X), FMath::RoundToFloat(Y + H) - FMath::RoundToFloat(Y));
 208 }
 209 
 210 void AGBHUD::DrawOutline(float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FLinearColor& Color, float Thickness)
 211 {
 212 	const float T = FMath::Max(1.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(Thickness));
 213 	FillRect(X, Y, W, T, Color);
 214 	FillRect(X, Y + H - T, W, T, Color);
 215 	FillRect(X, Y + T, T, H - 2.f * T, Color);
 216 	FillRect(X + W - T, Y + T, T, H - 2.f * T, Color);
 217 }
 218 
 219 void AGBHUD::FillPoly(const TArray<FVector2D>& Points, const FLinearColor& Color)
 220 {
 221 	if (!Canvas || Points.Num() < 3 || Color.A <= 0.f)
 222 	{
 223 		return;
 224 	}
 225 	TArray<FCanvasUVTri> Tris;
 226 	Tris.Reserve(Points.Num() - 2);
 227 	for (int32 i = 1; i + 1 < Points.Num(); ++i)
 228 	{
 229 		FCanvasUVTri Tri;
 230 		Tri.V0_Pos = Points[0];
 231 		Tri.V1_Pos = Points[i];
 232 		Tri.V2_Pos = Points[i + 1];
 233 		Tri.V0_UV = Tri.V1_UV = Tri.V2_UV = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 234 		Tri.V0_Color = Tri.V1_Color = Tri.V2_Color = Color;
 235 		Tris.Add(Tri);
 236 	}
 237 	FCanvasTriangleItem Item(Tris, GWhiteTexture);
 238 	Item.BlendMode = SE_BLEND_Translucent;
 239 	Canvas->DrawItem(Item);
 240 }
 241 
 242 void AGBHUD::StrokePoly(const TArray<FVector2D>& Points, const FLinearColor& Color, float Thickness)
 243 {
 244 	if (Points.Num() < 2 || Color.A <= 0.f)
 245 	{
 246 		return;
 247 	}
 248 	for (int32 i = 0; i < Points.Num(); ++i)
 249 	{
 250 		const FVector2D& A = Points[i];
 251 		const FVector2D& B = Points[(i + 1) % Points.Num()];
 252 		DrawLine(A.X, A.Y, B.X, B.Y, Color, Thickness);
 253 	}
 254 }
 255 
 256 void AGBHUD::DrawGlow(float CX, float CY, float RX, float RY, const FLinearColor& Color)
 257 {
 258 	EnsureArt();
 259 	if (!GlowTexture || Color.A <= 0.f)
 260 	{
 261 		return;
 262 	}
 263 	DrawTexture(GlowTexture, CX - RX, CY - RY, 2.f * RX, 2.f * RY, 0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f, Color, BLEND_Translucent);
 264 }
 265 
 266 void AGBHUD::DrawFade(float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bFadeRight)
 267 {
 268 	EnsureArt();
 269 	if (!RampTexture || W <= 0.f || H <= 0.f)
 270 	{
 271 		return;
 272 	}
 273 	if (bFadeRight)
 274 	{
 275 		DrawTexture(RampTexture, X, Y, W, H, 0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f, Color, BLEND_Translucent);
 276 	}
 277 	else
 278 	{
 279 		DrawTexture(RampTexture, X, Y, W, H, 1.f, 0.f, -1.f, 1.f, Color, BLEND_Translucent);
 280 	}
 281 }

 283 void AGBHUD::DrawSprite(UTexture2D* Texture, float X, float Y, float PixelScale, const FLinearColor& Tint)
 284 {
 285 	if (!Texture)
 286 	{
 287 		return;
 288 	}
 289 	const float W = Texture->GetSizeX() * PixelScale;
 290 	const float H = Texture->GetSizeY() * PixelScale;
 291 	DrawTexture(Texture, FMath::RoundToFloat(X), FMath::RoundToFloat(Y), W, H, 0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f, Tint, BLEND_Translucent);
 292 }
 293 
 294 bool AGBHUD::DrawSpriteFit(FName SpriteId, float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FLinearColor& Tint, bool bSilhouette)
 295 {
 296 	UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Sprites = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this);
 297 	if (!Sprites || SpriteId == NAME_None || !Sprites->HasSprite(SpriteId))
 298 	{
 299 		return false;
 300 	}
 301 	UTexture2D* Texture = Sprites->GetSprite(SpriteId);
 302 	if (!Texture)
 303 	{
 304 		return false;
 305 	}
 306 	const float SW = Texture->GetSizeX();
 307 	const float SH = Texture->GetSizeY();
 308 	float Scale = FMath::Min(W / SW, H / SH);
 309 	// Whole pixels when there is room; a smaller fractional scale only for sprites larger than the box.
 310 	if (Scale >= 1.f)
 311 	{
 312 		Scale = FMath::FloorToFloat(Scale);
 313 	}
 314 	const float DW = SW * Scale;
 315 	const float DH = SH * Scale;
 316 	const float DX = FMath::RoundToFloat(X + (W - DW) * 0.5f);
 317 	const float DY = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y + (H - DH) * 0.5f);
 318 	if (bSilhouette)
 319 	{
 320 		// A soft glow under the icon (holographic look of the cyberpunk skin).
 321 		DrawGlow(DX + DW * 0.5f, DY + DH * 0.5f, DW * 0.7f, DH * 0.7f, GBAlpha(Tint, 0.25f));
 322 	}
 323 	DrawTexture(Texture, DX, DY, DW, DH, 0.f, 0.f, 1.f, 1.f, Tint, BLEND_Translucent);
 324 	return true;
 325 }

 329 void AGBHUD::DrawLabel(const FString& Text, const FLinearColor& Color, float X, float Y, float SizePx, float AlignX, bool bShadow, bool bLarge)
 330 {

 350 	}

 352 }

 372 // ======================================================================== pixel font
 373 
 374 FVector2D AGBHUD::MeasurePixelText(const FString& Text, int32 Scale) const
 375 {
 376 	if (!PixelFont || Text.IsEmpty())
 377 	{
 378 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 379 	}
 380 	return FVector2D(PixelFont->MeasureWidth(Text) * Scale, UGBPixelFont::CapHeight * Scale);
 381 }
 382 
 383 FVector2D AGBHUD::DrawPixelText(const FString& Text, float X, float Y, const FPixelTextStyle& Style, float AlignX)
 384 {
 385 	EnsureArt();
 386 	if (!Canvas || !PixelFont || Text.IsEmpty() || Style.Color.A <= 0.f)
 387 	{
 388 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 389 	}
 390 	const int32 Scale = FMath::Max(1, Style.Scale);
 391 	const FVector2D Size = MeasurePixelText(Text, Scale);
 392 	const float X0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(X - Size.X * AlignX);
 393 	const float Y0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y);
 394 	const FIntPoint Atlas = PixelFont->GetAtlasSize();
 395 	if (Atlas.X <= 0 || Atlas.Y <= 0)
 396 	{
 397 		return Size;
 398 	}
 399 	UTexture2D* InkAtlas = PixelFont->GetInkAtlas(Style.Ink ? *Style.Ink : FGBPixelInk::Flat());
 400 	UTexture2D* OutlineAtlas = PixelFont->GetOutlineAtlas();
 401 	const FLinearColor InkTint = Style.Ink ? FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, Style.Color.A) : Style.Color;
 402 	const float CellH = (float)(UGBPixelFont::CellRows + 2);
 403 
 404 	auto Pass = [&](UTexture2D* Texture, const FLinearColor& Tint, float OffX, float OffY)
 405 	{
 406 		if (!Texture || Tint.A <= 0.f)
 407 		{
 408 			return;
 409 		}
 410 		float Pen = X0;
 411 		for (const TCHAR Char : Text)
 412 		{
 413 			const UGBPixelFont::FGlyph* Glyph = PixelFont->FindGlyph(Char);
 414 			if (!Glyph)
 415 			{
 416 				continue;
 417 			}
 418 			const float CellW = (float)(Glyph->Width + 2);
 419 			DrawTexture(Texture, Pen - Scale + OffX, Y0 - Scale + OffY, CellW * Scale, CellH * Scale,
 420 				Glyph->AtlasX / (float)Atlas.X, Glyph->AtlasY / (float)Atlas.Y, CellW / Atlas.X, CellH / Atlas.Y, Tint, BLEND_Translucent);
 421 			Pen += (Glyph->Width + UGBPixelFont::Spacing) * Scale;
 422 		}
 423 	};
 424 
 425 	const bool bOutline = Style.Outline.A > 0.f;
 426 	if (Style.Shadow.A > 0.f)
 427 	{
 428 		const FLinearColor Shadow = GBAlpha(Style.Shadow, Style.Color.A);
 429 		if (bOutline)
 430 		{
 431 			Pass(OutlineAtlas, Shadow, Scale, Scale);
 432 		}
 433 		Pass(InkAtlas, Shadow, Scale, Scale);
 434 	}
 435 	if (bOutline)
 436 	{
 437 		Pass(OutlineAtlas, GBAlpha(Style.Outline, Style.Color.A), 0.f, 0.f);
 438 	}
 439 	Pass(InkAtlas, InkTint, 0.f, 0.f);
 440 	return Size;
 441 }

 443 // ======================================================================== cyberpunk type
 444 
 445 FVector2D AGBHUD::MeasureCyText(const FString& Text, const FCyTextStyle& Style) const
 446 {
 447 	if (Text.IsEmpty())
 448 	{
 449 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 450 	}
 451 	FSlateFontInfo Font = GBPaintLocal::CyFont((uint8)Style.Face);
 452 	Font.Size = FMath::Max(1.f, Style.Px * UIScale() * 0.75f);
 453 	Font.LetterSpacing = Style.Spacing;
 454 	const TSharedPtr<FSlateFontMeasure> Measure = FEngineFontServices::IsInitialized() ? FEngineFontServices::Get().GetFontMeasure() : nullptr;
 455 	if (!Measure.IsValid())
 456 	{
 457 		return FVector2D(Text.Len() * Style.Px * UIScale() * 0.5f, Style.Px * UIScale());
 458 	}
 459 	const FString Shown = Style.bUpper ? Text.ToUpper() : Text;
 460 	return FVector2D(Measure->Measure(Shown, Font));
 461 }
 462 
 463 FVector2D AGBHUD::DrawCyText(const FString& Text, float X, float Y, const FCyTextStyle& Style, float AlignX)
 464 {
 465 	if (!Canvas || !RuntimeFont || Text.IsEmpty() || Style.Color.A <= 0.f)
 466 	{
 467 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 468 	}
 469 	FSlateFontInfo Font = GBPaintLocal::CyFont((uint8)Style.Face);
 470 	Font.Size = FMath::Max(1.f, Style.Px * UIScale() * 0.75f);
 471 	Font.LetterSpacing = Style.Spacing;
 472 	const FString Shown = Style.bUpper ? Text.ToUpper() : Text;
 473 	const FVector2D Size = MeasureCyText(Text, Style);
 474 	const FVector2D Pos(FMath::RoundToFloat(X - Size.X * AlignX), FMath::RoundToFloat(Y));
 475 	const FText AsText = FText::FromString(Shown);
 476 
 477 	auto Draw = [this, &Font, &AsText](const FVector2D& At, const FLinearColor& Color)
 478 	{
 479 		FCanvasTextItem Item(At, AsText, Font, Color);
 480 		Item.Font = RuntimeFont;
 481 		Item.BlendMode = SE_BLEND_Translucent;
 482 		Canvas->DrawItem(Item);
 483 	};
 484 	const FLinearColor Shadow = Style.Shadow.A > 0.f ? Style.Shadow : (bForceTextShadow ? FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.55f) : FLinearColor::Transparent);
 485 	if (Shadow.A > 0.f)
 486 	{
 487 		const float Off = FMath::Max(1.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(2.f * UIScale()));
 488 		Draw(Pos + FVector2D(Off, Off), GBAlpha(Shadow, Style.Color.A));
 489 	}
 490 	if (Style.Split > 0.f)
 491 	{
 492 		Draw(Pos + FVector2D(-Style.Split, 0.f), GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.7f * Style.Color.A));
 493 		Draw(Pos + FVector2D(Style.Split, 0.f), GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.7f * Style.Color.A));
 494 	}
 495 	Draw(Pos, Style.Color);
 496 	return Size;
 497 }

 499 // ======================================================================== text roles
 500 
 501 FLinearColor AGBHUD::ToneColor(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextTone Tone) const
 502 {
 503 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s)
 504 	{
 505 		switch (Tone)
 506 		{
 507 		case EGBTextTone::Normal: return GBRpg::Bone;
 508 		case EGBTextTone::Accent: return GBRpg::Gold3;
 509 		case EGBTextTone::Muted: return GBRpg::BoneDim;
 510 		case EGBTextTone::Value: return GBRpg::Gold4;
 511 		case EGBTextTone::Good: return GBRpg::Good;
 512 		case EGBTextTone::Bad: return GBHex(TEXT("#FF5A40"));
 513 		case EGBTextTone::Warn: return GBRpg::EmberLight;
 514 		case EGBTextTone::Selected: return GBRpg::Gold4;
 515 		case EGBTextTone::Disabled: return GBRpg::BoneDark;
 516 		}
 517 		return GBRpg::Bone;
 518 	}
 519 	switch (Tone)
 520 	{
 521 	case EGBTextTone::Normal: return GBCy::RedBright;
 522 	case EGBTextTone::Accent: return GBCy::Red;
 523 	case EGBTextTone::Muted: return GBCy::RedDim;
 524 	case EGBTextTone::Value: return GBCy::Cyan;
 525 	case EGBTextTone::Good: return GBCy::Green;
 526 	case EGBTextTone::Bad: return GBCy::Red;
 527 	case EGBTextTone::Warn: return GBCy::Yellow;
 528 	case EGBTextTone::Selected: return GBCy::Pale;
 529 	case EGBTextTone::Disabled: return GBCy::Grey;
 530 	}
 531 	return GBCy::RedBright;
 532 }
 533 
 534 namespace GBPaintLocal
 535 {
 536 	int32 PixScale(float ClipY, float AtTenEighty)
 537 	{
 538 		return FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(AtTenEighty * ClipY / 1080.f));
 539 	}
 540 
 541 	int32 RpgScale(float ClipY, EGBTextSize Size)
 542 	{
 543 		switch (Size)
 544 		{
 545 		case EGBTextSize::Title: return PixScale(ClipY, 6.f);
 546 		case EGBTextSize::Heading: return PixScale(ClipY, 4.f);
 547 		case EGBTextSize::Body: return PixScale(ClipY, 3.f);
 548 		case EGBTextSize::Small: return FMath::Max(2, PixScale(ClipY, 2.f));
 549 		case EGBTextSize::Number: return PixScale(ClipY, 5.f);
 550 		}
 551 		return 2;
 552 	}
 553 
 554 }
 555 
 556 namespace
 557 {
 558 	struct FCyRole
 559 	{
 560 		uint8 Face;
 561 		float Px;
 562 		int32 Spacing;
 563 		bool bUpper;
 564 	};
 565 	FCyRole CyRole(EGBTextSize Size)
 566 	{
 567 		switch (Size)
 568 		{
 569 		case EGBTextSize::Title: return { 0, 54.f, 60, true };
 570 		case EGBTextSize::Heading: return { 0, 30.f, 50, true };
 571 		case EGBTextSize::Body: return { 1, 21.f, 0, false };
 572 		case EGBTextSize::Small: return { 2, 16.f, 0, false };
 573 		case EGBTextSize::Number: return { 0, 64.f, 0, false };
 574 		}
 575 		return { 1, 20.f, 0, false };
 576 	}
 577 }
 578 
 579 FVector2D AGBHUD::UiMeasure(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextSize Size, const FString& Text) const
 580 {
 581 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s)
 582 	{
 583 		return MeasurePixelText(Text, GBPaintLocal::RpgScale(Canvas ? Canvas->ClipY : 1080.f, Size));
 584 	}
 585 	const FCyRole CyR = CyRole(Size);
 586 	FCyTextStyle Style;
 587 	Style.Face = (ECyFace)CyR.Face;
 588 	Style.Px = CyR.Px;
 589 	Style.Spacing = CyR.Spacing;
 590 	Style.bUpper = CyR.bUpper;
 591 	return MeasureCyText(Text, Style);
 592 }
 593 
 594 float AGBHUD::UiLineHeight(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextSize Size) const
 595 {
 596 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s)
 597 	{
 598 		const int32 Scale = GBPaintLocal::RpgScale(Canvas ? Canvas->ClipY : 1080.f, Size);
 599 		return (UGBPixelFont::CellRows + 2) * Scale;
 600 	}
 601 	return CyRole(Size).Px * UIScale() * 1.32f;
 602 }
 603 
 604 FVector2D AGBHUD::UiText(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextSize Size, EGBTextTone Tone, const FString& Text, float X, float Y, float AlignX, const FLinearColor* OverrideColor)
 605 {
 606 	if (Text.IsEmpty())
 607 	{
 608 		return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
 609 	}
 610 	const FLinearColor Color = OverrideColor ? *OverrideColor : ToneColor(Skin, Tone);
 611 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s)
 612 	{
 613 		FPixelTextStyle Style;
 614 		Style.Scale = GBPaintLocal::RpgScale(Canvas ? Canvas->ClipY : 1080.f, Size);
 615 		Style.Color = Color;
 616 		const bool bBig = Size == EGBTextSize::Title || Size == EGBTextSize::Heading || Size == EGBTextSize::Number;
 617 		if (!OverrideColor)
 618 		{
 619 			switch (Tone)
 620 			{
 621 			case EGBTextTone::Normal: Style.Ink = bBig ? &GBRpg::InkGold : &GBRpg::InkBone; break;
 622 			case EGBTextTone::Accent:
 623 			case EGBTextTone::Selected: Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold; break;
 624 			case EGBTextTone::Warn: Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkEmber; break;
 625 			case EGBTextTone::Bad: Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBlood; break;
 626 			case EGBTextTone::Value: Style.Ink = bBig ? &GBRpg::InkBone : nullptr; break;
 627 			default: break;
 628 			}
 629 		}
 630 		Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.85f);
 631 		if (bBig)
 632 		{
 633 			Style.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
 634 		}
 635 		return DrawPixelText(Text, X, Y, Style, AlignX);
 636 	}
 637 	const FCyRole CyR = CyRole(Size);
 638 	FCyTextStyle Style;
 639 	Style.Face = (ECyFace)CyR.Face;
 640 	Style.Px = CyR.Px;
 641 	Style.Spacing = CyR.Spacing;
 642 	Style.bUpper = CyR.bUpper;
 643 	Style.Color = Color;
 644 	return DrawCyText(Text, X, Y, Style, AlignX);
 645 }
 646 
 647 float AGBHUD::UiWrapped(EGBHudSkin Skin, EGBTextSize Size, EGBTextTone Tone, const FString& Text, float X, float Y, float MaxWidth, const FLinearColor* OverrideColor)
 648 {
 649 	TArray<FString> Words;
 650 	Text.ParseIntoArrayWS(Words);
 651 	const float LineH = UiLineHeight(Skin, Size);
 652 	float CurY = Y;
 653 	FString Line;
 654 	for (const FString& Word : Words)
 655 	{
 656 		const FString Candidate = Line.IsEmpty() ? Word : Line + TEXT(" ") + Word;
 657 		if (!Line.IsEmpty() && UiMeasure(Skin, Size, Candidate).X > MaxWidth)
 658 		{
 659 			UiText(Skin, Size, Tone, Line, X, CurY, 0.f, OverrideColor);
 660 			CurY += LineH;
 661 			Line = Word;
 662 		}
 663 		else
 664 		{
 665 			Line = Candidate;
 666 		}
 667 	}
 668 	if (!Line.IsEmpty())
 669 	{
 670 		UiText(Skin, Size, Tone, Line, X, CurY, 0.f, OverrideColor);
 671 		CurY += LineH;
 672 	}
 673 	return CurY - Y;
 674 }

  24 TArray<FVector2D> AGBHUD::CyChamfer(float X, float Y, float W, float H, float Cut, uint8 Corners) const
  25 {
  26 	TArray<FVector2D> Points;
  27 	Points.Reserve(8);
  28 	const float C = FMath::Min(Cut, FMath::Min(W, H) * 0.5f);
  29 	if (Corners & 1) { Points.Add(FVector2D(X, Y + C)); Points.Add(FVector2D(X + C, Y)); }
  30 	else { Points.Add(FVector2D(X, Y)); }
  31 	if (Corners & 2) { Points.Add(FVector2D(X + W - C, Y)); Points.Add(FVector2D(X + W, Y + C)); }
  32 	else { Points.Add(FVector2D(X + W, Y)); }
  33 	if (Corners & 4) { Points.Add(FVector2D(X + W, Y + H - C)); Points.Add(FVector2D(X + W - C, Y + H)); }
  34 	else { Points.Add(FVector2D(X + W, Y + H)); }
  35 	if (Corners & 8) { Points.Add(FVector2D(X + C, Y + H)); Points.Add(FVector2D(X, Y + H - C)); }
  36 	else { Points.Add(FVector2D(X, Y + H)); }
  37 	return Points;
  38 }
  39 
  40 void AGBHUD::CyBox(float X, float Y, float W, float H, float Cut, const FLinearColor& Fill, const FLinearColor& Line, uint8 Corners, float Thick)
  41 {
  42 	X = FMath::RoundToFloat(X);
  43 	Y = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y);
  44 	W = FMath::RoundToFloat(W);
  45 	H = FMath::RoundToFloat(H);
  46 	if (Fill.A > 0.f)
  47 	{
  48 		FillPoly(CyChamfer(X, Y, W, H, Cut, Corners), Fill);
  49 	}
  50 	if (Line.A > 0.f)
  51 	{
  52 		// Lines on pixel centers so 1-pixel edges stay crisp.
  53 		StrokePoly(CyChamfer(X + 0.5f, Y + 0.5f, W - 1.f, H - 1.f, Cut, Corners), Line, Thick);
  54 	}
  55 }
  56 
  57 void AGBHUD::CyBrackets(float X, float Y, float W, float H, float Len, const FLinearColor& Color, float Thick)
  58 {
  59 	const float T = FMath::Max(1.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(Thick));
  60 	FillRect(X, Y, Len, T, Color);
  61 	FillRect(X, Y, T, Len, Color);
  62 	FillRect(X + W - Len, Y, Len, T, Color);
  63 	FillRect(X + W - T, Y, T, Len, Color);
  64 	FillRect(X, Y + H - T, Len, T, Color);
  65 	FillRect(X, Y + H - Len, T, Len, Color);
  66 	FillRect(X + W - Len, Y + H - T, Len, T, Color);
  67 	FillRect(X + W - T, Y + H - Len, T, Len, Color);
  68 }
  69 
  70 void AGBHUD::CySlantBar(float X, float Y, float W, float H, float Slant, float Fraction, int32 Segments, const FLinearColor& Fill, const FLinearColor& Empty, float Trail)
  71 {
  72 	const int32 N = FMath::Max(1, Segments);
  73 	const float Gap = FMath::Max(1.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(2.f * UIScale()));
  74 	const float SW = (W - Slant - (N - 1) * Gap) / N;
  75 	const float Frac = FMath::Clamp(Fraction, 0.f, 1.f);
  76 	const float TrailFrac = FMath::Clamp(Trail, 0.f, 1.f);
  77 	auto Seg = [&](float X0, float Width, const FLinearColor& Color)
  78 	{
  79 		if (Width <= 0.f || Color.A <= 0.f)
  80 		{
  81 			return;
  82 		}
  83 		TArray<FVector2D> Points;
  84 		Points.Add(FVector2D(X0 + Slant, Y));
  85 		Points.Add(FVector2D(X0 + Slant + Width, Y));
  86 		Points.Add(FVector2D(X0 + Width, Y + H));
  87 		Points.Add(FVector2D(X0, Y + H));
  88 		FillPoly(Points, Color);
  89 	};
  90 	for (int32 i = 0; i < N; ++i)
  91 	{
  92 		const float X0 = X + i * (SW + Gap);
  93 		const float Start = (float)i / N;
  94 		const float End = (float)(i + 1) / N;
  95 		const float Filled = FMath::Clamp((Frac - Start) / (End - Start), 0.f, 1.f);
  96 		const float Trailed = FMath::Clamp((TrailFrac - Start) / (End - Start), 0.f, 1.f);
  97 		Seg(X0, SW, Empty);
  98 		if (Trailed > Filled)
  99 		{
 100 			Seg(X0, SW * Trailed, GBAlpha(GBCy::Pale, 0.65f));
 101 		}
 102 		Seg(X0, SW * Filled, Fill);
 103 	}
 104 }
 105 
 106 float AGBHUD::CyKey(float X, float Y, const FString& Key, const FLinearColor& Color, float Height)
 107 {
 108 	const float S = UIScale();
 109 	FCyTextStyle Style;
 110 	Style.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 111 	Style.Px = Height * 0.72f;
 112 	Style.Color = Color;
 113 	Style.bUpper = true;
 114 	const FVector2D Size = MeasureCyText(Key, Style);
 115 	const float H = FMath::RoundToFloat(Height * S);
 116 	const float W = FMath::Max(H, FMath::RoundToFloat(Size.X + 10.f * S));
 117 	CyBox(X, Y, W, H, 0.f, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.6f), Color, 0);
 118 	DrawCyText(Key, X + W * 0.5f, Y + (H - Size.Y) * 0.5f, Style, 0.5f);
 119 	return W;
 120 }
 121 
 122 void AGBHUD::CyScanlines(float X, float Y, float W, float H, float Alpha)
 123 {
 124 	const float Step = FMath::Max(3.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(4.f * UIScale()));
 125 	const FLinearColor Line(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, Alpha);
 126 	for (float LY = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y); LY < Y + H; LY += Step)
 127 	{
 128 		FillRect(X, LY, W, 1.f, Line);
 129 	}
 130 }
 131 
 132 void AGBHUD::CyRuler(float X, float Y, float W, const FLinearColor& Color)
 133 {
 134 	const float S = UIScale();
 135 	FillRect(X, Y, W, 1.f, Color);
 136 	const float Step = FMath::Max(4.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(10.f * S));
 137 	int32 i = 0;
 138 	for (float TX = X; TX <= X + W; TX += Step, ++i)
 139 	{
 140 		const float L = (i % 5 == 0) ? 7.f * S : 3.f * S;
 141 		FillRect(TX, Y - L, 1.f, L, Color);
 142 	}
 143 }
