 147 namespace GBCyLocal
 148 {
 149 	/** Boot glitch after a switch to the gun: strong chromatic split and flicker that settles in 0.35 s. */
 150 	float BootSplit(double Since, float S)
 151 	{
 152 		if (Since < 0.0 || Since > 0.35)
 153 		{
 154 			return 0.f;
 155 		}
 156 		return (float)(1.0 - Since / 0.35) * 6.f * S;
 157 	}
 158 }
 159 
 160 void AGBHUD::DrawCyberHud(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
 161 {
 162 	const float S = UIScale();
 163 	const float W = Canvas->ClipX;
 164 	const float H = Canvas->ClipY;
 165 	const double SinceBoot = GetRealTime() - SkinChangeTime;
 166 	// Flicker in: the HUD blinks twice before it settles.
 167 	if (SinceBoot >= 0.0 && SinceBoot < 0.2 && FMath::Frac((float)SinceBoot * 20.f) < 0.35f)
 168 	{
 169 		return;
 170 	}
 171 
 172 	UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player->GetEquipment();
 173 	AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment ? Equipment->GetActiveItem() : nullptr;
 174 	TGuardValue<bool> ShadowGuard(bForceTextShadow, true);
 175 
 176 	// Low health: the screen edge pulses red.
 177 	if (const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Player->GetAttributes())
 178 	{
 179 		const float Frac = Attributes->GetMaxHealth() > 0.f ? Attributes->GetHealth() / Attributes->GetMaxHealth() : 1.f;
 180 		if (Frac <= 0.3f && Frac > 0.f)
 181 		{
 182 			const float Pulse = 0.5f + 0.5f * FMath::Sin((float)GetRealTime() * 6.f);
 183 			const FLinearColor Edge = GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.18f + 0.12f * Pulse);
 184 			DrawFade(0.f, 0.f, W * 0.16f, H, Edge, true);
 185 			DrawFade(W * 0.84f, 0.f, W * 0.16f, H, Edge, false);
 186 		}
 187 	}
 188 
 189 	DrawCyberVitals(Player, 44.f * S, 38.f * S);
 190 	float EffectsY = 38.f * S + 104.f * S;
 191 	EffectsY = DrawCyberEffects(Player, 44.f * S, EffectsY);
 192 	DrawCyberShield(Player, 44.f * S, EffectsY);
 193 
 194 	if (Item)
 195 	{
 196 		DrawCyberWeapon(Player, Item, W - 52.f * S, H - 44.f * S);
 197 	}
 198 
 199 	// Quick-use item, bottom left.
 200 	if (const UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player->GetInventory())
 201 	{
 202 		const float Box = 76.f * S;
 203 		const float BX = 52.f * S;
 204 		const float BY = H - 44.f * S - Box;
 205 		CyBox(BX, BY, Box, Box, 12.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.62f), GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.75f), 0x5);
 206 		CyBrackets(BX - 4.f * S, BY - 4.f * S, Box + 8.f * S, Box + 8.f * S, 8.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDim, 0.8f));
 207 		const FName QuickId = Inventory->GetQuickUseItem();
 208 		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 209 		const FGBItemDefinition* ItemDef = (Data && QuickId != NAME_None) ? Data->FindItem(QuickId) : nullptr;
 210 		FCyTextStyle Name;
 211 		Name.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 212 		Name.Px = 17.f;
 213 		Name.Spacing = 80;
 214 		Name.bUpper = true;
 215 		Name.Color = GBCy::RedBright;
 216 		if (QuickId != NAME_None)
 217 		{
 218 			const FString* Sprite = ItemDef ? ItemDef->Strings.Find(GBString::Sprite) : nullptr;
 219 			DrawSpriteFit(Sprite ? FName(**Sprite) : NAME_None, BX + 12.f * S, BY + 12.f * S, Box - 24.f * S, Box - 24.f * S, FLinearColor(0.85f, 1.f, 1.f, 1.f), true);
 220 			FCyTextStyle Count;
 221 			Count.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 222 			Count.Px = 24.f;
 223 			Count.Color = GBCy::Pale;
 224 			Count.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.8f);
 225 			DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), Inventory->GetCount(QuickId)), BX + Box - 8.f * S, BY + Box - 32.f * S, Count, 1.f);
 226 			const FString ItemName = (ItemDef && !ItemDef->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? ItemDef->DisplayName : QuickId.ToString();
 227 			DrawCyText(ItemName, BX + Box + 14.f * S, BY + Box - 24.f * S, Name, 0.f);
 228 		}
 229 		else
 230 		{
 231 			Name.Color = GBCy::Grey;
 232 			DrawCyText(TEXT("Empty"), BX + Box + 14.f * S, BY + Box - 24.f * S, Name, 0.f);
 233 		}
 234 		CyKey(BX + Box + 14.f * S, BY + 4.f * S, TEXT("F"), GBCy::Cyan, 24.f);
 235 	}
 236 
 237 	// Skill points: a yellow notice, top right.
 238 	if (const UGBSkillTreeComponent* SkillTree = Player->GetSkillTree(); SkillTree && SkillTree->GetPoints() > 0)
 239 	{
 240 		FCyTextStyle Notice;
 241 		Notice.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 242 		Notice.Px = 19.f;
 243 		Notice.Spacing = 90;
 244 		Notice.bUpper = true;
 245 		Notice.Color = GBCy::Ink;
 246 		const FString Text = FString::Printf(TEXT("Skill point%s available  %d"), SkillTree->GetPoints() == 1 ? TEXT("") : TEXT("s"), SkillTree->GetPoints());
 247 		const FVector2D Size = MeasureCyText(Text, Notice);
 248 		const float NH = 32.f * S;
 249 		const float NW = Size.X + 34.f * S;
 250 		const float NX = W - 52.f * S - NW;
 251 		const float NY = 40.f * S;
 252 		CyBox(NX, NY, NW, NH, 10.f * S, GBCy::Yellow, FLinearColor::Transparent, 0x2);
 253 		FillRect(NX - 8.f * S, NY, 4.f * S, NH, GBCy::Yellow);
 254 		DrawCyText(Text, NX + 14.f * S, NY + (NH - Size.Y) * 0.5f, Notice, 0.f);
 255 		CyKey(NX - 8.f * S - 40.f * S, NY + 3.f * S, TEXT("K"), GBCy::Yellow, 26.f);
 256 	}
 257 }
 258 
 259 void AGBHUD::DrawCyberVitals(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, float X, float Y)
 260 {
 261 	const float S = UIScale();
 262 	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Player->GetAttributes();
 263 	if (!Attributes)
 264 	{
 265 		return;
 266 	}
 267 	const float Split = GBCyLocal::BootSplit(GetRealTime() - SkinChangeTime, S) + GBCyLocal::BootSplit(GetRealTime() - LastDamageTime, S) * 0.6f;
 268 	const float MaxHealth = Attributes->GetMaxHealth();
 269 	const float MaxMana = Attributes->GetMaxMana();
 270 	const float HealthFrac = MaxHealth > 0.f ? Attributes->GetHealth() / MaxHealth : 0.f;
 271 	const float ManaFrac = MaxMana > 0.f ? Attributes->GetMana() / MaxMana : 0.f;
 272 	const bool bLow = HealthFrac <= 0.3f;
 273 
 274 	// Health readout box.
 275 	const float BoxW = 104.f * S;
 276 	const float BoxH = 62.f * S;
 277 	DrawGlow(X + 200.f * S, Y + 30.f * S, 330.f * S, 90.f * S, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.32f));
 278 	CyBox(X, Y, BoxW, BoxH, 12.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedBlack, 0.8f), GBAlpha(bLow ? GBCy::Yellow : GBCy::Red, 0.9f), 0x5);
 279 	FillRect(X - 6.f * S, Y + 10.f * S, 2.f * S, BoxH - 20.f * S, GBCy::Red);
 280 	FCyTextStyle Tag;
 281 	Tag.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
 282 	Tag.Px = 12.f;
 283 	Tag.Color = GBCy::RedDim;
 284 	DrawCyText(TEXT("HP"), X + 14.f * S, Y + 6.f * S, Tag, 0.f);
 285 	FCyTextStyle Big;
 286 	Big.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 287 	Big.Px = 44.f;
 288 	Big.Color = bLow ? GBCy::Yellow : GBCy::Pale;
 289 	Big.Split = Split;
 290 	DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), FMath::CeilToInt(Attributes->GetHealth())), X + BoxW - 12.f * S, Y + 8.f * S, Big, 1.f);
 291 
 292 	// Bars, with a faint red bloom under the health bar.
 293 	const float BX = X + BoxW + 14.f * S;
 294 	DrawGlow(BX + 165.f * S, Y + 14.f * S, 190.f * S, 22.f * S, GBAlpha(bLow ? GBCy::Yellow : GBCy::Red, 0.18f));
 295 	CySlantBar(BX, Y + 6.f * S, 330.f * S, 16.f * S, 7.f * S, HealthFrac, 22, bLow ? GBCy::Yellow : GBCy::Red, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDeep, 0.85f), HealthTrail01);
 296 	CySlantBar(BX, Y + 30.f * S, 250.f * S, 8.f * S, 4.f * S, ManaFrac, 18, GBCy::Cyan, GBAlpha(GBCy::CyanDeep, 0.9f));
 297 	FCyTextStyle Small;
 298 	Small.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 299 	Small.Px = 18.f;
 300 	Small.Color = GBCy::Cyan;
 301 	DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), FMath::FloorToInt(Attributes->GetMana())), BX + 262.f * S, Y + 24.f * S, Small, 0.f);
 302 	Tag.Color = GBAlpha(GBCy::CyanDim, 1.f);
 303 	DrawCyText(TEXT("MANA"), BX + 292.f * S, Y + 28.f * S, Tag, 0.f);
 304 
 305 	// Max health label at the end of the health bar.
 306 	Tag.Color = GBCy::RedDim;
 307 	DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("/%d"), FMath::RoundToInt(MaxHealth)), BX + 338.f * S, Y + 6.f * S, Tag, 0.f);
 308 
 309 	// Dash.
 310 	const float Remaining = Player->GetDashCooldownRemaining();
 311 	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = Player->GetStats();
 312 	const float Total = Stats ? Stats->GetStat(GBStat::DashCooldown, 0.f) : 0.f;
 313 	const float DashFrac = (Remaining <= 0.f || Total <= 0.f) ? 1.f : 1.f - Remaining / Total;
 314 	const bool bDashReady = DashFrac >= 1.f;
 315 	Tag.Color = bDashReady ? GBCy::Cyan : GBCy::RedDim;
 316 	DrawCyText(TEXT("DASH"), BX, Y + 44.f * S, Tag, 0.f);
 317 	CySlantBar(BX + 40.f * S, Y + 47.f * S, 110.f * S, 6.f * S, 3.f * S, DashFrac, 4, bDashReady ? GBCy::Cyan : GBCy::RedDim, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDeep, 0.8f));
 318 	if (bLow)
 319 	{
 320 		FCyTextStyle Warn;
 321 		Warn.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 322 		Warn.Px = 16.f;
 323 		Warn.Spacing = 150;
 324 		Warn.bUpper = true;
 325 		Warn.Color = GBAlpha(GBCy::Yellow, 0.6f + 0.4f * FMath::Sin((float)GetRealTime() * 8.f));
 326 		DrawCyText(TEXT("Critical"), BX + 170.f * S, Y + 41.f * S, Warn, 0.f);
 327 	}
 328 }
 329 
 330 void AGBHUD::DrawCyberWeapon(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, AGBHeldItem* Item, float Right, float Bottom)
 331 {
 332 	// Layout, right-aligned on Right:
 333 	//   [element tag]  WEAPON NAME
 334 	//   -------------------------------------- rule
 335 	//   [enchant]          AMMO | RESERVE
 336 	//   [enchanted / MP]   ////////////// pips
 337 	const float S = UIScale();
 338 	const float Split = GBCyLocal::BootSplit(GetRealTime() - SkinChangeTime, S);
 339 	const float BlockW = 300.f * S;
 340 	const float Left = Right - BlockW;
 341 	DrawGlow(Right - BlockW * 0.45f, Bottom - 70.f * S, BlockW * 0.75f, 130.f * S, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.34f));
 342 
 343 	FCyTextStyle NameStyle;
 344 	NameStyle.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 345 	NameStyle.Px = 26.f;
 346 	NameStyle.Spacing = 140;
 347 	NameStyle.bUpper = true;
 348 	NameStyle.Color = GBCy::Red;
 349 	NameStyle.Split = Split;
 350 
 351 	const float NameY = Bottom - 158.f * S;
 352 	const float RuleY = Bottom - 118.f * S;
 353 	const float NumTop = Bottom - 110.f * S;
 354 	const float PipsH = 12.f * S;
 355 	const float PipsY = Bottom - PipsH;
 356 
 357 	const FVector2D NameSize = DrawCyText(Item->GetDisplayName(), Right, NameY, NameStyle, 1.f);
 358 	FillRect(Left + 8.f * S, RuleY, BlockW - 8.f * S, 1.f, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.65f));
 359 	FillRect(Right - 60.f * S, RuleY - 1.f * S, 60.f * S, 3.f * S, GBCy::Red);
 360 	TArray<FVector2D> Tick = { FVector2D(Left, RuleY + 6.f * S), FVector2D(Left + 6.f * S, RuleY), FVector2D(Left + 10.f * S, RuleY), FVector2D(Left + 4.f * S, RuleY + 6.f * S) };
 361 	FillPoly(Tick, GBCy::Red);
 362 
 363 	// Weapon enchant: the active element as a tag left of the name.
 364 	UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook();
 365 	const EGBElement Element = Spellbook ? Spellbook->GetEnchantElement() : EGBElement::Fire;
 366 	const FLinearColor ElementColor = GBHudText::ElementColor(Element);
 367 	const FGBActiveStatusEffect* Enchant = nullptr;
 368 	if (const UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player->GetStatusEffects())
 369 	{
 370 		for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Effect : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
 371 		{
 372 			if (Effect.EffectId.ToString().StartsWith(TEXT("Enchant_")))
 373 			{
 374 				Enchant = &Effect;
 375 				break;
 376 			}
 377 		}
 378 	}
 379 	if (Enchant)
 380 	{
 381 		FCyTextStyle Tag;
 382 		Tag.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 383 		Tag.Px = 15.f;
 384 		Tag.Spacing = 120;
 385 		Tag.bUpper = true;
 386 		Tag.Color = GBCy::Ink;
 387 		const FString TagText = GBHudText::ElementName(Element);
 388 		const FVector2D TS = MeasureCyText(TagText, Tag);
 389 		const float TW = TS.X + 18.f * S;
 390 		const float TH = 22.f * S;
 391 		const float TX = Right - NameSize.X - 14.f * S - TW;
 392 		const float TY = NameY + (NameSize.Y - TH) * 0.5f;
 393 		CyBox(TX, TY, TW, TH, 6.f * S, ElementColor, FLinearColor::Transparent, 0x4);
 394 		DrawCyText(TagText, TX + TW * 0.5f, TY + (TH - TS.Y) * 0.5f, Tag, 0.5f);
 395 	}
 396 
 397 	if (Item->UsesAmmo())
 398 	{
 399 		const FGBAmmoState Ammo = Item->GetAmmo();
 400 		const int32 MagSize = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(Item->GetStat(GBStat::MagazineSize, 0.f)));
 401 		const bool bPerRound = Item->ReloadsPerRound();
 402 
 403 		FCyTextStyle Reserve;
 404 		Reserve.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 405 		Reserve.Px = 36.f;
 406 		Reserve.Color = Ammo.SpareMagazines > 0 ? GBCy::Cyan : GBCy::Grey;
 407 		const FString ReserveText = FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), Ammo.SpareMagazines);
 408 		const FVector2D ReserveSize = MeasureCyText(ReserveText, Reserve);
 409 		FCyTextStyle Unit;
 410 		Unit.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
 411 		Unit.Px = 12.f;
 412 		Unit.Color = GBCy::CyanDim;
 413 		const FString UnitText = bPerRound ? TEXT("SHELLS") : TEXT("MAGS");
 414 		const float ReserveW = FMath::Max(ReserveSize.X, MeasureCyText(UnitText, Unit).X);
 415 		const float ReserveX = Right - ReserveW;
 416 		DrawCyText(ReserveText, ReserveX, NumTop + 8.f * S, Reserve, 0.f);
 417 		DrawCyText(UnitText, ReserveX, NumTop + 8.f * S + ReserveSize.Y * 0.92f, Unit, 0.f);
 418 
 419 		FCyTextStyle BigAmmo;
 420 		BigAmmo.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 421 		BigAmmo.Px = 80.f;
 422 		BigAmmo.Color = Ammo.InMagazine > 0 ? GBCy::Pale : GBCy::Red;
 423 		BigAmmo.Split = Split;
 424 		const FString AmmoText = FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), Ammo.InMagazine);
 425 		const float DivX = ReserveX - 16.f * S;
 426 		FillRect(DivX, NumTop + 14.f * S, 1.f, 62.f * S, GBCy::RedDim);
 427 		DrawCyText(AmmoText, DivX - 16.f * S, NumTop - 6.f * S, BigAmmo, 1.f);
 428 
 429 		// Magazine pips, or the reload bar.
 430 		const float PipsLeft = Right - 260.f * S;
 431 		if (Item->IsReloading())
 432 		{
 433 			CySlantBar(PipsLeft, PipsY + 2.f * S, Right - PipsLeft, 8.f * S, 4.f * S, Item->GetReloadProgress(), 14, GBCy::Yellow, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDeep, 0.85f));
 434 			FCyTextStyle Reload;
 435 			Reload.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 436 			Reload.Px = 15.f;
 437 			Reload.Spacing = 220;
 438 			Reload.bUpper = true;
 439 			Reload.Color = GBAlpha(GBCy::Yellow, 0.65f + 0.35f * FMath::Sin((float)GetRealTime() * 12.f));
 440 			DrawCyText(TEXT("Reloading"), PipsLeft, PipsY - 20.f * S, Reload, 0.f);
 441 		}
 442 		else if (MagSize > 0)
 443 		{
 444 			const float Gap = 3.f * S;
 445 			const float PipW = FMath::Clamp(((Right - PipsLeft) - (MagSize - 1) * Gap) / MagSize, 3.f * S, 10.f * S);
 446 			const float TotalW = MagSize * PipW + (MagSize - 1) * Gap;
 447 			const float PX0 = Right - TotalW;
 448 			const float Sl = 4.f * S;
 449 			for (int32 i = 0; i < MagSize; ++i)
 450 			{
 451 				const bool bLoaded = i >= MagSize - Ammo.InMagazine;
 452 				const float PX = PX0 + i * (PipW + Gap);
 453 				TArray<FVector2D> Pip = { FVector2D(PX + Sl, PipsY), FVector2D(PX + PipW + Sl, PipsY), FVector2D(PX + PipW, PipsY + PipsH), FVector2D(PX, PipsY + PipsH) };
 454 				FillPoly(Pip, bLoaded ? GBCy::Red : GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDeep, 0.95f));
 455 			}
 456 			if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
 457 			{
 458 				FCyTextStyle Warn;
 459 				Warn.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 460 				Warn.Px = 15.f;
 461 				Warn.Spacing = 220;
 462 				Warn.bUpper = true;
 463 				Warn.Color = Ammo.SpareMagazines > 0 ? GBCy::Yellow : GBCy::Red;
 464 				if (Ammo.SpareMagazines > 0)
 465 				{
 466 					const float KW = CyKey(PX0, PipsY - 28.f * S, TEXT("R"), GBCy::Yellow, 22.f);
 467 					DrawCyText(TEXT("Reload"), PX0 + KW + 8.f * S, PipsY - 26.f * S, Warn, 0.f);
 468 				}
 469 				else
 470 				{
 471 					DrawCyText(TEXT("No ammo"), PX0, PipsY - 26.f * S, Warn, 0.f);
 472 				}
 473 			}
 474 		}
 475 	}
 476 
 477 	// Enchant module at the left of the block: icon, key, cost or the enchant's time left.
 478 	if (!Spellbook)
 479 	{
 480 		return;
 481 	}
 482 	const FName EnchantId = Spellbook->GetEnchantSpell();
 483 	const float Box = 56.f * S;
 484 	const float EX = Right - Box;
 485 	const float EY = NameY - Box - 22.f * S;
 486 	CyBox(EX, EY, Box, Box, 10.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.72f), GBAlpha(ElementColor, 0.95f), 0x5);
 487 	DrawGlow(EX + Box * 0.5f, EY + Box * 0.5f, Box * 0.6f, Box * 0.6f, GBAlpha(ElementColor, Enchant ? 0.35f : 0.16f));
 488 	DrawSpriteFit(GBHudText::SpellIconId(EnchantId), EX + 10.f * S, EY + 10.f * S, Box - 20.f * S, Box - 20.f * S, FLinearColor::White);
 489 	CyKey(EX - 10.f * S, EY - 10.f * S, TEXT("T"), GBCy::Cyan, 20.f);
 490 	const float InfoRight = EX - 14.f * S;
 491 	if (EnchantId != NAME_None)
 492 	{
 493 		const float Remaining = Spellbook->GetCooldownRemaining(EnchantId);
 494 		const EGBCastFailReason Reason = Spellbook->CanCast(EnchantId);
 495 		if (Remaining > 0.f)
 496 		{
 497 			const float Duration = Spellbook->GetCooldownDuration(EnchantId);
 498 			const float Frac = Duration > 0.f ? FMath::Clamp(Remaining / Duration, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
 499 			FillRect(EX + 2.f * S, EY + Box * (1.f - Frac), Box - 4.f * S, Box * Frac - 2.f * S, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.6f));
 500 		}
 501 		else if (Reason != EGBCastFailReason::None && Reason != EGBCastFailReason::OnCooldown)
 502 		{
 503 			FillRect(EX + 2.f * S, EY + 2.f * S, Box - 4.f * S, Box - 4.f * S, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.55f));
 504 		}
 505 		FCyTextStyle Label;
 506 		Label.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
 507 		Label.Px = 12.f;
 508 		Label.Color = GBCy::RedBright;
 509 		FCyTextStyle Value;
 510 		Value.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 511 		Value.Px = 22.f;
 512 		FString LabelText;
 513 		FString ValueText;
 514 		if (Enchant)
 515 		{
 516 			LabelText = TEXT("ENCHANTED");
 517 			ValueText = FString::Printf(TEXT("%.1fs"), Enchant->TimeRemaining);
 518 			Value.Color = ElementColor;
 519 		}
 520 		else if (Remaining > 0.f)
 521 		{
 522 			LabelText = TEXT("ENCHANT COOLDOWN");
 523 			ValueText = FString::Printf(TEXT("%.1fs"), Remaining);
 524 			Value.Color = GBCy::Pale;
 525 		}
 526 		else
 527 		{
 528 			LabelText = FString::Printf(TEXT("%s ENCHANT"), *GBHudText::ElementName(Element).ToUpper());
 529 			ValueText = FString::Printf(TEXT("%d MP"), FMath::RoundToInt(Spellbook->GetManaCost(EnchantId)));
 530 			Value.Color = Reason == EGBCastFailReason::NotEnoughMana ? GBCy::Red : GBCy::Cyan;
 531 		}
 532 		DrawCyText(LabelText, InfoRight, EY + 4.f * S, Label, 1.f);
 533 		DrawCyText(ValueText, InfoRight, EY + 18.f * S, Value, 1.f);
 534 		if (Enchant)
 535 		{
 536 			const float Frac = Enchant->TotalDuration > 0.f ? FMath::Clamp(Enchant->TimeRemaining / Enchant->TotalDuration, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
 537 			CySlantBar(InfoRight - 110.f * S, EY + Box - 8.f * S, 110.f * S, 5.f * S, 3.f * S, Frac, 10, ElementColor, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDeep, 0.85f));
 538 		}
 539 	}
 540 }
 541 
 542 float AGBHUD::DrawCyberEffects(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, float X, float Y)
 543 {
 544 	const UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player->GetStatusEffects();
 545 	if (!Effects)
 546 	{
 547 		return Y;
 548 	}
 549 	const float S = UIScale();
 550 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 551 	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Effect : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
 552 	{
 553 		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindStatusEffect(Effect.EffectId) : nullptr;
 554 		FString Name = (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : Effect.EffectId.ToString();
 555 		if (Effect.Stacks > 1)
 556 		{
 557 			Name += FString::Printf(TEXT(" x%d"), Effect.Stacks);
 558 		}
 559 		const float EW = 236.f * S;
 560 		const float EH = 26.f * S;
 561 		CyBox(X, Y, EW, EH, 7.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.6f), GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDim, 0.9f), 0x4);
 562 		FillRect(X, Y, 3.f * S, EH, GBCy::Red);
 563 		FCyTextStyle Text;
 564 		Text.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 565 		Text.Px = 16.f;
 566 		Text.Spacing = 80;
 567 		Text.bUpper = true;
 568 		Text.Color = GBCy::RedBright;
 569 		DrawCyText(GBHudText::Abbreviate(Name, 22), X + 12.f * S, Y + 3.f * S, Text, 0.f);
 570 		if (Effect.TotalDuration > 0.f)
 571 		{
 572 			FCyTextStyle Time;
 573 			Time.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
 574 			Time.Px = 13.f;
 575 			Time.Color = GBCy::Cyan;
 576 			DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%04.1f"), Effect.TimeRemaining), X + EW - 10.f * S, Y + 5.f * S, Time, 1.f);
 577 			FillRect(X + 3.f * S, Y + EH - 2.f * S, (EW - 10.f * S) * FMath::Clamp(Effect.TimeRemaining / Effect.TotalDuration, 0.f, 1.f), 2.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.9f));
 578 		}
 579 		Y += EH + 6.f * S;
 580 	}
 581 	return Y;
 582 }
 583 
 584 void AGBHUD::DrawCyberShield(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, float X, float Y)
 585 {
 586 	const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook();
 587 	if (!Spellbook || !Spellbook->HasShield())
 588 	{
 589 		return;
 590 	}
 591 	const float S = UIScale();
 592 	const bool bRaised = Spellbook->IsBlockRaised();
 593 	const FLinearColor Ice = GBHudText::ElementColor(EGBElement::Ice);
 594 	const float EW = 236.f * S;
 595 	const float EH = 34.f * S;
 596 	CyBox(X, Y, EW, EH, 8.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.6f), GBAlpha(Ice, 0.9f), 0x5);
 597 	FCyTextStyle Text;
 598 	Text.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 599 	Text.Px = 16.f;
 600 	Text.Spacing = 100;
 601 	Text.bUpper = true;
 602 	Text.Color = Ice;
 603 	DrawCyText(bRaised ? TEXT("Blocking") : TEXT("Ice shield"), X + 10.f * S, Y + 3.f * S, Text, 0.f);
 604 	FCyTextStyle Time;
 605 	Time.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
 606 	Time.Px = 13.f;
 607 	Time.Color = Ice;
 608 	DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%.1fs"), FMath::Max(0.f, Spellbook->GetShieldTimeRemaining())), X + EW - 10.f * S, Y + 5.f * S, Time, 1.f);
 609 	CySlantBar(X + 10.f * S, Y + EH - 10.f * S, EW - 20.f * S, 5.f * S, 3.f * S, Spellbook->GetShieldHealthFraction(), 12, Ice, GBAlpha(GBCy::CyanDeep, 0.9f));
 610 	if (!bRaised)
 611 	{
 612 		CyKey(X + EW + 8.f * S, Y + 5.f * S, TEXT("Q"), GBCy::Cyan, 24.f);
 613 	}
 614 }

The UObject references in the painting layer are held by UPROPERTY members, and its drawing lambdas run within their local scope. I’m now checking menu hit registration and the status-effect calls between capturing and using the enchant pointer.

  32 void AGBHUD::RpgStone(float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FLinearColor& Tint)
  33 {
  34 	EnsureArt();
  35 	if (!RpgStoneTexture || W <= 0.f || H <= 0.f)
  36 	{
  37 		return;
  38 	}
  39 	const float P = ArtPixel();
  40 	const float TileW = RpgStoneTexture->GetSizeX() * P;
  41 	const float TileH = RpgStoneTexture->GetSizeY() * P;
  42 	// UVs from the screen position: panels next to each other share one masonry.
  43 	const float X0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(X);
  44 	const float Y0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y);
  45 	DrawTexture(RpgStoneTexture, X0, Y0, FMath::RoundToFloat(W), FMath::RoundToFloat(H), X0 / TileW, Y0 / TileH, W / TileW, H / TileH, Tint, BLEND_Translucent);
  46 }
  47 
  48 namespace GBRpgLocal
  49 {
  50 	/** One bevel ring T pixels thick: top and left in A, bottom and right in B. */
  51 	void Ring(AGBHUD* Hud, TFunctionRef<void(float, float, float, float, const FLinearColor&)> Fill, float X, float Y, float W, float H, float T, const FLinearColor& A, const FLinearColor& B)
  52 	{
  53 		Fill(X, Y, W, T, A);
  54 		Fill(X, Y + T, T, H - T, A);
  55 		Fill(X + T, Y + H - T, W - T, T, B);
  56 		Fill(X + W - T, Y + T, T, H - 2.f * T, B);
  57 	}
  58 }
  59 
  60 void AGBHUD::RpgFrame(float X, float Y, float W, float H, ERpgFrame Kind)
  61 {
  62 	using namespace GBRpgLocal;
  63 	const float P = ArtPixel();
  64 	auto Fill = [this](float FX, float FY, float FW, float FH, const FLinearColor& C) { FillRect(FX, FY, FW, FH, C); };
  65 	switch (Kind)
  66 	{
  67 	case ERpgFrame::Iron:
  68 		Ring(this, Fill, X, Y, W, H, P, GBRpg::Outline, GBRpg::Outline);
  69 		Ring(this, Fill, X + P, Y + P, W - 2 * P, H - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron4, GBRpg::Iron1);
  70 		Ring(this, Fill, X + 2 * P, Y + 2 * P, W - 4 * P, H - 4 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron2, GBRpg::Iron0);
  71 		Ring(this, Fill, X + 3 * P, Y + 3 * P, W - 6 * P, H - 6 * P, P, GBRpg::Outline, GBRpg::Outline);
  72 		break;
  73 	case ERpgFrame::Gold:
  74 		Ring(this, Fill, X, Y, W, H, P, GBRpg::Outline, GBRpg::Outline);
  75 		Ring(this, Fill, X + P, Y + P, W - 2 * P, H - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Gold3, GBRpg::Gold1);
  76 		Ring(this, Fill, X + 2 * P, Y + 2 * P, W - 4 * P, H - 4 * P, P, GBRpg::Gold1, GBRpg::Gold3);
  77 		break;
  78 	case ERpgFrame::Inset:
  79 		Ring(this, Fill, X, Y, W, H, P, GBRpg::Outline, GBRpg::Stone3);
  80 		Ring(this, Fill, X + P, Y + P, W - 2 * P, H - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::StoneDeep, GBRpg::Stone2);
  81 		break;
  82 	case ERpgFrame::Bone:
  83 		Ring(this, Fill, X, Y, W, H, P, GBRpg::Outline, GBRpg::Outline);
  84 		Ring(this, Fill, X + P, Y + P, W - 2 * P, H - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Bone, GBRpg::BoneDim);
  85 		break;
  86 	}
  87 }
  88 
  89 void AGBHUD::RpgRivet(float X, float Y)
  90 {
  91 	const float P = ArtPixel();
  92 	FillRect(X + P, Y, 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Outline);
  93 	FillRect(X, Y + P, P, 2 * P, GBRpg::Outline);
  94 	FillRect(X + 3 * P, Y + P, P, 2 * P, GBRpg::Outline);
  95 	FillRect(X + P, Y + 3 * P, 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Outline);
  96 	FillRect(X + P, Y + P, P, P, GBRpg::Iron4);
  97 	FillRect(X + 2 * P, Y + P, P, P, GBRpg::Iron3);
  98 	FillRect(X + P, Y + 2 * P, P, P, GBRpg::Iron2);
  99 	FillRect(X + 2 * P, Y + 2 * P, P, P, GBRpg::Iron1);
 100 }
 101 
 102 void AGBHUD::RpgGem(float CX, float CY, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bLit)
 103 {
 104 	using namespace GBRpgLocal;
 105 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 106 	// A 7 x 7 cut gem: outline, dark lower right, light upper left, one white specular pixel.
 107 	static const TCHAR* Rows[] = { TEXT("..ooo.."), TEXT(".oLLBo."), TEXT("oLSBBDo"), TEXT("oLBBBDo"), TEXT("oBBBDDo"), TEXT(".oDDDo."), TEXT("..ooo..") };
 108 	const FLinearColor Base = bLit ? Color : Darker(Color, 0.35f);
 109 	const FLinearColor Light = bLit ? Lighter(Color, 0.45f) : Darker(Color, 0.5f);
 110 	const FLinearColor Dark = Darker(Color, bLit ? 0.45f : 0.2f);
 111 	const FLinearColor Spec = bLit ? GBRpg::Gold4 : Darker(Color, 0.6f);
 112 	const float X0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(CX - 3.5f * P);
 113 	const float Y0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(CY - 3.5f * P);
 114 	if (bLit)
 115 	{
 116 		DrawGlow(CX, CY, 9.f * P, 9.f * P, GBAlpha(Color, 0.35f));
 117 	}
 118 	for (int32 Row = 0; Row < 7; ++Row)
 119 	{
 120 		for (int32 Col = 0; Col < 7; ++Col)
 121 		{
 122 			const TCHAR C = Rows[Row][Col];
 123 			const FLinearColor* Pick = nullptr;
 124 			switch (C)
 125 			{
 126 			case TEXT('o'): Pick = &GBRpg::Outline; break;
 127 			case TEXT('L'): Pick = &Light; break;
 128 			case TEXT('B'): Pick = &Base; break;
 129 			case TEXT('D'): Pick = &Dark; break;
 130 			case TEXT('S'): Pick = &Spec; break;
 131 			default: break;
 132 			}
 133 			if (Pick)
 134 			{
 135 				FillRect(X0 + Col * P, Y0 + Row * P, P, P, *Pick);
 136 			}
 137 		}
 138 	}
 139 }
 140 
 141 void AGBHUD::RpgWindow(float X, float Y, float W, float H)
 142 {
 143 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 144 	// Drop shadow, stone, then the frame on top.
 145 	FillRect(X + 2 * P, Y + 2 * P, W, H, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.5f));
 146 	RpgStone(X, Y, W, H);
 147 	// A warm light pool in the upper part of the window, darker corners.
 148 	DrawGlow(X + W * 0.5f, Y + H * 0.3f, W * 0.6f, H * 0.6f, FLinearColor(0.35f, 0.22f, 0.12f, 0.12f));
 149 	RpgFrame(X, Y, W, H, ERpgFrame::Iron);
 150 	// Gold trim just inside the iron.
 151 	const float In = 4 * P;
 152 	DrawOutline(X + In, Y + In, W - 2 * In, H - 2 * In, GBRpg::Gold1, P);
 153 	FillRect(X + In + P, Y + In + P, W - 2 * In - 2 * P, P, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Outline, 0.6f));
 154 	// Corner bosses.
 155 	const float B = 4 * P;
 156 	RpgRivet(X + P * 0.f, Y + P * 0.f);
 157 	RpgRivet(X + W - B, Y);
 158 	RpgRivet(X, Y + H - B);
 159 	RpgRivet(X + W - B, Y + H - B);
 160 }
 161 
 162 void AGBHUD::RpgWell(float X, float Y, float W, float H)
 163 {
 164 	FillRect(X, Y, W, H, GBAlpha(GBRpg::StoneDeep, 0.88f));
 165 	RpgFrame(X, Y, W, H, ERpgFrame::Inset);
 166 }
 167 
 168 FBox2D AGBHUD::RpgPlaque(const FString& Title, float CX, float Y, int32 Scale)
 169 {
 170 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 171 	const FVector2D Size = MeasurePixelText(Title, Scale);
 172 	const float W = FMath::RoundToFloat((Size.X + 20.f * P) / P) * P;
 173 	const float H = FMath::RoundToFloat((Size.Y + 8.f * P) / P) * P;
 174 	const float X = FMath::RoundToFloat(CX - W * 0.5f);
 175 	// Pointed ends: three steps on each side, then the body.
 176 	for (int32 Step = 1; Step <= 3; ++Step)
 177 	{
 178 		const float Inset = Step * P;
 179 		const float SH = H - 2.f * Step * P;
 180 		FillRect(X - Step * P, Y + Inset, P, SH, Step == 3 ? GBRpg::Outline : GBRpg::Gold1);
 181 		FillRect(X + W + (Step - 1) * P, Y + Inset, P, SH, Step == 3 ? GBRpg::Outline : GBRpg::Gold1);
 182 	}
 183 	FillRect(X, Y, W, H, GBRpg::Stone1);
 184 	RpgStone(X + 2 * P, Y + 2 * P, W - 4 * P, H - 4 * P, FLinearColor(0.85f, 0.8f, 0.8f, 1.f));
 185 	RpgFrame(X, Y, W, H, ERpgFrame::Gold);
 186 	FPixelTextStyle Style;
 187 	Style.Scale = Scale;
 188 	Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold;
 189 	Style.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
 190 	Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.7f);
 191 	DrawPixelText(Title, CX, Y + (H - Size.Y) * 0.5f, Style, 0.5f);
 192 	return FBox2D(FVector2D(X, Y), FVector2D(X + W, Y + H));
 193 }
 194 
 195 void AGBHUD::RpgDivider(float X, float Y, float W)
 196 {
 197 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 198 	FillRect(X, Y, W, P, GBRpg::Gold1);
 199 	FillRect(X, Y + P, W, P, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Outline, 0.8f));
 200 	FillRect(X + 2 * P, Y - P, W - 4 * P, P, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Gold2, 0.35f));
 201 	RpgGem(X + W * 0.5f, Y + 0.5f * P, GBRpg::Gold2, true);
 202 }
 203 
 204 void AGBHUD::RpgTube(float X, float Y, float W, float H, float Fraction, const FLinearColor Ramp[4], float Trail)
 205 {
 206 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 207 	X = FMath::RoundToFloat(X);
 208 	Y = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y);
 209 	const float Cap = 3 * P;
 210 	// Glass body.
 211 	FillRect(X, Y, W, H, GBRpg::Outline);
 212 	const float GX = X + Cap;
 213 	const float GY = Y + P;
 214 	const float GW = W - 2 * Cap;
 215 	const float GH = H - 2 * P;
 216 	FillRect(GX, GY, GW, GH, FMath::Lerp(GBRpg::StoneDeep, Ramp[0], 0.25f));
 217 	// Liquid, snapped to whole art pixels.
 218 	const float Frac = FMath::Clamp(Fraction, 0.f, 1.f);
 219 	const float LW = FMath::RoundToFloat(GW * Frac / P) * P;
 220 	if (Trail > Frac)
 221 	{
 222 		const float TW = FMath::RoundToFloat(GW * FMath::Clamp(Trail, 0.f, 1.f) / P) * P;
 223 		FillRect(GX + LW, GY, TW - LW, GH, GBAlpha(Ramp[3], 0.55f));
 224 	}
 225 	if (LW > 0.f)
 226 	{
 227 		const int32 Rows = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(GH / P));
 228 		for (int32 Row = 0; Row < Rows; ++Row)
 229 		{
 230 			const FLinearColor& C = Row == 0 ? Ramp[2] : (Row == Rows - 1 ? Ramp[0] : Ramp[1]);
 231 			FillRect(GX, GY + Row * P, LW, P, C);
 232 		}
 233 		// Shine streak along the second row and a lit meniscus at the front.
 234 		if (Rows > 3)
 235 		{
 236 			FillRect(GX + 2 * P, GY + P, FMath::Max(0.f, LW - 6 * P), P, GBAlpha(Ramp[3], 0.55f));
 237 		}
 238 		FillRect(GX + LW - P, GY, P, GH, Ramp[2]);
 239 		// Two drifting motes inside the liquid.
 240 		const float Time = (float)GetRealTime();
 241 		for (int32 i = 0; i < 2; ++i)
 242 		{
 243 			const float Phase = FMath::Frac(Time * (0.11f + 0.07f * i) + i * 0.47f);
 244 			const float MX = GX + FMath::RoundToFloat(Phase * (LW - 2 * P) / P) * P;
 245 			const float MY = GY + (Rows > 3 ? (2 + i) * P : 0.f);
 246 			if (MX + P < GX + LW - P)
 247 			{
 248 				FillRect(MX, MY, P, P, GBAlpha(Ramp[3], 0.8f));
 249 			}
 250 		}
 251 	}
 252 	// Glass glare across the whole tube.
 253 	FillRect(GX, GY, GW, P, GBAlpha(FLinearColor::White, 0.10f));
 254 	// Iron end caps.
 255 	for (int32 Side = 0; Side < 2; ++Side)
 256 	{
 257 		const float CX = Side == 0 ? X : X + W - Cap;
 258 		FillRect(CX, Y, Cap, H, GBRpg::Outline);
 259 		FillRect(CX + P, Y + P, Cap - 2 * P, H - 2 * P, GBRpg::Iron2);
 260 		FillRect(CX + P, Y + P, Cap - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron4);
 261 		FillRect(CX + P, Y + H - 2 * P, Cap - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron0);
 262 	}
 263 }
 264 
 265 void AGBHUD::RpgKeyCap(float X, float Y, const FString& Key, float* OutWidth)
 266 {
 267 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 268 	const int32 Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
 269 	const FVector2D Size = MeasurePixelText(Key, Scale);
 270 	const float W = FMath::Max(FMath::CeilToFloat((Size.X + 6 * P) / P) * P, 9 * P);
 271 	const float H = FMath::CeilToFloat((Size.Y + 5 * P) / P) * P;
 272 	FillRect(X, Y, W, H, GBRpg::Outline);
 273 	FillRect(X + P, Y + P, W - 2 * P, H - 2 * P, GBRpg::Iron1);
 274 	FillRect(X + P, Y + P, W - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron3);
 275 	FillRect(X + P, Y + H - 2 * P, W - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron0);
 276 	FPixelTextStyle Style;
 277 	Style.Scale = Scale;
 278 	Style.Color = GBRpg::Bone;
 279 	Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
 280 	DrawPixelText(Key, X + W * 0.5f, Y + FMath::RoundToFloat((H - Size.Y) * 0.5f / Scale) * Scale - P * 0.f, Style, 0.5f);
 281 	if (OutWidth)
 282 	{
 283 		*OutWidth = W;
 284 	}
 285 }
 286 
 287 void AGBHUD::RpgCursor(float X, float CY)
 288 {
 289 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 290 	static const TCHAR* Rows[] = { TEXT("o...."), TEXT("oGo.."), TEXT("oLGo."), TEXT("oLGGo"), TEXT("oDGo."), TEXT("oDo.."), TEXT("o....") };
 291 	const float Bob = FMath::RoundToFloat(FMath::Sin((float)GetRealTime() * 7.f) * 0.8f) * P;
 292 	const float X0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(X + Bob);
 293 	const float Y0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(CY - 3.5f * P);
 294 	for (int32 Row = 0; Row < 7; ++Row)
 295 	{
 296 		for (int32 Col = 0; Col < 5; ++Col)
 297 		{
 298 			const TCHAR C = Rows[Row][Col];
 299 			const FLinearColor* Pick = C == TEXT('o') ? &GBRpg::Outline : C == TEXT('G') ? &GBRpg::Gold3 : C == TEXT('L') ? &GBRpg::Gold4 : C == TEXT('D') ? &GBRpg::Gold1 : nullptr;
 300 			if (Pick)
 301 			{
 302 				FillRect(X0 + Col * P, Y0 + Row * P, P, P, *Pick);
 303 			}
 304 		}
 305 	}
 306 }
 307 
 308 // ======================================================================== status bar
 309 
 310 void AGBHUD::DrawRpgHud(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
 311 {
 312 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 313 	const float W = Canvas->ClipX;
 314 	const float H = Canvas->ClipY;
 315 	const float BarH = 30 * P;
 316 	const float Top = H - BarH;
 317 	const float CX = FMath::RoundToFloat(W * 0.5f / P) * P;
 318 
 319 	UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player->GetEquipment();
 320 	AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment ? Equipment->GetActiveItem() : nullptr;
 321 	const bool bStaff = Item && Item->GetSlot() == EGBHeldSlot::Staff;
 322 
 323 	// Intro after a weapon switch: the bar rises from below.
 324 	const float Intro = FMath::Clamp((float)(GetRealTime() - SkinChangeTime) / 0.22f, 0.f, 1.f);
 325 	const float Drop = FMath::RoundToFloat((1.f - FMath::InterpEaseOut(0.f, 1.f, Intro, 3.f)) * (BarH + 12 * P) / P) * P;
 326 	const float T = Top + Drop;
 327 
 328 	// ---- bar body
 329 	FillRect(0.f, T - 2 * P, W, 2 * P, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.35f));
 330 	RpgStone(0.f, T, W, BarH + 2 * P);
 331 	FillRect(0.f, T, W, P, GBRpg::Outline);
 332 	FillRect(0.f, T + P, W, P, GBRpg::Iron4);
 333 	FillRect(0.f, T + 2 * P, W, P, GBRpg::Iron2);
 334 	FillRect(0.f, T + 3 * P, W, P, GBRpg::Outline);
 335 	FillRect(0.f, T + 4 * P, W, P, GBRpg::Gold1);
 336 	FillRect(0.f, T + 5 * P, W, P, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Outline, 0.55f));
 337 	for (float RX = 6 * P; RX < W - 4 * P; RX += 40 * P)
 338 	{
 339 		RpgRivet(RX, T);
 340 	}
 341 
 342 	// ---- center crest (spell sockets / held weapon)
 343 	const float Socket = 22 * P;
 344 	const float SocketGap = 3 * P;
 345 	const int32 NumSlots = UGBSpellbookComponent::NumSlots;
 346 	const float CrestW = NumSlots * Socket + (NumSlots - 1) * SocketGap + 16 * P;
 347 	const float CrestX = CX - FMath::RoundToFloat(CrestW * 0.5f / P) * P;
 348 	const float CrestTop = T - 10 * P;
 349 	// Stepped shoulders, then the plate.
 350 	for (int32 Step = 0; Step < 4; ++Step)
 351 	{
 352 		const float SY = CrestTop + (Step + 1) * 2 * P;
 353 		const float SW = (4 - Step) * 2 * P;
 354 		RpgStone(CrestX - SW, SY, SW, T - SY + P);
 355 		RpgStone(CrestX + CrestW, SY, SW, T - SY + P);
 356 		FillRect(CrestX - SW, SY, 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Outline);
 357 		FillRect(CrestX + CrestW + SW - 2 * P, SY, 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Outline);
 358 		FillRect(CrestX - SW, SY + P, 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron3);
 359 		FillRect(CrestX + CrestW + SW - 2 * P, SY + P, 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron3);
 360 	}
 361 	RpgStone(CrestX, CrestTop, CrestW, H - CrestTop);
 362 	FillRect(CrestX, CrestTop, CrestW, P, GBRpg::Outline);
 363 	FillRect(CrestX, CrestTop + P, CrestW, P, GBRpg::Iron4);
 364 	FillRect(CrestX, CrestTop + 2 * P, CrestW, P, GBRpg::Iron2);
 365 	FillRect(CrestX, CrestTop + 3 * P, CrestW, P, GBRpg::Outline);
 366 	FillRect(CrestX + 2 * P, CrestTop + 4 * P, CrestW - 4 * P, P, GBRpg::Gold1);
 367 	RpgGem(CX, CrestTop + 1.5f * P, GBRpg::Arcane, true);
 368 
 369 	if (bStaff)
 370 	{
 371 		DrawRpgSpellSockets(Player, CX, CrestTop + 7 * P);
 372 	}
 373 	else if (Item)
 374 	{
 375 		// Sword (or any non-staff RPG item): its name on the crest and its element as a lit gem.
 376 		const EGBElement Element = Item->GetElement();
 377 		FPixelTextStyle Name;
 378 		Name.Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P));
 379 		Name.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold;
 380 		Name.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
 381 		Name.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.7f);
 382 		DrawPixelText(Item->GetDisplayName().ToUpper(), CX, CrestTop + 11 * P, Name, 0.5f);
 383 		const FString ElementText = GBHudText::ElementName(Element);
 384 		const int32 Small = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
 385 		const FVector2D ES = MeasurePixelText(ElementText, Small);
 386 		RpgGem(CX - ES.X * 0.5f - 6 * P, CrestTop + 24 * P, GBHudText::ElementColor(Element), true);
 387 		FPixelTextStyle ElementStyle;
 388 		ElementStyle.Scale = Small;
 389 		ElementStyle.Color = GBHudText::ElementColor(Element);
 390 		ElementStyle.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
 391 		DrawPixelText(ElementText, CX, CrestTop + 22 * P, ElementStyle, 0.5f);
 392 	}
 393 
 394 	// ---- left: life, mana, dash
 395 	DrawRpgVitals(Player, 6 * P, T + 7 * P, FMath::Min(150 * P, CrestX - 26 * P));
 396 
 397 	// ---- right: quick-use socket, skill points
 398 	const float QS = 20 * P;
 399 	const float QX = W - 8 * P - QS;
 400 	const float QY = T + 7 * P;
 401 	RpgWell(QX, QY, QS, QS);
 402 	RpgFrame(QX - P, QY - P, QS + 2 * P, QS + 2 * P, ERpgFrame::Gold);
 403 	FillRect(QX + 2 * P, QY + 2 * P, QS - 4 * P, QS - 4 * P, GBRpg::StoneDeep);
 404 	FString QuickName;
 405 	int32 QuickCount = 0;
 406 	if (const UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player->GetInventory())
 407 	{
 408 		const FName QuickId = Inventory->GetQuickUseItem();
 409 		if (QuickId != NAME_None)
 410 		{
 411 			const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 412 			const FGBItemDefinition* ItemDef = Data ? Data->FindItem(QuickId) : nullptr;
 413 			QuickName = (ItemDef && !ItemDef->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? ItemDef->DisplayName : QuickId.ToString();
 414 			QuickCount = Inventory->GetCount(QuickId);
 415 			const FString* Sprite = ItemDef ? ItemDef->Strings.Find(GBString::Sprite) : nullptr;
 416 			DrawSpriteFit(Sprite ? FName(**Sprite) : NAME_None, QX + 3 * P, QY + 3 * P, QS - 6 * P, QS - 6 * P);
 417 		}
 418 	}
 419 	float KeyW = 0.f;
 420 	RpgKeyCap(QX - 4 * P, QY - 5 * P, TEXT("F"), &KeyW);
 421 	const int32 Small = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
 422 	if (QuickCount > 0)
 423 	{
 424 		FPixelTextStyle Count;
 425 		Count.Scale = Small;
 426 		Count.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBone;
 427 		Count.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
 428 		DrawPixelText(FString::Printf(TEXT("x%d"), QuickCount), QX + QS - P, QY + QS - 4 * P, Count, 1.f);
 429 	}
 430 	const float InfoRight = QX - 8 * P;
 431 	FPixelTextStyle Info;
 432 	Info.Scale = Small;
 433 	Info.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
 434 	Info.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBone;
 435 	DrawPixelText(QuickName.IsEmpty() ? FString(TEXT("Nothing to use")) : QuickName, InfoRight, QY + P, Info, 1.f);
 436 	if (const UGBSkillTreeComponent* SkillTree = Player->GetSkillTree())
 437 	{
 438 		if (SkillTree->GetPoints() > 0)
 439 		{
 440 			const FString Text = FString::Printf(TEXT("%d skill point%s"), SkillTree->GetPoints(), SkillTree->GetPoints() == 1 ? TEXT("") : TEXT("s"));
 441 			float KW = 0.f;
 442 			RpgKeyCap(InfoRight - 9 * P, QY + 10 * P, TEXT("K"), &KW);
 443 			FPixelTextStyle Points = Info;
 444 			Points.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold;
 445 			const FVector2D PS = DrawPixelText(Text, InfoRight - 12 * P, QY + 12 * P, Points, 1.f);
 446 			const float Pulse = 0.6f + 0.4f * FMath::Sin((float)GetRealTime() * 4.f);
 447 			RpgGem(InfoRight - 16 * P - PS.X, QY + 13.5f * P, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Gold3, 1.f) * FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, Pulse), true);
 448 		}
 449 	}
 450 
 451 	// ---- above the bar: active effects, summoned shield
 452 	DrawRpgEffects(Player, 6 * P, T - 4 * P);
 453 }
 454 
 455 void AGBHUD::DrawRpgVitals(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, float X, float Y, float W)
 456 {
 457 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 458 	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Player->GetAttributes();
 459 	if (!Attributes)
 460 	{
 461 		return;
 462 	}
 463 	const int32 NumScale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P));
 464 	const FVector2D NumSize = MeasurePixelText(TEXT("000"), NumScale);
 465 	const float LabelW = 12 * P;
 466 	const float TubeX = X + LabelW;
 467 	const float TubeW = FMath::RoundToFloat((W - LabelW - NumSize.X - 6 * P) / P) * P;
 468 	const float TubeH = 8 * P;
 469 
 470 	RpgWell(X - 2 * P, Y - 3 * P, W + 4 * P, 27 * P);
 471 
 472 	auto Row = [&](float RY, float Frac, float Trail, const FLinearColor Ramp[4], const FGBPixelInk& Ink, int32 Value, FName Icon)
 473 	{
 474 		// Small emblem at the left (heart / mana flask), a tube, then the number.
 475 		if (Icon == TEXT("heart"))
 476 		{
 477 			static const TCHAR* Heart[] = { TEXT(".oo.oo."), TEXT("oLRoRRo"), TEXT("oRRRRDo"), TEXT("oRRRRDo"), TEXT(".oRRDo."), TEXT("..oDo.."), TEXT("...o...") };
 478 			for (int32 R = 0; R < 7; ++R)
 479 			{
 480 				for (int32 C = 0; C < 7; ++C)
 481 				{
 482 					const TCHAR Ch = Heart[R][C];
 483 					const FLinearColor* Pick = Ch == TEXT('o') ? &GBRpg::Outline : Ch == TEXT('L') ? &Ramp[3] : Ch == TEXT('R') ? &Ramp[2] : Ch == TEXT('D') ? &Ramp[1] : nullptr;
 484 					if (Pick)
 485 					{
 486 						FillRect(X + (1 + C) * P, RY + (0.5f + R) * P, P, P, *Pick);
 487 					}
 488 				}
 489 			}
 490 		}
 491 		else
 492 		{
 493 			static const TCHAR* Flask[] = { TEXT("..ooo.."), TEXT("..oLo.."), TEXT(".oLBBo."), TEXT("oLBBBBo"), TEXT("oBBBBDo"), TEXT("oBBBDDo"), TEXT(".ooooo.") };
 494 			for (int32 R = 0; R < 7; ++R)
 495 			{
 496 				for (int32 C = 0; C < 7; ++C)
 497 				{
 498 					const TCHAR Ch = Flask[R][C];
 499 					const FLinearColor* Pick = Ch == TEXT('o') ? &GBRpg::Outline : Ch == TEXT('L') ? &Ramp[3] : Ch == TEXT('B') ? &Ramp[2] : Ch == TEXT('D') ? &Ramp[1] : nullptr;
 500 					if (Pick)
 501 					{
 502 						FillRect(X + (1 + C) * P, RY + (0.5f + R) * P, P, P, *Pick);
 503 					}
 504 				}
 505 			}
 506 		}
 507 		RpgTube(TubeX, RY, TubeW, TubeH, Frac, Ramp, Trail);
 508 		FPixelTextStyle Num;
 509 		Num.Scale = NumScale;
 510 		Num.Ink = &Ink;
 511 		Num.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
 512 		DrawPixelText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), Value), TubeX + TubeW + 4 * P, RY + FMath::RoundToFloat((TubeH - NumSize.Y) * 0.5f / P) * P, Num, 0.f);
 513 	};
 514 
 515 	const float MaxHealth = Attributes->GetMaxHealth();
 516 	const float MaxMana = Attributes->GetMaxMana();
 517 	const float HealthFrac = MaxHealth > 0.f ? Attributes->GetHealth() / MaxHealth : 0.f;
 518 	const float ManaFrac = MaxMana > 0.f ? Attributes->GetMana() / MaxMana : 0.f;
 519 	Row(Y, HealthFrac, HealthTrail01, GBRpg::HealthRamp, HealthFrac <= 0.3f ? GBRpg::InkBlood : GBRpg::InkBone, FMath::CeilToInt(Attributes->GetHealth()), TEXT("heart"));
 520 	Row(Y + 9 * P, ManaFrac, -1.f, GBRpg::ManaRamp, GBRpg::InkMana, FMath::FloorToInt(Attributes->GetMana()), TEXT("mana"));
 521 	if (HealthFrac <= 0.3f)
 522 	{
 523 		// Low life: the heart beats a red glow.
 524 		const float Beat = FMath::Pow(FMath::Abs(FMath::Sin((float)GetRealTime() * 4.f)), 6.f);
 525 		DrawGlow(X + 4.5f * P, Y + 4 * P, 10 * P, 10 * P, FLinearColor(1.f, 0.1f, 0.05f, 0.5f * Beat));
 526 	}
 527 
 528 	// Dash: a thin gilt tube, "DASH" when ready.
 529 	const float DashY = Y + 18 * P;
 530 	const float Remaining = Player->GetDashCooldownRemaining();
 531 	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = Player->GetStats();
 532 	const float Total = Stats ? Stats->GetStat(GBStat::DashCooldown, 0.f) : 0.f;
 533 	const float DashFrac = (Remaining <= 0.f || Total <= 0.f) ? 1.f : 1.f - Remaining / Total;
 534 	FPixelTextStyle Label;
 535 	Label.Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
 536 	Label.Ink = DashFrac >= 1.f ? &GBRpg::InkGold : nullptr;
 537 	Label.Color = DashFrac >= 1.f ? FLinearColor::White : GBRpg::BoneDark;
 538 	Label.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
 539 	// A thin gilt tube under the mana, its label at its right end (bottom-aligned, never below the screen edge).
 540 	const float DashW = FMath::RoundToFloat(TubeW * 0.5f / P) * P;
 541 	RpgTube(TubeX, DashY, DashW, 4 * P, DashFrac, GBRpg::StaminaRamp);
 542 	DrawPixelText(TEXT("DASH"), TubeX + DashW + 3 * P, DashY + 4 * P - 7 * Label.Scale, Label, 0.f);
 543 }
 544 
 545 void AGBHUD::DrawRpgSpellSockets(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, float CX, float Y)
 546 {
 547 	UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook();
 548 	if (!Spellbook)
 549 	{
 550 		return;
 551 	}
 552 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 553 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 554 	const float Socket = 22 * P;
 555 	const float Gap = 3 * P;
 556 	const int32 Num = UGBSpellbookComponent::NumSlots;
 557 	const float X0 = CX - FMath::RoundToFloat((Num * Socket + (Num - 1) * Gap) * 0.5f / P) * P;
 558 	static const TCHAR* Keys[] = { TEXT("RMB"), TEXT("1"), TEXT("2"), TEXT("3"), TEXT("4") };
 559 	const int32 Small = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
 560 
 561 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < Num; ++Slot)
 562 	{
 563 		const float X = X0 + Slot * (Socket + Gap);
 564 		RpgFrame(X, Y, Socket, Socket, ERpgFrame::Gold);
 565 		const float IX = X + 3 * P;
 566 		const float IY = Y + 3 * P;
 567 		const float IS = Socket - 6 * P;
 568 		FillRect(IX, IY, IS, IS, GBRpg::StoneDeep);
 569 		FillRect(IX, IY, IS, P, GBRpg::Outline);
 570 		FillRect(IX, IY, P, IS, GBRpg::Outline);
 571 
 572 		const FName SpellId = Spellbook->GetSlotSpell(Slot);
 573 		const FGBSpellDefinition* Def = (Data && SpellId != NAME_None) ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
 574 		if (Def)
 575 		{
 576 			const FLinearColor ElementColor = GBHudText::ElementColor(Def->Element);
 577 			const EGBCastFailReason Reason = Spellbook->CanCast(SpellId);
 578 			const float Remaining = Spellbook->GetCooldownRemaining(SpellId);
 579 			const bool bReady = Reason == EGBCastFailReason::None;
 580 			if (bReady)
 581 			{
 582 				DrawGlow(IX + IS * 0.5f, IY + IS * 0.5f, IS * 0.55f, IS * 0.55f, GBAlpha(ElementColor, 0.28f));
 583 			}
 584 			if (!DrawSpriteFit(GBHudText::SpellIconId(SpellId), IX + P, IY + P, IS - 2 * P, IS - 2 * P))
 585 			{
 586 				FPixelTextStyle Fallback;
 587 				Fallback.Scale = Small;
 588 				Fallback.Color = ElementColor;
 589 				DrawPixelText(GBHudText::Abbreviate(Def->DisplayName, 5), IX + IS * 0.5f, IY + IS * 0.4f, Fallback, 0.5f);
 590 			}
 591 			if (Remaining > 0.f)
 592 			{
 593 				const float Duration = Spellbook->GetCooldownDuration(SpellId);
 594 				const float Frac = Duration > 0.f ? FMath::Clamp(Remaining / Duration, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
 595 				const float CH = FMath::CeilToFloat(IS * Frac / P) * P;
 596 				FillRect(IX, IY + IS - CH, IS, CH, FLinearColor(0.02f, 0.02f, 0.05f, 0.72f));
 597 				FillRect(IX, IY + IS - CH, IS, P, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Gold3, 0.8f));
 598 				FPixelTextStyle Cool;
 599 				Cool.Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.75f));
 600 				Cool.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBone;
 601 				Cool.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
 602 				DrawPixelText(Remaining >= 10.f ? FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), FMath::CeilToInt(Remaining)) : FString::Printf(TEXT("%.1f"), Remaining),
 603 					IX + IS * 0.5f, IY + IS * 0.5f - 3.5f * Cool.Scale, Cool, 0.5f);
 604 			}
 605 			else if (!bReady && Reason != EGBCastFailReason::OnCooldown)
 606 			{
 607 				// Not enough mana (or not castable now): the socket sinks into a cold blue shade.
 608 				FillRect(IX, IY, IS, IS, FLinearColor(0.02f, 0.04f, 0.15f, 0.62f));
 609 			}
 610 			// Mana cost on a tiny blue tab at the bottom right.
 611 			FPixelTextStyle Cost;
 612 			Cost.Scale = Small;
 613 			Cost.Ink = Reason == EGBCastFailReason::NotEnoughMana ? &GBRpg::InkBlood : &GBRpg::InkMana;
 614 			Cost.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
 615 			DrawPixelText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%d"), FMath::RoundToInt(Spellbook->GetManaCost(SpellId))), X + Socket - 2 * P, Y + Socket - 3 * P - 7 * Small, Cost, 1.f);
 616 		}
 617 		else
 618 		{
 619 			// Empty socket: a faint carved rune.
 620 			const float MX = IX + IS * 0.5f;
 621 			const float MY = IY + IS * 0.5f;
 622 			FillRect(MX - P, MY - 4 * P, 2 * P, 8 * P, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Stone3, 0.6f));
 623 			FillRect(MX - 4 * P, MY - P, 8 * P, 2 * P, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Stone3, 0.6f));
 624 		}
 625 		// Key label under the socket.
 626 		FPixelTextStyle KeyStyle;
 627 		KeyStyle.Scale = Small;
 628 		KeyStyle.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold;
 629 		KeyStyle.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
 630 		DrawPixelText(Keys[Slot], X + Socket * 0.5f, Y + Socket + 2 * P, KeyStyle, 0.5f);
 631 	}
 632 }
 633 
 634 void AGBHUD::DrawRpgEffects(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, float X, float Bottom)
 635 {
 636 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 637 	const int32 Small = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
 638 	float Y = Bottom;
 639 	// Summoned shield first (lowest), then the effects stacked upward.
 640 	if (const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook(); Spellbook && Spellbook->HasShield())
 641 	{
 642 		Y -= 14 * P;
 643 		DrawRpgShield(Player, X, Y);
 644 		Y -= 2 * P;
 645 	}
 646 	const UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player->GetStatusEffects();
 647 	if (!Effects)
 648 	{
 649 		return;
 650 	}
 651 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 652 	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Effect : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
 653 	{
 654 		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindStatusEffect(Effect.EffectId) : nullptr;
 655 		FString Name = (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : Effect.EffectId.ToString();
 656 		if (Effect.Stacks > 1)
 657 		{
 658 			Name += FString::Printf(TEXT(" x%d"), Effect.Stacks);
 659 		}
 660 		const FString Time = Effect.TotalDuration > 0.f ? FString::Printf(TEXT("%.1fs"), Effect.TimeRemaining) : FString();
 661 		const float PW = 70 * P;
 662 		const float PH = 11 * P;
 663 		Y -= PH + P;
 664 		FillRect(X, Y, PW, PH, GBRpg::Outline);
 665 		RpgStone(X + P, Y + P, PW - 2 * P, PH - 2 * P, FLinearColor(0.8f, 0.8f, 0.85f, 1.f));
 666 		FillRect(X + P, Y + P, PW - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron3);
 667 		FillRect(X + P, Y + PH - 2 * P, PW - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron0);
 668 		FPixelTextStyle Text;
 669 		Text.Scale = Small;
 670 		Text.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBone;
 671 		Text.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
 672 		DrawPixelText(GBHudText::Abbreviate(Name, 16), X + 4 * P, Y + 2.f * P, Text, 0.f);
 673 		FPixelTextStyle TimeStyle = Text;
 674 		TimeStyle.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold;
 675 		DrawPixelText(Time, X + PW - 3 * P, Y + 2.f * P, TimeStyle, 1.f);
 676 		if (Effect.TotalDuration > 0.f)
 677 		{
 678 			const float Frac = FMath::Clamp(Effect.TimeRemaining / Effect.TotalDuration, 0.f, 1.f);
 679 			FillRect(X + 2 * P, Y + PH - 3 * P, FMath::RoundToFloat((PW - 4 * P) * Frac / P) * P, P, GBRpg::Ember);
 680 		}
 681 	}
 682 }
 683 
 684 void AGBHUD::DrawRpgShield(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, float X, float Y)
 685 {
 686 	const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook();
 687 	if (!Spellbook || !Spellbook->HasShield())
 688 	{
 689 		return;
 690 	}
 691 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 692 	const int32 Small = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
 693 	const bool bRaised = Spellbook->IsBlockRaised();
 694 	const float PW = 70 * P;
 695 	const float PH = 14 * P;
 696 	FillRect(X, Y, PW, PH, GBRpg::Outline);
 697 	RpgStone(X + P, Y + P, PW - 2 * P, PH - 2 * P, FLinearColor(0.8f, 0.8f, 0.85f, 1.f));
 698 	if (bRaised)
 699 	{
 700 		RpgFrame(X, Y, PW, PH, ERpgFrame::Gold);
 701 	}
 702 	else
 703 	{
 704 		FillRect(X + P, Y + P, PW - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron3);
 705 		FillRect(X + P, Y + PH - 2 * P, PW - 2 * P, P, GBRpg::Iron0);
 706 	}
 707 	FPixelTextStyle Text;
 708 	Text.Scale = Small;
 709 	Text.Ink = bRaised ? &GBRpg::InkIce : &GBRpg::InkBone;
 710 	Text.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
 711 	DrawPixelText(bRaised ? TEXT("BLOCKING") : TEXT("ICE SHIELD"), X + 4 * P, Y + 3 * P, Text, 0.f);
 712 	FPixelTextStyle TimeStyle = Text;
 713 	TimeStyle.Ink = &GBRpg::InkGold;
 714 	DrawPixelText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%.1fs"), FMath::Max(0.f, Spellbook->GetShieldTimeRemaining())), X + PW - 3 * P, Y + 3 * P, TimeStyle, 1.f);
 715 	RpgTube(X + 3 * P, Y + PH - 6 * P, PW - 6 * P, 4 * P, Spellbook->GetShieldHealthFraction(), GBRpg::IceRamp);
 716 	if (!bRaised)
 717 	{
 718 		RpgKeyCap(X + PW + 2 * P, Y + 2 * P, TEXT("Q"));
 719 	}
 720 }

+	void SplitName(const FString& Name, FString& A, FString& B)
+	{
+		int32 Best = INDEX_NONE;
+		for (int32 i = 0; i < Name.Len(); ++i)
+		{
+			if (Name[i] == TEXT(' ') && (Best == INDEX_NONE || FMath::Abs(i - Name.Len() / 2) < FMath::Abs(Best - Name.Len() / 2)))

+			}
+		}
+		if (Best == INDEX_NONE)
+		{
+			A = Name;
+			B.Reset();
+			return;
+		}
+		A = Name.Left(Best);
+		B = Name.Mid(Best + 1);
+	}

+bool AGBHUD::IsMouseOver(float X, float Y, float W, float H) const
+{
+	return bHasMenuMouse && LastMenuMouse.X >= X && LastMenuMouse.X <= X + W && LastMenuMouse.Y >= Y && LastMenuMouse.Y <= Y + H;

+void AGBHUD::MenuBackdrop(EGBHudSkin Skin)

+	DrawGlow(W * 0.5f, H * 0.45f, W * 0.6f, H * 0.65f, FLinearColor(0.25f, 0.12f, 0.05f, 0.18f));

+void AGBHUD::MenuButton(EGBHudSkin Skin, float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FString& Label, bool bEnabled, bool bHot)
+{
+	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk)
+	{
+		const float S = UIScale();
+		const FLinearColor Line = !bEnabled ? GBCy::Grey : (bHot ? GBCy::Cyan : GBCy::Red);
+		CyBox(X, Y, W, H, 8.f * S, bHot && bEnabled ? GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.18f) : GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.7f), Line, 0x4);
+		FCyTextStyle Style;
+		Style.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
+		Style.Px = 17.f;
+		Style.Spacing = 100;
+		Style.bUpper = true;
+		Style.Color = !bEnabled ? GBCy::Grey : (bHot ? GBCy::Pale : GBCy::RedBright);
+		const FVector2D Size = MeasureCyText(Label, Style);
+		DrawCyText(Label, X + W * 0.5f, Y + (H - Size.Y) * 0.5f, Style, 0.5f);
+		return;
+	}
+	const float P = ArtPixel();
+	FillRect(X, Y, W, H, GBRpg::Stone1);
+	RpgStone(X, Y, W, H, bHot && bEnabled ? FLinearColor(1.2f, 1.1f, 1.f, 1.f) : FLinearColor::White);
+	RpgFrame(X, Y, W, H, bEnabled ? (bHot ? ERpgFrame::Gold : ERpgFrame::Iron) : ERpgFrame::Inset);
+	FPixelTextStyle Style;
+	Style.Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
+	Style.Ink = bEnabled ? (bHot ? &GBRpg::InkGold : &GBRpg::InkBone) : nullptr;
+	Style.Color = bEnabled ? FLinearColor::White : GBRpg::BoneDark;
+	Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
+	const FVector2D Size = MeasurePixelText(Label, Style.Scale);
+	DrawPixelText(Label, X + W * 0.5f, Y + FMath::RoundToFloat((H - Size.Y) * 0.5f), Style, 0.5f);
+}
+
+void AGBHUD::MenuHints(EGBHudSkin Skin, const TArray<TPair<FString, FString>>& Hints, float X, float Y, float AlignX)
+{
+	// Measure first, then draw from the left.
+	const bool bCyber = Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk;
+	const float S = UIScale();
+	const float P = ArtPixel();
+	const float KeyGap = bCyber ? 8.f * S : 2.f * P;
+	const float PairGap = bCyber ? 28.f * S : 8.f * P;
+	FCyTextStyle CyStyle;
+	CyStyle.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
+	CyStyle.Px = 16.f;
+	CyStyle.Spacing = 100;
+	CyStyle.bUpper = true;
+	CyStyle.Color = GBCy::RedBright;
+	const int32 Small = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
+	auto KeyWidth = [&](const FString& Key)
+	{
+		if (bCyber)
+		{
+			FCyTextStyle K;
+			K.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
+			K.Px = 22.f * 0.72f;
+			K.bUpper = true;
+			return FMath::Max(FMath::RoundToFloat(22.f * S), FMath::RoundToFloat(MeasureCyText(Key, K).X + 10.f * S));
+		}
+		const FVector2D Size = MeasurePixelText(Key, Small);
+		return FMath::Max(FMath::CeilToFloat((Size.X + 6 * P) / P) * P, 9 * P);
+	};
+	auto TextWidth = [&](const FString& Text) { return bCyber ? MeasureCyText(Text, CyStyle).X : MeasurePixelText(Text, Small).X; };
+	float Total = 0.f;
+	for (int32 i = 0; i < Hints.Num(); ++i)
+	{
+		TArray<FString> Keys;
+		Hints[i].Key.ParseIntoArray(Keys, TEXT(" "));
+		for (const FString& Key : Keys)
+		{
+			Total += KeyWidth(Key) + KeyGap * 0.5f;
+		}
+		Total += KeyGap + TextWidth(Hints[i].Value) + (i + 1 < Hints.Num() ? PairGap : 0.f);
+	}
+	float PX = FMath::RoundToFloat(X - Total * AlignX);
+	for (const TPair<FString, FString>& Hint : Hints)
+	{
+		TArray<FString> Keys;
+		Hint.Key.ParseIntoArray(Keys, TEXT(" "));
+		for (const FString& Key : Keys)
+		{
+			if (bCyber)
+			{
+				PX += CyKey(PX, Y, Key, GBCy::Cyan, 22.f) + KeyGap * 0.5f;
+			}
+			else
+			{
+				float KW = 0.f;
+				RpgKeyCap(PX, Y, Key, &KW);
+				PX += KW + KeyGap * 0.5f;
+			}
+		}
+		PX += KeyGap * 0.5f;
+		if (bCyber)
+		{
+			const FVector2D Size = DrawCyText(Hint.Value, PX, Y + 1.f * S, CyStyle, 0.f);
+			PX += Size.X + PairGap;
+		}
+		else
+		{
+			FPixelTextStyle Style;
+			Style.Scale = Small;
+			Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBone;
+			Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
+			const FVector2D Size = DrawPixelText(Hint.Value, PX, Y + 2.f * P + FMath::RoundToFloat(0.5f * Small), Style, 0.f);
+			PX += Size.X + PairGap;
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+float AGBHUD::MenuDetailPanel(EGBHudSkin Skin, float X, float Y, float W, float H, const FString& Title, const FString& Subtitle, const FLinearColor* SubtitleColor)
+{
+	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk)
+	{
+		const float S = UIScale();
+		CyBox(X, Y, W, H, 18.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedBlack, 0.55f), GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDim, 0.9f), 0x5);
+		CyBrackets(X - 5.f * S, Y - 5.f * S, W + 10.f * S, H + 10.f * S, 14.f * S, GBCy::Red, 2.f * S);
+		float CY = Y + 22.f * S;
+		const FVector2D TS = UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Heading, EGBTextTone::Accent, Title, X + 24.f * S, CY);
+		CY += TS.Y + 10.f * S;
+		CyRuler(X + 24.f * S, CY, W - 48.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDim, 0.8f));
+		CY += 14.f * S;
+		if (!Subtitle.IsEmpty())
+		{
+			FCyTextStyle Sub;
+			Sub.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
+			Sub.Px = 18.f;
+			Sub.Spacing = 120;
+			Sub.bUpper = true;
+			Sub.Color = SubtitleColor ? *SubtitleColor : GBCy::Cyan;
+			CY += DrawCyText(Subtitle, X + 24.f * S, CY, Sub, 0.f).Y + 12.f * S;
+		}
+		return CY;
+	}
+	const float P = ArtPixel();
+	RpgWell(X, Y, W, H);
+	float CY = Y + 6.f * P;
+	const FVector2D TS = UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Heading, EGBTextTone::Accent, Title, X + 7.f * P, CY);
+	CY += TS.Y + 4.f * P;
+	RpgDivider(X + 6.f * P, CY, W - 12.f * P);
+	CY += 6.f * P;
+	if (!Subtitle.IsEmpty())
+	{
+		CY += UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Muted, Subtitle, X + 7.f * P, CY, 0.f, SubtitleColor).Y + 5.f * P;
+	}
+	return CY;
+}
+
+// ======================================================================== drawing: screens
+
+namespace GBHudMenuDraw
+{
+	FScreen Layout(EGBHudSkin Skin, float W, float H, float S, float P)
+	{
+		FScreen Screen;
+		if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk)
+		{
+			const float M = 64.f * S;
+			Screen.X = M;
+			Screen.Y = 158.f * S;
+			Screen.W = W - 2.f * M;
+			Screen.FooterY = H - 62.f * S;
+			Screen.H = Screen.FooterY - 22.f * S - Screen.Y;
+			return Screen;
+		}
+		const float WinX = FMath::RoundToFloat(W * 0.05f / P) * P;
+		const float WinY = FMath::RoundToFloat(H * 0.085f / P) * P;
+		const float WinW = FMath::RoundToFloat((W - 2.f * WinX) / P) * P;
+		const float WinH = FMath::RoundToFloat((H - WinY - 4.f * P) / P) * P;
+		Screen.X = WinX;
+		Screen.Y = WinY;
+		Screen.W = WinW;
+		Screen.H = WinH;
+		Screen.FooterY = WinY + WinH - 15.f * P;
+		return Screen;
 	}
-	return CurY - Y;
 }

-void AGBHUD::DrawSkillTrees(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, float Top)
+// ======================================================================== drawing: skill trees
+
+void AGBHUD::DrawSkillNode(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, int32 Tree, FName NodeId, float X, float Y, float W, float H)
+{
+	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
+	const UGBSkillTreeComponent* SkillTree = Player->GetSkillTree();
+	const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr;
+	const int32 Rank = SkillTree ? SkillTree->GetRank(NodeId) : 0;
+	const int32 MaxRank = Def ? FMath::Max(1, Def->MaxRank) : 1;
+	const bool bCan = SkillTree && SkillTree->CanUnlock(NodeId) == EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok;
+	const bool bSelected = NodeId == MenuState.SkillCursor;
+	const bool bMax = Rank >= MaxRank;
+	const FString Name = GBHudMenuLocal::SkillName(Data, NodeId);
+	FString LineA;
+	FString LineB;
+	const float S = UIScale();
+	const float P = ArtPixel();
+
+	if (Tree == 1)
+	{
+		// Cyberpunk node: cut-corner box, condensed caps, rank segments, cost tag.
+		const FLinearColor Line = bSelected ? GBCy::Cyan : (bMax ? GBCy::Red : (Rank > 0 ? GBCy::Red : (bCan ? GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.75f) : GBAlpha(GBCy::Grey, 0.6f))));
+		const FLinearColor Fill = bMax ? GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.28f) : (Rank > 0 ? GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDeep, 0.85f) : GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.85f));
+		if (bSelected)
+		{
+			DrawGlow(X + W * 0.5f, Y + H * 0.5f, W * 0.75f, H, GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.16f));
+		}
+		CyBox(X, Y, W, H, 10.f * S, Fill, Line, 0x5, bSelected ? 2.f : 1.f);
+		if (bSelected)
+		{
+			CyBrackets(X - 5.f * S, Y - 5.f * S, W + 10.f * S, H + 10.f * S, 9.f * S, GBCy::Cyan, 2.f * S);
+		}
+		FCyTextStyle NameStyle;
+		NameStyle.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
+		NameStyle.Px = 15.f;
+		NameStyle.Spacing = 60;
+		NameStyle.bUpper = true;
+		NameStyle.Color = bSelected ? GBCy::Pale : (Rank > 0 || bCan ? GBCy::RedBright : GBCy::Grey);
+		const float TextW = W - 16.f * S;
+		if (MeasureCyText(Name, NameStyle).X > TextW)
+		{
+			GBHudMenuDraw::SplitName(Name, LineA, LineB);
+		}
+		else
+		{
+			LineA = Name;
+		}
+		const float LH = MeasureCyText(TEXT("A"), NameStyle).Y;
+		const float TextY = Y + (LineB.IsEmpty() ? H * 0.5f - LH * 0.85f : H * 0.5f - LH * 1.3f);
+		DrawCyText(LineA, X + W * 0.5f, TextY, NameStyle, 0.5f);
+		if (!LineB.IsEmpty())
+		{
+			DrawCyText(LineB, X + W * 0.5f, TextY + LH * 0.92f, NameStyle, 0.5f);
+		}
+		// Rank segments.
+		const float SegW = 14.f * S;
+		const float SegGap = 4.f * S;
+		const float SegsW = MaxRank * SegW + (MaxRank - 1) * SegGap;
+		const float SegY = Y + H - 12.f * S;
+		for (int32 i = 0; i < MaxRank; ++i)
+		{
+			FillRect(X + (W - SegsW) * 0.5f + i * (SegW + SegGap), SegY, SegW, 3.f * S, i < Rank ? GBCy::Red : GBAlpha(GBCy::Grey, 0.35f));
+		}
+		if (Def && Def->Cost > 1)
+		{
+			FCyTextStyle Cost;
+			Cost.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
+			Cost.Px = 11.f;
+			Cost.Color = GBCy::Yellow;
+			DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%dPT"), Def->Cost), X + W - 8.f * S, Y + 3.f * S, Cost, 1.f);
+		}
+		return;
+	}
+
+	// RPG node: a stone tablet in a gold (or iron) frame, pixel name, gems for ranks.
+	const float NX = FMath::RoundToFloat(X / P) * P;
+	const float NY = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y / P) * P;
+	const float NW = FMath::RoundToFloat(W / P) * P;
+	const float NH = FMath::RoundToFloat(H / P) * P;
+	if (bSelected)
+	{
+		DrawGlow(NX + NW * 0.5f, NY + NH * 0.5f, NW * 0.8f, NH * 1.1f, GBAlpha(GBRpg::EmberLight, 0.30f));
+	}
+	FillRect(NX, NY, NW, NH, GBRpg::Stone1);
+	RpgStone(NX, NY, NW, NH, bMax ? FLinearColor(1.25f, 1.1f, 0.9f, 1.f) : (Rank > 0 || bCan ? FLinearColor::White : FLinearColor(0.6f, 0.6f, 0.65f, 1.f)));
+	if (Rank > 0)
+	{
+		FillRect(NX, NY, NW, NH, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Gold1, bMax ? 0.35f : 0.18f));
+	}
+	RpgFrame(NX, NY, NW, NH, (Rank > 0 || bCan || bSelected) ? ERpgFrame::Gold : ERpgFrame::Inset);
+	if (bSelected)
+	{
+		DrawOutline(NX - P, NY - P, NW + 2.f * P, NH + 2.f * P, GBRpg::Gold4, P);
+	}
+	const int32 Small = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
+	FPixelTextStyle NameStyle;
+	NameStyle.Scale = Small;
+	NameStyle.Ink = bSelected || Rank > 0 ? &GBRpg::InkGold : (bCan ? &GBRpg::InkBone : nullptr);
+	NameStyle.Color = (bSelected || Rank > 0 || bCan) ? FLinearColor::White : GBRpg::BoneDark;
+	NameStyle.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
+	// Text area: between the top frame (4 art pixels) and the pip row (6 from the bottom).
+	const float LineH = 8.f * Small;
+	const float AreaTop = NY + 4.f * P;
+	const float AreaH = NH - 10.f * P;
+	const float TextMaxW = NW - 6.f * P;
+	if (MeasurePixelText(Name, Small).X <= TextMaxW)
+	{
+		LineA = Name;
+	}
+	else if (AreaH >= LineH + 7.f * Small)
+	{
+		GBHudMenuDraw::SplitName(Name, LineA, LineB);
+	}
+	else
+	{
+		// Too short for two lines (small screens): one abbreviated line; the detail panel shows the full name.
+		const int32 MaxChars = FMath::Max(4, FMath::FloorToInt(TextMaxW / (6.f * Small)));
+		LineA = GBHudText::Abbreviate(Name, MaxChars);
+	}
+	const float BlockH = LineB.IsEmpty() ? 7.f * Small : LineH + 7.f * Small;
+	const float TextY = FMath::RoundToFloat((AreaTop + (AreaH - BlockH) * 0.5f) / Small) * Small;
+	DrawPixelText(LineA, NX + NW * 0.5f, TextY, NameStyle, 0.5f);
+	if (!LineB.IsEmpty())
+	{
+		DrawPixelText(LineB, NX + NW * 0.5f, TextY + LineH, NameStyle, 0.5f);
+	}
+	// Rank pips: tiny gems along the bottom.
+	const float PipW = 4.f * P;
+	const float PipGap = 2.f * P;
+	const float PipsW = MaxRank * PipW + (MaxRank - 1) * PipGap;
+	const float PipY = NY + NH - 5.f * P;
+	for (int32 i = 0; i < MaxRank; ++i)
+	{
+		const float PX = FMath::RoundToFloat((NX + (NW - PipsW) * 0.5f) / P) * P + i * (PipW + PipGap);
+		FillRect(PX, PipY, PipW, 2.f * P, GBRpg::Outline);
+		FillRect(PX + P, PipY, PipW - 2.f * P, P, i < Rank ? GBRpg::Gold4 : GBRpg::Iron1);
+	}
+	if (Def && Def->Cost > 1)
+	{
+		FPixelTextStyle Cost;
+		Cost.Scale = Small;
+		Cost.Ink = &GBRpg::InkEmber;
+		Cost.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
+		// A small cost badge hanging on the bottom-right corner of the tablet (only for nodes that cost more than 1).
+		const FString CostText = FString::Printf(TEXT("%dpt"), Def->Cost);
+		const FVector2D CS = MeasurePixelText(CostText, Small);
+		const float BW = FMath::CeilToFloat((CS.X + 4.f * P) / P) * P;
+		const float BH = FMath::CeilToFloat((CS.Y + 4.f * P) / P) * P;
+		const float BX = NX + NW - BW + 2.f * P;
+		const float BY = NY + NH - FMath::RoundToFloat(BH * 0.6f / P) * P;
+		FillRect(BX, BY, BW, BH, GBRpg::Outline);
+		FillRect(BX + P, BY + P, BW - 2.f * P, BH - 2.f * P, GBRpg::Gold0);
+		FillRect(BX + P, BY + P, BW - 2.f * P, P, GBRpg::Gold1);
+		DrawPixelText(CostText, BX + BW * 0.5f, BY + 2.f * P, Cost, 0.5f);
+	}
+}

+void AGBHUD::UpdateTrails(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+{
+	const double Now = GetRealTime();
+	const float Dt = LastTrailUpdateTime < 0.0 ? 0.f : FMath::Clamp((float)(Now - LastTrailUpdateTime), 0.f, 0.1f);
+	LastTrailUpdateTime = Now;
+	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Player ? Player->GetAttributes() : nullptr;
+	if (!Attributes || Attributes->GetMaxHealth() <= 0.f)
+	{
+		return;
+	}
+	HealthShown01 = FMath::Clamp(Attributes->GetHealth() / Attributes->GetMaxHealth(), 0.f, 1.f);
+	if (HealthShown01 >= HealthTrail01)
+	{
+		HealthTrail01 = HealthShown01;
+	}
+	else if (Now - LastDamageTime > GBHudLayout::TrailHoldTime)
+	{
+		HealthTrail01 = FMath::Max(HealthShown01, HealthTrail01 - GBHudLayout::TrailDrainPerSecond * Dt);
+	}
+}

 743 void AGBHUD::DrawInteractPrompt(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, EGBHudSkin Skin)
 744 {
 745 	IGBInteractable* Interactable = Player->GetFocusedInteractable();
 746 	if (!Interactable)
 747 	{
 748 		return;
 749 	}
 750 	const FString Text = Interactable->GetInteractPrompt(Player);
 751 	const float CX = Canvas->ClipX * 0.5f;
 752 	const float Y = FMath::RoundToFloat(Canvas->ClipY * 0.6f);
 753 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk)
 754 	{
 755 		const float S = UIScale();
 756 		FCyTextStyle Style;
 757 		Style.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 758 		Style.Px = 22.f;
 759 		Style.Spacing = 100;
 760 		Style.bUpper = true;
 761 		Style.Color = GBCy::Pale;
 762 		const FVector2D Size = MeasureCyText(Text, Style);
 763 		const float KeyW = 30.f * S;
 764 		const float Total = KeyW + 12.f * S + Size.X;
 765 		const float X = CX - Total * 0.5f;
 766 		DrawFade(X - 40.f * S, Y - 8.f * S, Total + 80.f * S, 46.f * S, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.45f), false);
 767 		CyKey(X, Y, TEXT("G"), GBCy::Cyan, 30.f);
 768 		DrawCyText(Text, X + KeyW + 12.f * S, Y + 1.f * S, Style, 0.f);
 769 		FillRect(X + KeyW + 12.f * S, Y + 32.f * S, Size.X, 1.f, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.7f));
 770 		return;
 771 	}
 772 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 773 	const int32 Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.75f));
 774 	const FVector2D Size = MeasurePixelText(Text, Scale);
 775 	float KeyW = 0.f;
 776 	const float PW = FMath::CeilToFloat((Size.X + 22.f * P) / P) * P;
 777 	const float PH = 14.f * P;
 778 	const float X = FMath::RoundToFloat((CX - PW * 0.5f) / P) * P;
 779 	FillRect(X, Y, PW, PH, GBAlpha(GBRpg::StoneDeep, 0.9f));
 780 	RpgFrame(X, Y, PW, PH, ERpgFrame::Bone);
 781 	RpgKeyCap(X + 3.f * P, Y + 2.f * P, TEXT("G"), &KeyW);
 782 	FPixelTextStyle Style;
 783 	Style.Scale = Scale;
 784 	Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBone;
 785 	Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
 786 	DrawPixelText(Text, X + 3.f * P + KeyW + 4.f * P, Y + FMath::RoundToFloat((PH - Size.Y) * 0.5f), Style, 0.f);
