 789 void AGBHUD::DrawToasts(EGBHudSkin Skin, float TopY)
 790 {
 791 	const double Now = GetRealTime();
 792 	const float CX = Canvas->ClipX * 0.5f;
 793 	float Y = FMath::RoundToFloat(TopY >= 0.f ? TopY : Canvas->ClipY * 0.13f);
 794 	for (const FToast& Toast : Toasts)
 795 	{
 796 		const float Alpha = FMath::Clamp((float)(Toast.EndTime - Now) / GBHudLayout::ToastFadeTime, 0.f, 1.f);
 797 		const float In = FMath::Clamp((float)(Now - Toast.StartTime) / GBHudLayout::ToastInTime, 0.f, 1.f);
 798 		if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk)
 799 		{
 800 			const float S = UIScale();
 801 			FCyTextStyle Style;
 802 			Style.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 803 			Style.Px = 21.f;
 804 			Style.Spacing = 90;
 805 			Style.bUpper = true;
 806 			Style.Color = GBAlpha(GBCy::Pale, Alpha);
 807 			const FVector2D Size = MeasureCyText(Toast.Text, Style);
 808 			const float W = Size.X + 44.f * S;
 809 			const float H = 38.f * S;
 810 			const float X = CX - W * 0.5f;
 811 			CyBox(X, Y, W, H, 10.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.8f * Alpha), GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDim, Alpha), 0x2);
 812 			FillRect(X, Y, 4.f * S, H, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, Alpha));
 813 			// Typed on: the letters appear left to right while the box opens.
 814 			const int32 Shown = FMath::Clamp(FMath::CeilToInt(Toast.Text.Len() * FMath::Clamp((float)(Now - Toast.StartTime) / 0.3f, 0.f, 1.f)), 0, Toast.Text.Len());
 815 			Style.Split = (1.f - In) * 4.f * S;
 816 			DrawCyText(Toast.Text.Left(Shown), X + 24.f * S, Y + (H - Size.Y) * 0.5f, Style, 0.f);
 817 			Y += H + 8.f * S;
 818 			continue;
 819 		}
 820 		const float P = ArtPixel();
 821 		const int32 Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.75f));
 822 		const FVector2D Size = MeasurePixelText(Toast.Text, Scale);
 823 		const float W = FMath::CeilToFloat((Size.X + 16.f * P) / P) * P;
 824 		const float H = 13.f * P;
 825 		const float X = FMath::RoundToFloat((CX - W * 0.5f) / P) * P;
 826 		const float Slide = FMath::RoundToFloat((1.f - In) * 6.f) * P;
 827 		const float TY = Y - Slide;
 828 		FillRect(X, TY, W, H, GBAlpha(GBRpg::StoneDeep, 0.92f * Alpha));
 829 		RpgStone(X + P, TY + P, W - 2.f * P, H - 2.f * P, FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, Alpha));
 830 		if (Alpha >= 1.f)
 831 		{
 832 			RpgFrame(X, TY, W, H, ERpgFrame::Gold);
 833 		}
 834 		else
 835 		{
 836 			DrawOutline(X, TY, W, H, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Gold2, Alpha), P);
 837 		}
 838 		FPixelTextStyle Style;
 839 		Style.Scale = Scale;
 840 		Style.Ink = &GBRpg::InkBone;
 841 		Style.Color = FLinearColor(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, Alpha);
 842 		Style.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
 843 		DrawPixelText(Toast.Text, CX, TY + FMath::RoundToFloat((H - Size.Y) * 0.5f), Style, 0.5f);
 844 		Y += H + 3.f * P;
 845 	}
 846 }
 847 
 848 // ======================================================================== QTE (GDD 7)
 849 
 850 void AGBHUD::DrawQTE(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, EGBHudSkin Skin)
 851 {
 852 	const UGBQTEComponent* QTE = Player->GetQTE();
 853 	const FGBQTESession& Session = QTE->GetSession();
 854 	const float S = UIScale();
 855 	const float W = Canvas->ClipX;
 856 	const float H = Canvas->ClipY;
 857 	const float CX = W * 0.5f;
 858 	const bool bCyber = Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk;
 859 	const float P = ArtPixel();
 860 
 861 	// Backdrop.
 862 	FillRect(0.f, 0.f, W, H, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, bCyber ? 0.5f : 0.38f));
 863 	if (bCyber)
 864 	{
 865 		DrawGlow(CX, H * 0.4f, W * 0.42f, H * 0.5f, FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.55f));
 866 		CyScanlines(0.f, 0.f, W, H, 0.12f);
 867 		CyBrackets(W * 0.22f, H * 0.06f, W * 0.56f, H * 0.74f, 26.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.7f), 2.f * S);
 868 	}
 869 	else
 870 	{
 871 		DrawGlow(CX, H * 0.48f, W * 0.42f, H * 0.5f, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Arcane, 0.18f));
 872 	}
 873 
 874 	// Title and time.
 875 	const FString Title = GBHudText::SpellName(this, QTE->GetSpellId());
 876 	const float Limit = Session.Config.TimeLimit;
 877 	const float TimeFrac = Limit > 0.f ? FMath::Clamp(Session.GetTimeRemaining() / Limit, 0.f, 1.f) : 0.f;
 878 	const float BarW = FMath::RoundToFloat(560.f * S);
 879 	float TimeY = 0.f;
 880 	if (bCyber)
 881 	{
 882 		FCyTextStyle Sub;
 883 		Sub.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
 884 		Sub.Px = 16.f;
 885 		Sub.Color = GBCy::RedBright;
 886 		DrawCyText(TEXT("// SYNC SEQUENCE"), CX, H * 0.08f, Sub, 0.5f);
 887 		FCyTextStyle Big;
 888 		Big.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 889 		Big.Px = 50.f;
 890 		Big.Spacing = 120;
 891 		Big.bUpper = true;
 892 		Big.Color = GBCy::Red;
 893 		Big.Split = 2.f * S;
 894 		DrawCyText(Title, CX, H * 0.105f, Big, 0.5f);
 895 		TimeY = H * 0.19f;
 896 		CySlantBar(CX - BarW * 0.5f, TimeY, BarW, 10.f * S, 5.f * S, TimeFrac, 30, TimeFrac > 0.3f ? GBCy::Cyan : GBCy::Yellow, GBAlpha(GBCy::CyanDeep, 0.8f));
 897 		FCyTextStyle T;
 898 		T.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
 899 		T.Px = 14.f;
 900 		T.Color = TimeFrac > 0.3f ? GBCy::Cyan : GBCy::Yellow;
 901 		DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%05.2f"), Session.GetTimeRemaining()), CX + BarW * 0.5f + 12.f * S, TimeY - 4.f * S, T, 0.f);
 902 	}
 903 	else
 904 	{
 905 		const int32 TitleScale = FMath::Max(3, FMath::RoundToInt(5.f * S));
 906 		const FBox2D Plaque = RpgPlaque(Title.ToUpper(), CX, FMath::RoundToFloat(H * 0.08f), TitleScale);
 907 		TimeY = FMath::RoundToFloat(Plaque.Max.Y + 5.f * P);
 908 		RpgTube(FMath::RoundToFloat((CX - BarW * 0.5f) / P) * P, TimeY, FMath::RoundToFloat(BarW / P) * P, 6.f * P, TimeFrac, TimeFrac > 0.3f ? GBRpg::StaminaRamp : GBRpg::HealthRamp);
 909 	}
 910 
 911 	// Part 1: the key sequence (power).
 912 	const int32 NumKeys = Session.Sequence.Num();
 913 	const int32 Done = Session.SequenceResults.Num();
 914 	const float Box = bCyber ? FMath::RoundToFloat(60.f * S) : 18.f * P;
 915 	const float Gap = bCyber ? FMath::RoundToFloat(14.f * S) : 4.f * P;
 916 	const float RowY = FMath::RoundToFloat(H * 0.255f);
 917 	const float X0 = FMath::RoundToFloat(CX - (NumKeys * Box + FMath::Max(0, NumKeys - 1) * Gap) * 0.5f);
 918 	int32 RightKeys = 0;
 919 	for (int32 i = 0; i < NumKeys; ++i)
 920 	{
 921 		const bool bConsumed = i < Done;
 922 		const bool bCurrent = Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Sequence && i == Done;
 923 		const bool bRight = bConsumed && Session.SequenceResults[i];
 924 		if (bRight)
 925 		{
 926 			++RightKeys;
 927 		}
 928 		const float X = X0 + i * (Box + Gap);
 929 		const FString Letter = GBHudLocal::QTEKeyLetter(Session.Sequence[i]);
 930 		if (bCyber)
 931 		{
 932 			const FLinearColor Fill = bConsumed ? (bRight ? GBAlpha(GBCy::Green, 0.25f) : GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.35f)) : GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.75f);
 933 			const FLinearColor Line = bConsumed ? (bRight ? GBCy::Green : GBCy::Red) : (bCurrent ? GBCy::Cyan : GBCy::RedDim);
 934 			CyBox(X, RowY, Box, Box, 10.f * S, Fill, Line, 0x5, bCurrent ? 2.f : 1.f);
 935 			if (bCurrent)
 936 			{
 937 				CyBrackets(X - 6.f * S, RowY - 6.f * S, Box + 12.f * S, Box + 12.f * S, 10.f * S, GBCy::Cyan, 2.f * S);
 938 				DrawGlow(X + Box * 0.5f, RowY + Box * 0.5f, Box, Box, GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.18f));
 939 			}
 940 			FCyTextStyle KeyStyle;
 941 			KeyStyle.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
 942 			KeyStyle.Px = 34.f;
 943 			KeyStyle.Color = bConsumed ? (bRight ? GBCy::Green : GBCy::Red) : (bCurrent ? GBCy::Pale : GBCy::RedBright);
 944 			const FVector2D KS = MeasureCyText(Letter, KeyStyle);
 945 			DrawCyText(Letter, X + Box * 0.5f, RowY + (Box - KS.Y) * 0.5f, KeyStyle, 0.5f);
 946 		}
 947 		else
 948 		{
 949 			if (bCurrent)
 950 			{
 951 				DrawGlow(X + Box * 0.5f, RowY + Box * 0.5f, Box * 1.1f, Box * 1.1f, GBAlpha(GBRpg::EmberLight, 0.35f));
 952 			}
 953 			RpgFrame(X, RowY, Box, Box, bCurrent ? ERpgFrame::Gold : ERpgFrame::Iron);
 954 			const float In = bCurrent ? 3.f * P : 4.f * P;
 955 			RpgStone(X + In, RowY + In, Box - 2.f * In, Box - 2.f * In, bConsumed ? (bRight ? FLinearColor(0.5f, 1.f, 0.5f, 1.f) : FLinearColor(1.f, 0.4f, 0.35f, 1.f)) : FLinearColor::White);
 956 			FPixelTextStyle KeyStyle;
 957 			KeyStyle.Scale = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P));
 958 			KeyStyle.Ink = bConsumed ? (bRight ? &GBRpg::InkIce : &GBRpg::InkBlood) : (bCurrent ? &GBRpg::InkGold : &GBRpg::InkBone);
 959 			KeyStyle.Outline = GBRpg::Outline;
 960 			DrawPixelText(Letter, X + Box * 0.5f, RowY + FMath::RoundToFloat((Box - 7.f * KeyStyle.Scale) * 0.5f), KeyStyle, 0.5f);
 961 		}
 962 	}
 963 	const float BelowKeys = RowY + Box + (bCyber ? 18.f * S : 5.f * P);
 964 	if (Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Sequence)
 965 	{
 966 		UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Normal, TEXT("Press the keys in order"), CX, BelowKeys, 0.5f);
 967 	}
 968 
 969 	// Part 2: the gesture (reach).
 970 	const int32 NumGestures = Session.Gestures.Num();
 971 	const int32 Judged = Session.GestureScores.Num();
 972 	float ScoreSum = 0.f;
 973 	for (const float Score : Session.GestureScores)
 974 	{
 975 		ScoreSum += Score;
 976 	}
 977 	if (Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Gesture && Judged < NumGestures)
 978 	{
 979 		const EGBGesture Gesture = Session.Gestures[Judged];
 980 		FString GestureTitle = GBHudLocal::GestureText(Gesture);
 981 		if (NumGestures > 1)
 982 		{
 983 			GestureTitle += FString::Printf(TEXT(" (%d/%d)"), Judged + 1, NumGestures);
 984 		}
 985 		UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Heading, EGBTextTone::Accent, GestureTitle, CX, H * 0.34f, 0.5f);
 986 		const float CY = H * 0.52f;
 987 		const FLinearColor HintColor = bCyber ? GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.35f) : GBAlpha(GBRpg::Gold3, 0.4f);
 988 		DrawGestureHint((uint8)Gesture, CX, CY, 110.f * S, HintColor, (bCyber ? 2.f : 5.f) * S);
 989 
 990 		// The stroke is in accumulated screen units from (0,0): draw it centered on screen, shrunk if it is too big.
 991 		const TArray<FVector2D>& Stroke = Session.CurrentStroke;
 992 		if (Stroke.Num() >= 2)
 993 		{
 994 			FVector2D Min = Stroke[0];
 995 			FVector2D Max = Stroke[0];
 996 			for (const FVector2D& Point : Stroke)
 997 			{
 998 				Min = FVector2D(FMath::Min(Min.X, Point.X), FMath::Min(Min.Y, Point.Y));
 999 				Max = FVector2D(FMath::Max(Max.X, Point.X), FMath::Max(Max.Y, Point.Y));
1000 			}
1001 			const FVector2D Center = (Min + Max) * 0.5f;
1002 			const float Extent = FMath::Max3((float)(Max.X - Min.X), (float)(Max.Y - Min.Y), 1.f);
1003 			const float Fit = FMath::Min(1.f, 320.f * S / Extent);
1004 			const FLinearColor Glow = bCyber ? GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.3f) : GBAlpha(GBRpg::ArcaneLight, 0.3f);
1005 			const FLinearColor Core = bCyber ? GBCy::Pale : GBRpg::Gold4;
1006 			for (int32 i = 1; i < Stroke.Num(); ++i)
1007 			{
1008 				const FVector2D A = (Stroke[i - 1] - Center) * Fit;
1009 				const FVector2D B = (Stroke[i] - Center) * Fit;
1010 				DrawLine(CX + A.X, CY + A.Y, CX + B.X, CY + B.Y, Glow, 10.f * S);
1011 			}
1012 			for (int32 i = 1; i < Stroke.Num(); ++i)
1013 			{
1014 				const FVector2D A = (Stroke[i - 1] - Center) * Fit;
1015 				const FVector2D B = (Stroke[i] - Center) * Fit;
1016 				DrawLine(CX + A.X, CY + A.Y, CX + B.X, CY + B.Y, Core, 3.f * S);
1017 			}
1018 			const FVector2D Tip = (Stroke.Last() - Center) * Fit;
1019 			DrawGlow(CX + Tip.X, CY + Tip.Y, 22.f * S, 22.f * S, bCyber ? GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.6f) : GBAlpha(GBRpg::EmberLight, 0.6f));
1020 		}
1021 	}
1022 
1023 	// Running scores.
1024 	const float Power = NumKeys > 0 ? (float)RightKeys / NumKeys : 0.f;
1025 	const float Reach = NumGestures > 0 ? ScoreSum / NumGestures : 0.f;
1026 	const float ScoreY = H * 0.72f;
1027 	for (int32 i = 0; i < 2; ++i)
1028 	{
1029 		const float SX = CX + (i == 0 ? -220.f : 220.f) * S;
1030 		const FString Label = i == 0 ? TEXT("POWER") : TEXT("REACH");
1031 		const FString Value = FString::Printf(TEXT("%d%%"), FMath::RoundToInt((i == 0 ? Power : Reach) * 100.f));
1032 		if (bCyber)
1033 		{
1034 			FCyTextStyle L;
1035 			L.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
1036 			L.Px = 16.f;
1037 			L.Color = GBCy::RedBright;
1038 			CyBox(SX - 90.f * S, ScoreY - 10.f * S, 180.f * S, 96.f * S, 12.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.7f), GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDim, 0.9f), 0x5);
1039 			DrawCyText(Label, SX, ScoreY, L, 0.5f);
1040 			FCyTextStyle V;
1041 			V.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
1042 			V.Px = 44.f;
1043 			V.Color = GBCy::Cyan;
1044 			DrawCyText(Value, SX, ScoreY + 18.f * S, V, 0.5f);
1045 			FillRect(SX - 60.f * S, ScoreY + 74.f * S, 120.f * S, 1.f, GBCy::RedDim);
1046 		}
1047 		else
1048 		{
1049 			const float PW = 44.f * P;
1050 			const float PH = 26.f * P;
1051 			const float PX = FMath::RoundToFloat((SX - PW * 0.5f) / P) * P;
1052 			const float PY = FMath::RoundToFloat((ScoreY - 4.f * P) / P) * P;
1053 			FillRect(PX, PY, PW, PH, GBRpg::Stone1);
1054 			RpgStone(PX, PY, PW, PH);
1055 			RpgFrame(PX, PY, PW, PH, ERpgFrame::Gold);
1056 			UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Normal, Label, SX, PY + 5.f * P, 0.5f);
1057 			UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Number, EGBTextTone::Accent, Value, SX, PY + 11.f * P, 0.5f);
1058 		}
1059 	}
1060 }
1061 
1062 void AGBHUD::DrawGestureHint(uint8 GestureByte, float CenterX, float CenterY, float Radius, const FLinearColor& Color, float Thick)
1063 {
1064 	const EGBGesture Gesture = (EGBGesture)GestureByte;
1065 	const float S = UIScale();
1066 	const FVector2D C(CenterX, CenterY);
1067 	auto Line = [this, &Color, Thick](const FVector2D& A, const FVector2D& B)
1068 	{
1069 		DrawLine(A.X, A.Y, B.X, B.Y, Color, Thick);
1070 	};
1071 	auto Arrowhead = [&Line, S](const FVector2D& Tip, const FVector2D& InDir)
1072 	{
1073 		const FVector2D Dir = InDir.GetSafeNormal();
1074 		const FVector2D Normal(-Dir.Y, Dir.X);
1075 		const float Len = 24.f * S;
1076 		Line(Tip, Tip - Dir * Len + Normal * Len * 0.6f);
1077 		Line(Tip, Tip - Dir * Len - Normal * Len * 0.6f);
1078 	};
1079 
1080 	FVector2D Dir = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
1081 	switch (Gesture)
1082 	{
1083 	case EGBGesture::LineRight: Dir = FVector2D(1.f, 0.f); break;
1084 	case EGBGesture::LineLeft: Dir = FVector2D(-1.f, 0.f); break;
1085 	case EGBGesture::LineDown: Dir = FVector2D(0.f, 1.f); break;
1086 	case EGBGesture::LineUp: Dir = FVector2D(0.f, -1.f); break;
1087 	default: break;
1088 	}
1089 	if (!Dir.IsZero())
1090 	{
1091 		Line(C - Dir * Radius, C + Dir * Radius);
1092 		Arrowhead(C + Dir * Radius, Dir);
1093 		return;
1094 	}
1095 
1096 	// Circles: screen +Y is down, so a growing angle turns clockwise on screen. 300 degrees, starting at the top.
1097 	const float Sign = Gesture == EGBGesture::CircleClockwise ? 1.f : -1.f;
1098 	const int32 Segments = 28;
1099 	const float Start = -UE_HALF_PI;
1100 	const float Sweep = FMath::DegreesToRadians(300.f);
1101 	FVector2D Prev = C + FVector2D(FMath::Cos(Start), FMath::Sin(Start)) * Radius;
1102 	float Angle = Start;
1103 	for (int32 i = 1; i <= Segments; ++i)
1104 	{
1105 		Angle = Start + Sign * Sweep * i / Segments;
1106 		const FVector2D Next = C + FVector2D(FMath::Cos(Angle), FMath::Sin(Angle)) * Radius;
1107 		Line(Prev, Next);
1108 		Prev = Next;
1109 	}
1110 	Arrowhead(Prev, FVector2D(-FMath::Sin(Angle), FMath::Cos(Angle)) * Sign);
1111 }
1112 
1113 // ======================================================================== full-screen effects
1114 
1115 void AGBHUD::DrawDamageFlash(EGBHudSkin Skin)
1116 {
1117 	const float Elapsed = (float)(GetRealTime() - LastDamageTime);
1118 	if (Elapsed < 0.f || Elapsed >= GBHudLayout::DamageFlashTime)
1119 	{
1120 		return;
1121 	}
1122 	const float Strength = 1.f - Elapsed / GBHudLayout::DamageFlashTime;
1123 	const float W = Canvas->ClipX;
1124 	const float H = Canvas->ClipY;
1125 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk)
1126 	{
1127 		const float S = UIScale();
1128 		const FLinearColor Edge = GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.45f * Strength);
1129 		DrawFade(0.f, 0.f, W * 0.2f, H, Edge, true);
1130 		DrawFade(W * 0.8f, 0.f, W * 0.2f, H, Edge, false);
1131 		// A few torn scan bands: the "signal hit" look.
1132 		const int32 Seed = FMath::FloorToInt((float)GetRealTime() * 30.f);
1133 		for (int32 i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
1134 		{
1135 			const float R1 = FMath::Frac(FMath::Sin((Seed + i * 17) * 12.9898f) * 43758.5453f);
1136 			const float R2 = FMath::Frac(FMath::Sin((Seed + i * 31) * 78.233f) * 43758.5453f);
1137 			const float BY = R1 * H;
1138 			const float BH = (2.f + R2 * 8.f) * S;
1139 			FillRect(0.f, BY, W, BH, GBAlpha(i % 2 ? GBCy::Cyan : GBCy::Red, 0.12f * Strength));
1140 		}
1141 		return;
1142 	}
1143 	// RPG: a stepped blood vignette in whole art pixels.
1144 	const float P = ArtPixel();
1145 	constexpr int32 FlashLayers = 5;
1146 	for (int32 k = 0; k < FlashLayers; ++k)
1147 	{
1148 		const float T = 3.f * P;
1149 		const float Inset = k * T;
1150 		const FLinearColor Color = GBAlpha(GBRpg::Blood, 0.42f * Strength * (1.f - (float)k / FlashLayers));
1151 		FillRect(Inset, Inset, W - 2.f * Inset, T, Color);
1152 		FillRect(Inset, H - Inset - T, W - 2.f * Inset, T, Color);
1153 		FillRect(Inset, Inset + T, T, H - 2.f * (Inset + T), Color);
1154 		FillRect(W - Inset - T, Inset + T, T, H - 2.f * (Inset + T), Color);
1155 	}
1156 }
1157 
1158 void AGBHUD::DrawEraFade()
1159 {
1160 	const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this);
1161 	const float Alpha = Era ? FMath::Clamp(Era->GetFadeAlpha(), 0.f, 1.f) : 0.f;
1162 	if (Alpha <= 0.f)
1163 	{
1164 		return;
1165 	}
1166 	FillRect(0.f, 0.f, Canvas->ClipX, Canvas->ClipY, FLinearColor(0.82f, 0.9f, 1.f, Alpha));
1167 }

1510 // ======================================================================== drawing: pages
1511 
1512 void AGBHUD::DrawItemsPage(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, EGBHudSkin Skin, float X, float Y, float W, float H)
1513 {
1514 	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
1515 	const float S = UIScale();
1516 	const float P = ArtPixel();

1525 	}

1543 		const bool bSelected = i == MenuState.Cursor;
1544 		const float Pad = MenuRow(Skin, ListX, RowY, ListW - (bCyber ? 0.f : 8.f * P), RowH, bSelected, MenuState.PendingCombine == ItemId);

1561 			TagText += TEXT("[F]  ");
1562 		}

1569 		}
1570 	}

1573 	const FName Selected = List[MenuState.Cursor];

1575 	const float DX = X + ListW + (bCyber ? 40.f * S : 14.f * P);
1576 	const float DW = X + W - DX;
1577 	const FLinearColor TypeColor = ToneColor(Skin, EGBTextTone::Value);
1578 	float DY = MenuDetailPanel(Skin, DX, Y - (bCyber ? 0.f : 3.f * P), DW, H + (bCyber ? 0.f : 3.f * P), ItemName(Data, Selected), Def ? ItemTypeName(Def->Type) : FString(), &TypeColor);
1579 	const float Inner = bCyber ? 24.f * S : 7.f * P;
1580 	if (!Def)
1581 	{
1582 		return;
1583 	}
1584 	// Large icon on the right of the panel.
1585 	const float Big = bCyber ? 168.f * S : 40.f * P;
1586 	const FString* Sprite = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::Sprite);
1587 	if (bCyber)

1589 		CyBox(DX + DW - Inner - Big, DY, Big, Big, 14.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::CyanDeep, 0.35f), GBAlpha(GBCy::CyanDim, 0.9f), 0x5);
1590 	}
1591 	else
1592 	{
1593 		RpgFrame(DX + DW - Inner - Big, DY, Big, Big, ERpgFrame::Gold);
1594 		FillRect(DX + DW - Inner - Big + 3.f * P, DY + 3.f * P, Big - 6.f * P, Big - 6.f * P, GBRpg::StoneDeep);
1595 		DrawGlow(DX + DW - Inner - Big * 0.5f, DY + Big * 0.5f, Big * 0.45f, Big * 0.45f, GBAlpha(GBRpg::EmberLight, 0.15f));

1598 		bCyber ? FLinearColor(0.9f, 1.f, 1.f, 1.f) : FLinearColor::White, bCyber);
1599 	const float TextW = DW - 3.f * Inner - Big;
1600 	DY += UiWrapped(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Normal, Def->Description, DX + Inner, DY, TextW) + (bCyber ? 20.f * S : 6.f * P);
1601 	DY = FMath::Max(DY, DY);
1602 
1603 	TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Actions;
1604 	switch (Def->Type)
1605 	{
1606 	case EGBItemType::Consumable: Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Enter"), TEXT("Use"))); break;
1607 	case EGBItemType::Throwable: Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("F"), TEXT("Throw (quick-use)"))); Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Shift"), TEXT("Cycle"))); break;
1608 	case EGBItemType::Armor: Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Enter"), FString(Inventory->GetEquippedArmor() == Selected ? TEXT("Unequip") : TEXT("Equip")))); break;
1609 	case EGBItemType::Material:
1610 	case EGBItemType::KeyItem:
1611 		if (MenuState.PendingCombine == NAME_None)
1612 		{
1613 			Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Enter"), TEXT("Combine with another item")));
1614 		}
1615 		else if (MenuState.PendingCombine == Selected)
1616 		{
1617 			Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Esc"), TEXT("Cancel (choose a second item to combine)")));
1618 		}
1619 		else
1620 		{
1621 			Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Enter"), FString::Printf(TEXT("Combine with %s"), *ItemName(Data, MenuState.PendingCombine))));
1622 		}
1623 		break;
1624 	default: break;
1625 	}
1626 	const float HintY = FMath::Max(DY, Y + Big + (bCyber ? 60.f * S : 18.f * P));
1627 	MenuHints(Skin, Actions, DX + Inner, HintY, 0.f);
1628 }

1630 void AGBHUD::DrawWeaponsPage(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, EGBHudSkin Skin, float X, float Y, float W, float H)
1631 {
1632 	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
1633 	const float S = UIScale();
1634 	const float P = ArtPixel();
1635 	const bool bCyber = Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk;
1636 	const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player->GetEquipment();
1637 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
1638 	const TArray<FName> List = GetWeaponList(Player);
1639 	if (!Equipment || List.Num() == 0)
1640 	{
1641 		UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Muted, TEXT("You carry no weapons."), X, Y);
1642 		return;
1643 	}
1644 	MenuState.Cursor = FMath::Clamp(MenuState.Cursor, 0, List.Num() - 1);
1645 
1646 	// Display rows: a header before each kind, then its weapons. WeaponIndex -1 = header.
1647 	struct FRow
1648 	{
1649 		FString Text;
1650 		int32 WeaponIndex = -1;
1651 	};
1652 	TArray<FRow> Rows;
1653 	FString LastGroup;

1655 	for (int32 i = 0; i < List.Num(); ++i)
1656 	{

1658 		const FString Group = Def ? SlotName(Def->Slot, true) : FString(TEXT("Other"));
1659 		if (Group != LastGroup)
1660 		{
1661 			Rows.Add(FRow{ Group, -1 });
1662 			LastGroup = Group;
1663 		}
1664 		if (i == MenuState.Cursor)
1665 		{
1666 			CursorRow = Rows.Num();
1667 		}
1668 		Rows.Add(FRow{ WeaponName(Data, List[i]), i });
1669 	}
1670 
1671 	const float ListW = FMath::RoundToFloat(W * 0.46f);
1672 	const float ListX = X + (bCyber ? 0.f : 8.f * P);

1675 	if (!bCyber)
1676 	{
1677 		RpgWell(X - 2.f * P, Y - 3.f * P, ListW + 12.f * P, H + 3.f * P);
1678 	}
1679 	float RowY = Y;
1680 	const int32 Visible = FMath::Max(1, FMath::FloorToInt(H / RowH));
1681 	const int32 First = FMath::Clamp(CursorRow - Visible + 1, 0, FMath::Max(0, Rows.Num() - Visible));
1682 	for (int32 r = First; r < Rows.Num() && RowY + RowH <= Y + H + 1.f; ++r)

1685 		if (Row.WeaponIndex < 0)
1686 		{
1687 			const float HY = RowY + (bCyber ? 10.f * S : 4.f * P);
1688 			if (bCyber)
1689 			{
1690 				FCyTextStyle Head;
1691 				Head.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
1692 				Head.Px = 14.f;
1693 				Head.Color = GBCy::RedDim;
1694 				const FVector2D HS = DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("// %s"), *Row.Text.ToUpper()), ListX, HY, Head, 0.f);
1695 				FillRect(ListX + HS.X + 10.f * S, HY + HS.Y * 0.5f, ListW - HS.X - 10.f * S, 1.f, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDeep, 1.f));
1696 			}
1697 			else
1698 			{
1699 				const FVector2D HS = UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Accent, Row.Text.ToUpper(), ListX + 2.f * P, HY);
1700 				FillRect(ListX + HS.X + 6.f * P, HY + 3.f * P, ListW - HS.X - 18.f * P, P, GBRpg::Gold1);
1701 			}
1702 			RowY += HeaderH;
1703 			continue;
1704 		}

1718 		}

1720 		const FLinearColor EC = GBHudText::ElementColor(Element);

1729 			RpgGem(RightX - 2.f * P, RowY + (RowH - P) * 0.5f, EC, true);
1730 			const float SH = UiMeasure(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, TEXT("A")).Y;
1731 			UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Normal, GBHudText::ElementName(Element), RightX - 8.f * P, RowY + FMath::RoundToFloat((RowH - P - SH) * 0.5f), 1.f, &EC);
1732 		}
1733 		RowY += RowH;
1734 	}
1735 
1736 	// Details of the selected weapon.
1737 	const FName Selected = List[MenuState.Cursor];
1738 	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(Selected) : nullptr;
1739 	const float DX = X + ListW + (bCyber ? 40.f * S : 14.f * P);
1740 	const float DW = X + W - DX;
1741 	const float Inner = bCyber ? 24.f * S : 7.f * P;
1742 	const EGBElement Element = Equipment->GetWeaponElement(Selected);
1743 	const FLinearColor EC = GBHudText::ElementColor(Element);
1744 	const FString Subtitle = Def ? FString::Printf(TEXT("%s  -  %s"), *SlotName(Def->Slot, false), *GBHudText::ElementName(Element)) : FString();
1745 	float DY = MenuDetailPanel(Skin, DX, Y - (bCyber ? 0.f : 3.f * P), DW, H + (bCyber ? 0.f : 3.f * P), WeaponName(Data, Selected), Subtitle, &EC);
1746 	if (!Def)
1747 	{
1748 		UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Muted, TEXT("Unknown weapon"), DX + Inner, DY);
1749 		return;
1750 	}

1752 	const float LineH = UiLineHeight(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body) + (bCyber ? 4.f * S : P);
1753 	for (const FString& Line : WeaponStatLines(*Def))
1754 	{
1755 		// "Damage 10 x8" -> label "Damage", value "10 x8": split at the first digit.
1756 		int32 Split = INDEX_NONE;
1757 		for (int32 c = 0; c < Line.Len(); ++c)
1758 		{
1759 			if (FChar::IsDigit(Line[c]))
1760 			{
1761 				Split = c;
1762 				break;
1763 			}
1764 		}
1765 		const FString Label = Split == INDEX_NONE ? Line : Line.Left(Split).TrimEnd();
1766 		const FString Value = Split == INDEX_NONE ? FString() : Line.Mid(Split);
1767 		UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Muted, Label, DX + Inner, DY);
1768 		UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Value, Value, DX + DW - Inner, DY, 1.f);

1772 		}

1789 		MenuHints(Skin, Actions, DX + Inner, DY, 0.f);

1793 void AGBHUD::DrawSpellsPage(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, EGBHudSkin Skin, float X, float Y, float W, float H)
1794 {
1795 	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
1796 	const float S = UIScale();
1797 	const float P = ArtPixel();
1798 	const bool bCyber = Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk;

1800 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
1801 	if (!Spellbook)

1803 		return;
1804 	}
1805 	const int32 NumSlots = UGBSpellbookComponent::NumSlots;
1806 	const float ListW = FMath::RoundToFloat(W * 0.46f);
1807 	const float ListX = X + (bCyber ? 0.f : 8.f * P);
1808 	const float RowH = bCyber ? 52.f * S : 17.f * P;
1809 	const float RowGap = bCyber ? 6.f * S : 2.f * P;

1813 	}

1815 	const float ListY = Y + HeadSize.Y + (bCyber ? 14.f * S : 6.f * P);
1816 	for (int32 i = 0; i <= NumSlots; ++i)

1821 		const float Pad = MenuRow(Skin, ListX, RowY, RowWidth, RowH, bSelected && !MenuState.bChoosingSpell, bSelected && MenuState.bChoosingSpell);
1822 		AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::SpellSlot, ListX, RowY, RowWidth, RowH, i);
1823 		const FName SpellId = i < NumSlots ? Spellbook->GetSlotSpell(i) : Spellbook->GetEnchantSpell();
1824 		const FString Key = i < NumSlots ? FString(SlotKey(i)) : FString(TEXT("T"));
1825 		// Key, icon, name.
1826 		float KX = ListX + Pad;
1827 		if (bCyber)
1828 		{
1829 			KX += CyKey(KX, RowY + (RowH - 24.f * S) * 0.5f, Key, GBCy::Cyan, 24.f) + 12.f * S;
1830 		}
1831 		else
1832 		{
1833 			float KW = 0.f;
1834 			RpgKeyCap(KX, RowY + FMath::RoundToFloat((RowH - 11.f * P) * 0.5f / P) * P, Key, &KW);
1835 			KX += KW + 3.f * P;
1836 		}
1837 		const float IconS = RowH - (bCyber ? 14.f * S : 5.f * P);
1838 		if (SpellId != NAME_None)
1839 		{
1840 			if (bCyber)
1841 			{
1842 				CyBox(KX, RowY + (RowH - IconS) * 0.5f, IconS, IconS, 6.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.8f), GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDim, 1.f), 0x4);
1843 			}
1844 			else

1847 				DrawOutline(KX, RowY + (RowH - IconS) * 0.5f, IconS, IconS, GBRpg::Gold1, P);
1848 			}
1849 			DrawSpriteFit(GBHudText::SpellIconId(SpellId), KX + 2.f, RowY + (RowH - IconS) * 0.5f + 2.f, IconS - 4.f, IconS - 4.f);
1850 		}

1854 		if (i < NumSlots)
1855 		{
1856 			Name = SpellId == NAME_None ? FString(TEXT("(empty)")) : GBHudText::SpellName(this, SpellId);

1863 		if (i == NumSlots)

1866 			FillRect(ListX + RowWidth - (bCyber ? 10.f * S : 4.f * P), RowY + (bCyber ? 8.f * S : 3.f * P), bCyber ? 4.f * S : P, RowH - (bCyber ? 16.f * S : 6.f * P), EC);

1871 	FString Passives;
1872 	if (Data)

1874 		for (const FName& SpellId : Spellbook->GetKnownSpells())
1875 		{
1876 			const FGBSpellDefinition* Def = Data->FindSpell(SpellId);
1877 			if (Def && Def->bPassive)
1878 			{
1879 				Passives += (Passives.IsEmpty() ? TEXT("") : TEXT(", ")) + GBHudText::SpellName(this, SpellId);
1880 			}
1881 		}
1882 	}
1883 	float PassY = ListY + (NumSlots + 1) * (RowH + RowGap) + (bCyber ? 18.f * S : 5.f * P);
1884 	PassY += UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Accent, TEXT("PASSIVES (ALWAYS ACTIVE)"), ListX + (bCyber ? 0.f : 2.f * P), PassY).Y + (bCyber ? 10.f * S : 4.f * P);
1885 	UiWrapped(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Normal, Passives.IsEmpty() ? FString(TEXT("None yet")) : Passives, ListX + (bCyber ? 0.f : 2.f * P), PassY, ListW - 12.f * P);
1886 
1887 	// Right side: the spell chooser, or details of the selected slot.
1888 	const float DX = X + ListW + (bCyber ? 40.f * S : 14.f * P);
1889 	const float DW = X + W - DX;
1890 	const float Inner = bCyber ? 24.f * S : 7.f * P;
1891 	const float PanelY = Y - (bCyber ? 0.f : 3.f * P);
1892 	const float PanelH = H + (bCyber ? 0.f : 3.f * P);
1893 	if (MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
1894 	{
1895 		const TArray<FName> Options = GetAssignableSpells(Player);
1896 		MenuState.SubCursor = FMath::Clamp(MenuState.SubCursor, 0, FMath::Max(0, Options.Num() - 1));
1897 		float DY = MenuDetailPanel(Skin, DX, PanelY, DW, PanelH, FString::Printf(TEXT("Spell for slot %s"), SlotKey(MenuState.Cursor)), TEXT("Choose a spell"), nullptr);
1898 		const float OptH = bCyber ? 46.f * S : 13.f * P;
1899 		const float DescReserve = bCyber ? 110.f * S : 34.f * P;
1900 		const int32 Visible = FMath::Max(1, FMath::FloorToInt((PanelY + PanelH - DY - DescReserve) / OptH));
1901 		const int32 First = FMath::Clamp(MenuState.SubCursor - Visible + 1, 0, FMath::Max(0, Options.Num() - Visible));
1902 		for (int32 i = First; i < Options.Num() && i < First + Visible; ++i)
1903 		{
1904 			const FName SpellId = Options[i];
1905 			const bool bSel = i == MenuState.SubCursor;
1906 			const float RX = DX + Inner + (bCyber ? 0.f : 6.f * P);
1907 			const float RW = DW - 2.f * Inner - (bCyber ? 0.f : 6.f * P);
1908 			const float Pad = MenuRow(Skin, RX, DY, RW, OptH - (bCyber ? 4.f * S : P), bSel);
1909 			AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::SpellChoice, RX, DY, RW, OptH, i);
1910 			FString Text = SpellId == NAME_None ? FString(TEXT("(empty the slot)")) : GBHudText::SpellName(this, SpellId);
1911 			for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < NumSlots; ++Slot)
1912 			{
1913 				if (SpellId != NAME_None && Spellbook->GetSlotSpell(Slot) == SpellId)
1914 				{
1915 					Text += FString::Printf(TEXT("  (in %s)"), SlotKey(Slot));
1916 				}
1917 			}
1918 			const float IconS = bCyber ? 34.f * S : OptH - 5.f * P;
1919 			DrawSpriteFit(GBHudText::SpellIconId(SpellId), RX + Pad, DY + (OptH - IconS) * 0.5f - (bCyber ? 2.f * S : 0.f), IconS, IconS);
1920 			const float LH = UiMeasure(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, TEXT("Ag")).Y;
1921 			UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, bSel ? EGBTextTone::Selected : EGBTextTone::Normal, Text, RX + Pad + IconS + (bCyber ? 10.f * S : 3.f * P), DY + FMath::RoundToFloat((OptH - (bCyber ? 4.f * S : P) - LH) * 0.5f));
1922 			DY += OptH;
1923 		}
1924 		const FName Hovered = Options.IsValidIndex(MenuState.SubCursor) ? Options[MenuState.SubCursor] : NAME_None;
1925 		const FGBSpellDefinition* Def = (Data && Hovered != NAME_None) ? Data->FindSpell(Hovered) : nullptr;
1926 		if (Def)
1927 		{
1928 			UiWrapped(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Normal, Def->Description, DX + Inner, PanelY + PanelH - DescReserve + (bCyber ? 10.f * S : 3.f * P), DW - 2.f * Inner);
1929 		}
1930 		return;
1931 	}
1932 
1933 	if (MenuState.Cursor < NumSlots)
1934 	{
1935 		const FName SpellId = Spellbook->GetSlotSpell(MenuState.Cursor);
1936 		const FGBSpellDefinition* Def = (Data && SpellId != NAME_None) ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
1937 		if (Def)
1938 		{
1939 			const FLinearColor EC = GBHudText::ElementColor(Def->Element);
1940 			float DY = MenuDetailPanel(Skin, DX, PanelY, DW, PanelH, GBHudText::SpellName(this, SpellId),
1941 				FString::Printf(TEXT("%s  -  %d mana"), *GBHudText::ElementName(Def->Element), FMath::RoundToInt(Spellbook->GetManaCost(SpellId))), &EC);
1942 			const float Big = bCyber ? 112.f * S : 30.f * P;
1943 			if (bCyber)
1944 			{
1945 				CyBox(DX + DW - Inner - Big, DY, Big, Big, 12.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.7f), GBAlpha(EC, 0.9f), 0x5);
1946 			}
1947 			else
1948 			{
1949 				RpgFrame(DX + DW - Inner - Big, DY, Big, Big, ERpgFrame::Gold);
1950 				FillRect(DX + DW - Inner - Big + 3.f * P, DY + 3.f * P, Big - 6.f * P, Big - 6.f * P, GBRpg::StoneDeep);
1951 			}
1952 			DrawGlow(DX + DW - Inner - Big * 0.5f, DY + Big * 0.5f, Big * 0.5f, Big * 0.5f, GBAlpha(EC, 0.25f));
1953 			DrawSpriteFit(GBHudText::SpellIconId(SpellId), DX + DW - Inner - Big + Big * 0.15f, DY + Big * 0.15f, Big * 0.7f, Big * 0.7f);
1954 			DY += UiWrapped(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Normal, Def->Description, DX + Inner, DY, DW - 3.f * Inner - Big);
1955 			TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Actions;
1956 			Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Enter"), TEXT("Choose a spell")));
1957 			MenuHints(Skin, Actions, DX + Inner, FMath::Max(DY + (bCyber ? 20.f * S : 6.f * P), PanelY + Big + (bCyber ? 150.f * S : 40.f * P)), 0.f);
1958 		}
1959 		else
1960 		{
1961 			const float DY = MenuDetailPanel(Skin, DX, PanelY, DW, PanelH, TEXT("Empty slot"), FString(), nullptr);
1962 			TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Actions;
1963 			Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Enter"), TEXT("Choose a spell")));
1964 			MenuHints(Skin, Actions, DX + Inner, DY, 0.f);
1965 		}
1966 		return;
1967 	}
1968 	const FName EnchantId = Spellbook->GetEnchantSpell();
1969 	const EGBElement Element = Spellbook->GetEnchantElement();
1970 	const FLinearColor EC = GBHudText::ElementColor(Element);
1971 	float DY = MenuDetailPanel(Skin, DX, PanelY, DW, PanelH, EnchantId != NAME_None ? GBHudText::SpellName(this, EnchantId) : FString(TEXT("Weapon enchant")),
1972 		FString::Printf(TEXT("Element: %s"), *GBHudText::ElementName(Element)), &EC);
1973 	if (const FGBSpellDefinition* Def = (Data && EnchantId != NAME_None) ? Data->FindSpell(EnchantId) : nullptr)
1974 	{
1975 		DY += UiWrapped(Skin, EGBTextSize::Body, EGBTextTone::Normal, Def->Description, DX + Inner, DY, DW - 2.f * Inner) + (bCyber ? 12.f * S : 4.f * P);
1976 	}
1977 	DY += UiWrapped(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Muted, TEXT("Only with a gun in hand (T). Enchants the gun with the chosen element."), DX + Inner, DY, DW - 2.f * Inner) + (bCyber ? 16.f * S : 5.f * P);
1978 	// The three elements as swatches, the chosen one lit.
1979 	const EGBElement Choices[3] = { EGBElement::Fire, EGBElement::Lightning, EGBElement::Ice };
1980 	float SX = DX + Inner;
1981 	for (const EGBElement Choice : Choices)
1982 	{
1983 		const bool bOn = Choice == Element;
1984 		const FLinearColor C = GBHudText::ElementColor(Choice);
1985 		const FString Name = GBHudText::ElementName(Choice);
1986 		if (bCyber)
1987 		{
1988 			const float SW = 140.f * S;
1989 			CyBox(SX, DY, SW, 40.f * S, 8.f * S, bOn ? GBAlpha(C, 0.25f) : GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.6f), bOn ? C : GBAlpha(C, 0.35f), 0x4);
1990 			UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Normal, Name.ToUpper(), SX + SW * 0.5f, DY + 12.f * S, 0.5f, bOn ? &C : &GBCy::Grey);
1991 			SX += SW + 10.f * S;
1992 		}
1993 		else
1994 		{
1995 			RpgGem(SX + 4.f * P, DY + 4.f * P, C, bOn);
1996 			const FLinearColor TextColor = bOn ? C : GBRpg::BoneDark;
1997 			const FVector2D NS = UiText(Skin, EGBTextSize::Small, EGBTextTone::Normal, Name, SX + 10.f * P, DY + 2.f * P, 0.f, &TextColor);
1998 			SX += NS.X + 18.f * P;
1999 		}
2000 	}
2001 	DY += bCyber ? 60.f * S : 14.f * P;
2002 	TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Actions;
2003 	Actions.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Enter"), TEXT("Change element")));
2004 	MenuHints(Skin, Actions, DX + Inner, DY, 0.f);
2005 }

2009 void AGBHUD::DrawSkillNode(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, int32 Tree, FName NodeId, float X, float Y, float W, float H)
2010 {
2011 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
2012 	const UGBSkillTreeComponent* SkillTree = Player->GetSkillTree();
2013 	const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr;
2014 	const int32 Rank = SkillTree ? SkillTree->GetRank(NodeId) : 0;
2015 	const int32 MaxRank = Def ? FMath::Max(1, Def->MaxRank) : 1;
2016 	const bool bCan = SkillTree && SkillTree->CanUnlock(NodeId) == EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok;
2017 	const bool bSelected = NodeId == MenuState.SkillCursor;
2018 	const bool bMax = Rank >= MaxRank;
2019 	const FString Name = GBHudMenuLocal::SkillName(Data, NodeId);
2020 	FString LineA;
2021 	FString LineB;
2022 	const float S = UIScale();
2023 	const float P = ArtPixel();
2024 
2025 	if (Tree == 1)
2026 	{
2027 		// Cyberpunk node: cut-corner box, condensed caps, rank segments, cost tag.
2028 		const FLinearColor Line = bSelected ? GBCy::Cyan : (bMax ? GBCy::Red : (Rank > 0 ? GBCy::Red : (bCan ? GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.75f) : GBAlpha(GBCy::Grey, 0.6f))));
2029 		const FLinearColor Fill = bMax ? GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.28f) : (Rank > 0 ? GBAlpha(GBCy::RedDeep, 0.85f) : GBAlpha(GBCy::Ink, 0.85f));
2030 		if (bSelected)
2031 		{
2032 			DrawGlow(X + W * 0.5f, Y + H * 0.5f, W * 0.75f, H, GBAlpha(GBCy::Cyan, 0.16f));
2033 		}
2034 		CyBox(X, Y, W, H, 10.f * S, Fill, Line, 0x5, bSelected ? 2.f : 1.f);
2035 		if (bSelected)
2036 		{
2037 			CyBrackets(X - 5.f * S, Y - 5.f * S, W + 10.f * S, H + 10.f * S, 9.f * S, GBCy::Cyan, 2.f * S);
2038 		}
2039 		FCyTextStyle NameStyle;
2040 		NameStyle.Face = ECyFace::Condensed;
2041 		NameStyle.Px = 15.f;
2042 		NameStyle.Spacing = 60;
2043 		NameStyle.bUpper = true;
2044 		NameStyle.Color = bSelected ? GBCy::Pale : (Rank > 0 || bCan ? GBCy::RedBright : GBCy::Grey);
2045 		const float TextW = W - 16.f * S;
2046 		if (MeasureCyText(Name, NameStyle).X > TextW)
2047 		{
2048 			GBHudMenuDraw::SplitName(Name, LineA, LineB);
2049 		}
2050 		else
2051 		{
2052 			LineA = Name;
2053 		}
2054 		const float LH = MeasureCyText(TEXT("A"), NameStyle).Y;
2055 		const float TextY = Y + (LineB.IsEmpty() ? H * 0.5f - LH * 0.85f : H * 0.5f - LH * 1.3f);
2056 		DrawCyText(LineA, X + W * 0.5f, TextY, NameStyle, 0.5f);
2057 		if (!LineB.IsEmpty())
2058 		{
2059 			DrawCyText(LineB, X + W * 0.5f, TextY + LH * 0.92f, NameStyle, 0.5f);
2060 		}
2061 		// Rank segments.
2062 		const float SegW = 14.f * S;
2063 		const float SegGap = 4.f * S;
2064 		const float SegsW = MaxRank * SegW + (MaxRank - 1) * SegGap;
2065 		const float SegY = Y + H - 12.f * S;
2066 		for (int32 i = 0; i < MaxRank; ++i)
2067 		{
2068 			FillRect(X + (W - SegsW) * 0.5f + i * (SegW + SegGap), SegY, SegW, 3.f * S, i < Rank ? GBCy::Red : GBAlpha(GBCy::Grey, 0.35f));
2069 		}
2070 		if (Def && Def->Cost > 1)
2071 		{
2072 			FCyTextStyle Cost;
2073 			Cost.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
2074 			Cost.Px = 11.f;
2075 			Cost.Color = GBCy::Yellow;
2076 			DrawCyText(FString::Printf(TEXT("%dPT"), Def->Cost), X + W - 8.f * S, Y + 3.f * S, Cost, 1.f);
2077 		}
2078 		return;
2079 	}
2080 
2081 	// RPG node: a stone tablet in a gold (or iron) frame, pixel name, gems for ranks.
2082 	const float NX = FMath::RoundToFloat(X / P) * P;
2083 	const float NY = FMath::RoundToFloat(Y / P) * P;
2084 	const float NW = FMath::RoundToFloat(W / P) * P;
2085 	const float NH = FMath::RoundToFloat(H / P) * P;
2086 	if (bSelected)
2087 	{
2088 		DrawGlow(NX + NW * 0.5f, NY + NH * 0.5f, NW * 0.8f, NH * 1.1f, GBAlpha(GBRpg::EmberLight, 0.30f));
2089 	}
2090 	FillRect(NX, NY, NW, NH, GBRpg::Stone1);
2091 	RpgStone(NX, NY, NW, NH, bMax ? FLinearColor(1.25f, 1.1f, 0.9f, 1.f) : (Rank > 0 || bCan ? FLinearColor::White : FLinearColor(0.6f, 0.6f, 0.65f, 1.f)));
2092 	if (Rank > 0)
2093 	{
2094 		FillRect(NX, NY, NW, NH, GBAlpha(GBRpg::Gold1, bMax ? 0.35f : 0.18f));
2095 	}
2096 	RpgFrame(NX, NY, NW, NH, (Rank > 0 || bCan || bSelected) ? ERpgFrame::Gold : ERpgFrame::Inset);
2097 	if (bSelected)
2098 	{
2099 		DrawOutline(NX - P, NY - P, NW + 2.f * P, NH + 2.f * P, GBRpg::Gold4, P);
2100 	}
2101 	const int32 Small = FMath::Max(2, FMath::RoundToInt(P * 0.5f));
2102 	FPixelTextStyle NameStyle;
2103 	NameStyle.Scale = Small;
2104 	NameStyle.Ink = bSelected || Rank > 0 ? &GBRpg::InkGold : (bCan ? &GBRpg::InkBone : nullptr);
2105 	NameStyle.Color = (bSelected || Rank > 0 || bCan) ? FLinearColor::White : GBRpg::BoneDark;
2106 	NameStyle.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
2107 	// Text area: between the top frame (4 art pixels) and the pip row (6 from the bottom).
2108 	const float LineH = 8.f * Small;
2109 	const float AreaTop = NY + 4.f * P;
2110 	const float AreaH = NH - 10.f * P;
2111 	const float TextMaxW = NW - 6.f * P;
2112 	if (MeasurePixelText(Name, Small).X <= TextMaxW)
2113 	{
2114 		LineA = Name;
2115 	}
2116 	else if (AreaH >= LineH + 7.f * Small)
2117 	{
2118 		GBHudMenuDraw::SplitName(Name, LineA, LineB);
2119 	}
2120 	else
2121 	{
2122 		// Too short for two lines (small screens): one abbreviated line; the detail panel shows the full name.
2123 		const int32 MaxChars = FMath::Max(4, FMath::FloorToInt(TextMaxW / (6.f * Small)));
2124 		LineA = GBHudText::Abbreviate(Name, MaxChars);
2125 	}
2126 	const float BlockH = LineB.IsEmpty() ? 7.f * Small : LineH + 7.f * Small;
2127 	const float TextY = FMath::RoundToFloat((AreaTop + (AreaH - BlockH) * 0.5f) / Small) * Small;
2128 	DrawPixelText(LineA, NX + NW * 0.5f, TextY, NameStyle, 0.5f);
2129 	if (!LineB.IsEmpty())
2130 	{
2131 		DrawPixelText(LineB, NX + NW * 0.5f, TextY + LineH, NameStyle, 0.5f);
2132 	}
2133 	// Rank pips: tiny gems along the bottom.
2134 	const float PipW = 4.f * P;
2135 	const float PipGap = 2.f * P;
2136 	const float PipsW = MaxRank * PipW + (MaxRank - 1) * PipGap;
2137 	const float PipY = NY + NH - 5.f * P;
2138 	for (int32 i = 0; i < MaxRank; ++i)
2139 	{
2140 		const float PX = FMath::RoundToFloat((NX + (NW - PipsW) * 0.5f) / P) * P + i * (PipW + PipGap);
2141 		FillRect(PX, PipY, PipW, 2.f * P, GBRpg::Outline);
2142 		FillRect(PX + P, PipY, PipW - 2.f * P, P, i < Rank ? GBRpg::Gold4 : GBRpg::Iron1);
2143 	}
2144 	if (Def && Def->Cost > 1)
2145 	{
2146 		FPixelTextStyle Cost;
2147 		Cost.Scale = Small;
2148 		Cost.Ink = &GBRpg::InkEmber;
2149 		Cost.Shadow = FLinearColor(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.9f);
2150 		// A small cost badge hanging on the bottom-right corner of the tablet (only for nodes that cost more than 1).

exec

1306 
1307 void AGBHUD::DrawDeathScreen(EGBHudSkin Skin)
1308 {
1309 	const float S = UIScale();
1310 	const float P = ArtPixel();
1311 	const float W = Canvas->ClipX;
1312 	const float H = Canvas->ClipY;
1313 	const float Since = (float)(GetRealTime() - MenuOpenTime);
1314 	AddMenuHit(EMenuHitKind::DeathScreen, 0.f, 0.f, W, H);
1315 	TArray<TPair<FString, FString>> Hints;
1316 	Hints.Add(TPair<FString, FString>(TEXT("Enter"), TEXT("Respawn at the last checkpoint")));
1317 
1318 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk)
1319 	{
1320 		FillRect(0.f, 0.f, W, H, GBAlpha(GBCy::RedBlack, 0.82f));
1321 		DrawGlow(W * 0.5f, H * 0.45f, W * 0.6f, H * 0.4f, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.18f));
1322 		CyScanlines(0.f, 0.f, W, H, 0.3f);
1323 		// A flat line across the screen with one heartbeat that dies out.
1324 		const float LineY = H * 0.62f;
1325 		FillRect(0.f, LineY, W, 2.f * S, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.75f));
1326 		const float BX = W * 0.30f;
1327 		TArray<FVector2D> Beat = { FVector2D(BX, LineY), FVector2D(BX + 14.f * S, LineY - 30.f * S), FVector2D(BX + 26.f * S, LineY + 22.f * S), FVector2D(BX + 38.f * S, LineY - 8.f * S), FVector2D(BX + 48.f * S, LineY) };
1328 		for (int32 i = 1; i < Beat.Num(); ++i)
1329 		{
1330 			DrawLine(Beat[i - 1].X, Beat[i - 1].Y, Beat[i].X, Beat[i].Y, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.9f), 2.f * S);
1331 		}
1332 		FCyTextStyle Sub;
1333 		Sub.Face = ECyFace::Mono;
1334 		Sub.Px = 16.f;
1335 		Sub.Color = GBCy::RedDim;
1336 		DrawCyText(TEXT("VITAL SIGNS LOST // RESTORE POINT FOUND"), W * 0.5f, H * 0.33f, Sub, 0.5f);
1337 		FCyTextStyle Title;

 347 EGBCastFailReason UGBSpellbookComponent::CanCast(FName SpellId) const
 348 {
 349 	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 350 	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
 351 	if (!Definition || Definition->bPassive)
 352 	{
 353 		return EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell;
 354 	}
 355 	if (!KnowsSpell(SpellId))
 356 	{
 357 		return EGBCastFailReason::NotKnown;
 358 	}
 359 	const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment();
 360 	const bool bRightItem = Equipment && (Definition->bRequiresGun ? Equipment->IsHoldingGun() : Equipment->IsHoldingStaff());
 361 	if (!bRightItem)
 362 	{
 363 		return EGBCastFailReason::WrongHeldItem;
 364 	}
 365 	if (IsBusy())
 366 	{
 367 		return EGBCastFailReason::Busy;
 368 	}
 369 	if (GetCooldownRemaining(SpellId) > 0.f)
 370 	{
 371 		return EGBCastFailReason::OnCooldown;
 372 	}
 373 	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes();
 374 	const float Cost = GetManaCost(SpellId);
 375 	if (Cost > 0.f && (!Attributes || !Attributes->HasMana(Cost)))
 376 	{
 377 		return EGBCastFailReason::NotEnoughMana;
 378 	}
 379 	return EGBCastFailReason::None;
 380 }
 381 
 382 bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastSlot(int32 Slot)
 383 {
 384 	const FName SpellId = GetSlotSpell(Slot);
 385 	if (!SpellId.IsNone() && IsValid(ActiveSustained) && ActiveSustained->GetSpellCast().SpellId == SpellId)
 386 	{
 387 		ActiveSustained->OnCastReleased();
 388 		return true;
 389 	}
 390 	if (SpellId.IsNone())
 391 	{
 392 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(NAME_None, EGBCastFailReason::EmptySlot);
 393 		return false;
 394 	}
 395 	return TryCastSpell(SpellId);
 396 }
 397 
 398 bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastEnchant()
 399 {
 400 	if (EnchantSpellId.IsNone())
 401 	{
 402 		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(NAME_None, EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell);
 403 		return false;
 404 	}
 405 	return TryCastSpell(EnchantSpellId);
 739 	CooldownLength.Add(SpellId, Seconds);
 740 }
 741 
 742 float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetCooldownRemaining(FName SpellId) const
 743 {
 744 	const double* End = CooldownEnd.Find(SpellId);
 745 	return End ? static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(0.0, *End - GetNow())) : 0.f;
 746 }
 747 
 748 float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetCooldownDuration(FName SpellId) const
 749 {
 750 	return CooldownLength.FindRef(SpellId);
 751 }
 752 
 753 float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetManaCost(FName SpellId) const
 754 {
 755 	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 756 	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
 757 	if (!Definition)
 758 	{
 759 		return 0.f;
 760 	}
 761 	return FMath::Max(0.f, GBSpell::ResolveParam(*Definition, GBStat::ManaCost, 0.f, 0.f, false, GetOwnerStats(), 0.f));
 762 }

 764 // ---- Snapshot ----
 765 
 766 void UGBSpellbookComponent::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
 767 {
 768 	Snapshot.KnownSpells = KnownSpells;
 769 	Snapshot.Loadout = Loadout;
 770 	Snapshot.EnchantElement = EnchantElement;
 771 }
 772 
 773 void UGBSpellbookComponent::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
 774 {
 775 	CancelSustained();
 776 	RemoveAllPassives();
 777 
 778 	KnownSpells.Reset();
 779 	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 780 	for (const FName SpellId : Snapshot.KnownSpells)
 781 	{
 782 		if (Data && Data->FindSpell(SpellId))
 783 		{
 784 			KnownSpells.AddUnique(SpellId);
 785 		}
 786 	}
 787 	if (!EnchantSpellId.IsNone() && Data && Data->FindSpell(EnchantSpellId))
 788 	{
 789 		KnownSpells.AddUnique(EnchantSpellId);
 790 	}
 791 
 792 	Loadout.Init(NAME_None, NumSlots);
 793 	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < NumSlots && Slot < Snapshot.Loadout.Num(); ++Slot)
 794 	{
 795 		const FName SpellId = Snapshot.Loadout[Slot];
 796 		if (IsEquippable(SpellId) && !Loadout.Contains(SpellId))
 797 		{
 798 			Loadout[Slot] = SpellId;
 799 		}
 800 	}
 801 	EnchantElement = Snapshot.EnchantElement;
 802 
 803 	CooldownEnd.Reset();
 804 	CooldownLength.Reset();
 805 	ReapplyPassives();
 806 	OnLoadoutChanged.Broadcast();
 807 }

2993 	static bool Tick(float)
2994 	{
2995 		UWorld* World = SmokeWorld.Get();
2996 		if (!World)
2997 		{
2998 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE aborted: the world went away"));
2999 			return false;
3000 		}
3001 		// Read the clock again for every step: a step may wind StartSeconds back (the reload steps stretch the run).
3002 		while (NextStep < Steps.Num() && Steps[NextStep].Time <= static_cast<float>(FPlatformTime::Seconds() - StartSeconds))
3003 		{
3004 			const FStep& Gate = Steps[NextStep];
3005 			if (Gate.MinSimSinceMark > 0.f && World->GetTimeSeconds() - SimMark < Gate.MinSimSinceMark
3006 				&& FPlatformTime::Seconds() - StartSeconds - Gate.Time < SimGateTimeoutSeconds)
3007 			{
3008 				break; // not enough simulated time since the first sample yet: try again next tick
3009 			}
3010 			const FStep& Step = Steps[NextStep++];
3011 			AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(World);
3012 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE step %d/%d (%.1f s): %s"), NextStep, Steps.Num(), Step.Time, Step.Name);
3013 			if (!Player)
3014 			{
3015 				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE step skipped: there is no player character"));
3016 				continue;
3017 			}
3018 			Step.Run(World, Player);
3019 		}
3020 		return NextStep < Steps.Num();
3021 	}
3022 
3023 	static void Start(const TArray<FString>& Args, UWorld* World)
3024 	{
3025 		if (!World || !World->IsGameWorld())
3026 		{
3027 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.Smoke needs a running game world"));
3028 			return;
3029 		}
3030 		BuildSteps();
3031 		if (Args.Num() > 0)
3032 		{
3033 			// Filtered run (gb.Smoke Entity): only the steps whose name starts with the argument, plus the quit step, from 1 s on.
3034 			const FString Prefix = Args[0];
3035 			Steps.RemoveAll([&Prefix](const FStep& Step) { return !FString(Step.Name).StartsWith(Prefix, ESearchCase::IgnoreCase) && FCString::Strcmp(Step.Name, TEXT("quit")) != 0; });
3036 			if (Steps.Num() <= 1)
3037 			{
3038 				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.Smoke %s: no step starts with that name"), *Prefix);
3039 			}
3040 			float FirstTime = Steps.Num() > 0 ? Steps[0].Time : 0.f;
3041 			for (const FStep& Step : Steps)
3042 			{
3043 				FirstTime = FMath::Min(FirstTime, Step.Time);
3044 			}
3045 			for (FStep& Step : Steps)
3046 			{
3047 				Step.Time -= FirstTime - 1.f;
3048 			}
3049 		}
3050 		NextStep = 0;
3051 		StartSeconds = FPlatformTime::Seconds();
3052 		SmokeWorld = World;
3053 		FTSTicker::GetCoreTicker().RemoveTicker(TickerHandle);
3054 		TickerHandle = FTSTicker::GetCoreTicker().AddTicker(FTickerDelegate::CreateStatic(&Tick));
3055 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE started: %d steps"), Steps.Num());
3056 	}

3061 		FConsoleCommandWithWorldAndArgsDelegate::CreateStatic(&Start));

3063 	static void RunBuildUp(const TArray<FString>& Args, UWorld* World)
3064 	{
3065 		if (Args.Num() < 2)
3066 		{
3067 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.BuildUp <Fire|Lightning|Ice> <Amount>"));
3068 			return;
3069 		}
3070 		EGBElement Element = EGBElement::Fire;
3071 		if (Args[0].Equals(TEXT("Fire"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
3072 		{
3073 			Element = EGBElement::Fire;
3074 		}
3075 		else if (Args[0].Equals(TEXT("Lightning"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
3076 		{
3077 			Element = EGBElement::Lightning;
3078 		}
3079 		else if (Args[0].Equals(TEXT("Ice"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
3080 		{
3081 			Element = EGBElement::Ice;
3082 		}
3083 		else
3084 		{
3085 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.BuildUp: unknown element '%s' (Fire|Lightning|Ice)"), *Args[0]);
3086 			return;
3087 		}
3088 		const float Amount = FCString::Atof(*Args[1]);
3089 		AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(World);
3090 		if (!Player)
3091 		{
3092 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.BuildUp: no player character"));
3093 			return;
3094 		}
3095 		const FVector PlayerLoc = Player->GetActorLocation();
3096 		for (TActorIterator<AGBEnemyCharacter> It(World); It; ++It)
3097 		{
3098 			AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = *It;
3099 			if (!Enemy || Enemy->IsDead())
3100 			{
3101 				continue;
3102 			}
3103 			if (FVector::Dist(PlayerLoc, Enemy->GetActorLocation()) > 1500.f)
3104 			{
3105 				continue;
3106 			}
3107 			FGBDamageInfo Info;
3108 			Info.Element = Element;
3109 			Info.Instigator = Player;
3110 			Info.BuildUp = Amount;
3111 			Info.BuildUpEventId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
3112 			UGBCombatLibrary::ApplyBuildUp(Enemy, Info);
3113 			if (const UGBElementalStatusComponent* Elemental = Enemy->GetElementalStatus())
3114 			{
3115 				UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("BuildUp %s: %.1f/%.1f phase=%d"),
3116 					*Enemy->GetEnemyId().ToString(),
3117 					Elemental->GetBuildUp(Element), Elemental->GetThreshold(Element),
3118 					static_cast<int32>(Elemental->GetPhase(Element)));
3119 			}
3120 		}
3121 	}

3126 		FConsoleCommandWithWorldAndArgsDelegate::CreateStatic(&RunBuildUp));

3128 	static void RunSpawnEntity(UWorld* World)
3129 	{
3130 		AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(World);
3131 		if (!Player)
3132 		{
3133 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.SpawnEntity: no player character"));
3134 			return;
3135 		}
3136 		FVector Aim = Player->GetAimDirection();
3137 		Aim.Z = 0.f;
3138 		if (Aim.IsNearlyZero())
3139 		{
3140 			Aim = Player->GetActorForwardVector();
3141 		}
3142 		const FVector At = Player->GetActorLocation() + Aim.GetSafeNormal() * 1200.f;
3143 		EGBEraPresence Presence = EGBEraPresence::Both;
3144 		if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(World))
3145 		{
3146 			Presence = Era->GetCurrentEra() == EGBEra::Past ? EGBEraPresence::PastOnly : EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly;
3147 		}
3148 		if (!AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(World, FName(TEXT("Entity_Phase1")), At, FRotator::ZeroRotator, Presence))
3149 		{
3150 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("gb.SpawnEntity: spawn of Entity_Phase1 failed"));
3151 			return;
3152 		}
3153 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("gb.SpawnEntity: spawned Entity_Phase1 at %s"), *At.ToCompactString());
3154 	}

FSlateFontInfo::FSlateFontInfo( const FString& InFontName, float InSize, EFontHinting InHinting, const FFontOutlineSettings& InOutlineSettings)
	: FontObject(nullptr)
	, FontMaterial(nullptr)
	, OutlineSettings(InOutlineSettings)
	, CompositeFont()
	, TypefaceFontName()
	, Size(FMath::Clamp<float>(InSize, 0.f, std::numeric_limits<uint16>::max()))
	, FontFallback(EFontFallback::FF_Max)
#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
	, FontName_DEPRECATED(*InFontName)
	, Hinting_DEPRECATED(InHinting)
#endif
{
	ensureMsgf(InSize >= 0.f && InSize <= std::numeric_limits<uint16>::max(), TEXT("The size provided is not supported by the renderer."));

	//Useful for debugging style breakages
	//check( FPaths::FileExists( FontName.ToString() ) );

	UpgradeLegacyFontInfo(FName(*InFontName), InHinting);
}

 675 void AGBHUD::DrawCrosshair(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, EGBHudSkin Skin)
 676 {
 677 	if (!Player->IsControlEnabled() || Player->IsDead())
 678 	{
 679 		return;
 680 	}
 681 	const float S = UIScale();
 682 	const float CX = FMath::RoundToFloat(Canvas->ClipX * 0.5f);
 683 	const float CY = FMath::RoundToFloat(Canvas->ClipY * 0.5f);
 684 	const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player->GetEquipment();
 685 	AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment ? Equipment->GetActiveItem() : nullptr;
 686 	const bool bGun = Item && Item->GetSlot() == EGBHeldSlot::Gun;
 687 
 688 	if (Skin == EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk || bGun)
 689 	{
 690 		// Center dot and four ticks at the current spread cone (degrees -> pixels with the camera FOV), with a dark keyline.
 691 		const float T = FMath::Max(1.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(2.f * S));
 692 		const FLinearColor Ink = GBCy::Pale;
 693 		const FLinearColor Key(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.55f);
 694 		float Offset = 6.f * S;
 695 		if (bGun)
 696 		{
 697 			const AGBGun* Gun = Cast<AGBGun>(Item);
 698 			const float SpreadDeg = Gun ? Gun->GetCurrentSpread() : Item->GetStat(Item->IsAiming() ? GBStat::AimSpread : GBStat::Spread, 0.f);
 699 			const float FOV = (PlayerOwner && PlayerOwner->PlayerCameraManager) ? PlayerOwner->PlayerCameraManager->GetFOVAngle() : 90.f;
 700 			const float HalfFOVTan = FMath::Tan(FMath::DegreesToRadians(FMath::Clamp(FOV, 5.f, 170.f) * 0.5f));
 701 			Offset = FMath::Max(FMath::Tan(FMath::DegreesToRadians(FMath::Clamp(SpreadDeg, 0.f, 80.f))) / HalfFOVTan * Canvas->ClipX * 0.5f, 6.f * S);
 702 		}
 703 		Offset = FMath::RoundToFloat(Offset);
 704 		const float L = FMath::RoundToFloat(9.f * S);
 705 		auto Tick = [this, &Key, &Ink](float X, float Y, float W, float H)
 706 		{
 707 			FillRect(X - 1.f, Y - 1.f, W + 2.f, H + 2.f, Key);
 708 			FillRect(X, Y, W, H, Ink);
 709 		};
 710 		Tick(CX - T * 0.5f, CY - T * 0.5f, T, T);
 711 		Tick(CX - Offset - L, CY - T * 0.5f, L, T);
 712 		Tick(CX + Offset, CY - T * 0.5f, L, T);
 713 		Tick(CX - T * 0.5f, CY + Offset, T, L);
 714 		// The top tick is a small chevron: the one distinctive mark of the gun reticle.
 715 		Tick(CX - T * 0.5f, CY - Offset - L, T, L * 0.55f);
 716 		FillRect(CX - 3.f * T, CY - Offset - L - T, 6.f * T, T, GBAlpha(GBCy::Red, 0.9f));
 717 		return;
 718 	}
 719 
 720 	// RPG: a small pixel rune, gold with a dark outline, on a whole-pixel grid.
 721 	const float Px = FMath::Max(2.f, FMath::RoundToFloat(ArtPixel() * 0.5f));
 722 	const bool bSword = Item && Item->GetSlot() == EGBHeldSlot::Sword;
 723 	auto Dot = [this, Px, CX, CY](int32 GX, int32 GY, const FLinearColor& C)
 724 	{
 725 		FillRect(CX + (GX - 0.5f) * Px, CY + (GY - 0.5f) * Px, Px, Px, C);
 726 	};
 727 	const FLinearColor Gold = GBRpg::Gold3;
 728 	const FLinearColor Out = GBAlpha(GBRpg::Outline, 0.85f);
 729 	if (bSword)
 730 	{
 731 		// A diamond.
 732 		const int32 Pts[][2] = { {0,-2}, {-1,-1}, {1,-1}, {-2,0}, {2,0}, {-1,1}, {1,1}, {0,2} };
 733 		for (const auto& Pt : Pts) { for (int32 DY = -1; DY <= 1; ++DY) for (int32 DX = -1; DX <= 1; ++DX) Dot(Pt[0] + DX, Pt[1] + DY, Out); }
 734 		for (const auto& Pt : Pts) { Dot(Pt[0], Pt[1], Gold); }
 735 		return;
 736 	}
 737 	// Four arms of two pixels around a gap, plus the center pixel.
 738 	const int32 Arms[][2] = { {0,-3}, {0,-4}, {0,3}, {0,4}, {-3,0}, {-4,0}, {3,0}, {4,0}, {0,0} };
 739 	for (const auto& Pt : Arms) { for (int32 DY = -1; DY <= 1; ++DY) for (int32 DX = -1; DX <= 1; ++DX) Dot(Pt[0] + DX, Pt[1] + DY, Out); }
 740 	for (const auto& Pt : Arms) { Dot(Pt[0], Pt[1], (Pt[0] == 0 && Pt[1] == 0) ? GBRpg::Gold4 : Gold); }
 741 }

 132 UTexture2D* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreateTexture(const TArray<FColor>& Pixels, int32 Width, int32 Height, FName DebugName, bool bWrap)
 133 {
 134 	return CreatePixelTexture(Pixels, Width, Height, DebugName, bWrap);
 135 }
 136 
 137 UTexture2D* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreatePixelTexture(const TArray<FColor>& Pixels, int32 Width, int32 Height, FName DebugName, bool bWrap)
 138 {
 139 	if (Width <= 0 || Height <= 0 || Pixels.Num() != Width * Height)
 140 	{
 141 		return nullptr;
 142 	}
 143 
 144 	UTexture2D* Texture = UTexture2D::CreateTransient(Width, Height, PF_B8G8R8A8);
 145 	if (!Texture || !Texture->GetPlatformData() || Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips.Num() == 0)
 146 	{
 147 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Pixel sprite '%s': could not create a %dx%d texture."), *DebugName.ToString(), Width, Height);
 148 		return nullptr;
 149 	}
 150 
 151 	Texture->Filter = TF_Nearest;
 152 	Texture->SRGB = true;
 153 	Texture->NeverStream = true;
 154 	Texture->CompressionSettings = TC_EditorIcon;
 155 	Texture->LODGroup = TEXTUREGROUP_Pixels2D;
 156 	// Sprites clamp (no bleeding from the opposite edge); tiles repeat across a structure's faces.
 157 	Texture->AddressX = bWrap ? TA_Wrap : TA_Clamp;
 158 	Texture->AddressY = bWrap ? TA_Wrap : TA_Clamp;
 159 #if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
 160 	Texture->MipGenSettings = TMGS_NoMipmaps;
 161 #endif
 162 
 163 	// FColor is laid out B, G, R, A in memory, which is exactly PF_B8G8R8A8; rows go top to bottom.
 164 	FTexture2DMipMap& Mip = Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips[0];
 165 	void* Data = Mip.BulkData.Lock(LOCK_READ_WRITE);
 166 	FMemory::Memcpy(Data, Pixels.GetData(), Pixels.Num() * sizeof(FColor));
 167 	Mip.BulkData.Unlock();
 168 	Texture->UpdateResource();
 169 	return Texture;
 170 }
 171

 173 {
 174 	return GetOrBuild(SpriteId, /*bWrap*/ false);

 185 	if (TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>* Cached = TargetCache.Find(SpriteId))

+/** sRGB hex -> linear color. */
+inline FLinearColor GBHex(const TCHAR* Hex, float Alpha = 1.f)
+{
+	FLinearColor Color = FLinearColor::FromSRGBColor(FColor::FromHex(Hex));
+	Color.A = Alpha;
+	return Color;
+}
+
+inline FLinearColor GBAlpha(const FLinearColor& Color, float Alpha)
+{
+	return FLinearColor(Color.R, Color.G, Color.B, Color.A * Alpha);
+}

 137 void AGBPlayerController::SetMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
 138 {
 139 	if (Menu == OpenMenu)
 140 	{
 141 		return;
 142 	}
 143 	const EGBMenu OldMenu = OpenMenu;
 144 	AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD());
 145 
 146 	if (OldMenu != EGBMenu::None && HUD)
 147 	{
 148 		HUD->OnMenuClosed(OldMenu);
 149 	}
 150 	OpenMenu = Menu;
 151

 153 	if (Menu == EGBMenu::None)
 154 	{
 155 		SetPause(false);
 156 		if (Subsystem && InputSet)
 157 		{
 158 			Subsystem->RemoveMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext);
 159 			// A key or button still held from the menu (the click that closed it) does nothing until released.
 160 			FModifyContextOptions Options;
 161 			Options.bIgnoreAllPressedKeysUntilRelease = true;
 162 			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext, GameplayContextPriority, Options);
 163 		}
 164 		bCenterCursorPending = false;
 165 		ApplyMouseMode(false);
 166 		return;

 168

 172 		{
 173 			PlayerCharacter->ReleaseHeldInputs();
 174 		}
 175 	}
 176 	if (Subsystem && InputSet)
 177 	{
 178 		// The death screen also drops the gameplay keys: nothing to do in the world while dead.
 179 		Subsystem->RemoveMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext);
 180 		Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext, MenuContextPriority);
 181 	}
 182 	SetPause(IsPausingMenu(Menu));
 183 
 184 	ApplyMouseMode(true);
 185 	if (OldMenu == EGBMenu::None)
 186 	{
 187 		CenterMouseCursor();
 188 		bCenterCursorPending = true;
 189 	}

		{ TEXT('&'), TEXT(".##..|#..#.|#.#..|.#...|#.#.#|#..#.|.##.#") },
		{ TEXT('~'), TEXT(".....|.....|.#...|#.#.#|...#.") },
	};

	static constexpr int32 AtlasColumns = 16;
	// Padded cell: 5 columns of glyph at most + the outline ring on each side; 9 rows + the ring.
	static constexpr int32 CellW = 5 + 2;
	static constexpr int32 CellH = UGBPixelFont::CellRows + 2;

	static int32 RowWidth(const TCHAR* Rows)
	{
		int32 Width = 0;
		while (Rows[Width] != 0 && Rows[Width] != TEXT('|'))
		{
			++Width;
		}
		return Width;
	}

	static FColor LerpColor(const FColor& A, const FColor& B, float T)
	{
		return FColor(
			(uint8)FMath::RoundToInt(FMath::Lerp((float)A.R, (float)B.R, T)),
			(uint8)FMath::RoundToInt(FMath::Lerp((float)A.G, (float)B.G, T)),
			(uint8)FMath::RoundToInt(FMath::Lerp((float)A.B, (float)B.B, T)),
			255);
	}
}

const TCHAR* UGBPixelFont::GetGlyphRows(TCHAR Char)
{
	for (const GBPixelFontData::FEntry& Entry : GBPixelFontData::Entries)
	{
		if (Entry.Char == Char)
		{
			return Entry.Rows;
		}
	}
	return nullptr;
}

void UGBPixelFont::Init()
{
	using namespace GBPixelFontData;
	if (OutlineAtlas)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 Count = UE_ARRAY_COUNT(Entries);
	const int32 RowsOfCells = (Count + AtlasColumns - 1) / AtlasColumns;
	AtlasSize = FIntPoint(AtlasColumns * CellW, RowsOfCells * CellH);
	Mask.SetNumZeroed(AtlasSize.X * AtlasSize.Y);

	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count; ++Index)
	{
		const FEntry& Entry = Entries[Index];
		FGlyph Glyph;
		Glyph.AtlasX = (Index % AtlasColumns) * CellW;
		Glyph.AtlasY = (Index / AtlasColumns) * CellH;
		Glyph.Width = RowWidth(Entry.Rows);
		int32 Row = 0;
		int32 Col = 0;
		for (const TCHAR* C = Entry.Rows; *C; ++C)
		{
			if (*C == TEXT('|'))
			{
				++Row;
				Col = 0;
				continue;
			}
			if (*C == TEXT('#') && Row < CellRows && Col < CellW - 2)
			{
				Mask[(Glyph.AtlasY + 1 + Row) * AtlasSize.X + Glyph.AtlasX + 1 + Col] = 1;
			}
			++Col;
		}
		Glyphs.Add(Entry.Char, Glyph);
	}

	// Outline ring: every empty pixel next to ink (8 neighbours), inside the same cell.
	TArray<FColor> Pixels;
	Pixels.Init(FColor(0, 0, 0, 0), AtlasSize.X * AtlasSize.Y);
	for (int32 Y = 0; Y < AtlasSize.Y; ++Y)
	{
		for (int32 X = 0; X < AtlasSize.X; ++X)
		{
			if (Mask[Y * AtlasSize.X + X])
			{
				continue;
			}
			const int32 CellX0 = (X / CellW) * CellW;
			const int32 CellY0 = (Y / CellH) * CellH;
			bool bNear = false;
			for (int32 DY = -1; DY <= 1 && !bNear; ++DY)
			{
				for (int32 DX = -1; DX <= 1 && !bNear; ++DX)
				{
					const int32 NX = X + DX;
					const int32 NY = Y + DY;
					if (NX < CellX0 || NY < CellY0 || NX >= CellX0 + CellW || NY >= CellY0 + CellH)
					{
						continue;
					}
					bNear = Mask[NY * AtlasSize.X + NX] != 0;
				}
			}
			if (bNear)
			{
				Pixels[Y * AtlasSize.X + X] = FColor::White;
			}
		}
	}
	OutlineAtlas = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreatePixelTexture(Pixels, AtlasSize.X, AtlasSize.Y, TEXT("PixelFontOutline"), false);
}

const UGBPixelFont::FGlyph* UGBPixelFont::FindGlyph(TCHAR Char) const
{
	if (const FGlyph* Glyph = Glyphs.Find(Char))
	{
		return Glyph;
	}
	// Letters missing in one case fall back to the other; anything else shows as '?'.
	if (const FGlyph* Upper = Glyphs.Find(FChar::ToUpper(Char)))
	{
		return Upper;
	}
	return Glyphs.Find(TEXT('?'));
}

int32 UGBPixelFont::MeasureWidth(const FString& Text) const
{
	int32 Width = 0;
	for (const TCHAR Char : Text)
	{
		if (const FGlyph* Glyph = FindGlyph(Char))
		{
			Width += Glyph->Width + Spacing;
		}
	}
	return FMath::Max(0, Width - Spacing);
}

UTexture2D* UGBPixelFont::GetInkAtlas(const FGBPixelInk& Ink)
{
	using namespace GBPixelFontData;
	Init();
	const uint32 Key = Ink.Key();
	if (TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>* Found = InkAtlases.Find(Key))
	{
		return *Found;
	}
	TArray<FColor> Pixels;
	Pixels.Init(FColor(0, 0, 0, 0), AtlasSize.X * AtlasSize.Y);
	for (int32 Y = 0; Y < AtlasSize.Y; ++Y)
	{
		// Ramp over the capital height: Top on row 0, Mid around row 3, Bottom from row 6 (descenders keep Bottom).
		const int32 Row = FMath::Clamp((Y % CellH) - 1, 0, CellRows - 1);
		const float T = FMath::Clamp(Row / (float)(CapHeight - 1), 0.f, 1.f);
		const FColor Color = T < 0.5f ? LerpColor(Ink.Top, Ink.Mid, T * 2.f) : LerpColor(Ink.Mid, Ink.Bottom, (T - 0.5f) * 2.f);
		for (int32 X = 0; X < AtlasSize.X; ++X)
		{
			if (Mask[Y * AtlasSize.X + X])
			{
				Pixels[Y * AtlasSize.X + X] = Color;
			}
		}
	}
	UTexture2D* Texture = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreatePixelTexture(Pixels, AtlasSize.X, AtlasSize.Y, TEXT("PixelFontInk"), false);
	InkAtlases.Add(Key, Texture);
	return Texture;
}

At 1280×720, settled RPG toasts start at Y=576, 624, 672, **720, 768**: four or five queued menu messages leave the newest messages invisible. **Smallest fix:** clamp the starting Y using the total toast-stack height and a bottom margin.
